We introduce SPECTRA (Scanner and Pathology Embedding Contrastive Tuning for Robust Alignment), a model-agnostic contrastive learning post-training framework which explicitly encourages scanner- and stain-robust representations, to optimize performance and generalization on downstream applications. Using parameter-efficient LoRA adapters trained on matched multi-scanner and multi-stain whole-slide images sourced from the PLISM dataset, our method aligns spatially corresponding regions across acquisition conditions (scanner, stain), using other locations within each candidate condition as negatives. SPECTRA improved PathoROB robustness index, gene-expression prediction on HEST, slide-level classification AUC on CPTAC, and held-out PLISM retrieval for every model. Overall tile-level classification on THUNDER also improved, with exceptions for segmentation and calibration tasks. We open-source our training and evaluation codebases and release adapters and model weights for selected backbones.

</details>


### [36] [A Privacy-Aware Deep Learning Benchmark for Facial Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10097)
*Larissa Palhares,Maria Machado,Hudson Bruno,Raphael Ramos,Andrew Vale,Barbara Barbosa,Inara Souza,Fabio Grassiotto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅使用面部关键点(478点)与blendshape(52维ARKit系数)的轻量隐私保护型面部表情识别流程，在CREMA-D+RAVDESS融合数据上以受试者独立5折交叉验证，几何模型在参数少12–124倍的情况下优于ResNet50像素基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FER依赖原始RGB图像，存在隐私泄露风险、对光照变化敏感，并易受身份混淆性视觉捷径干扰；需要一种无需存储原始视频的隐私友好替代方案。

Method: 以478点面部关键点与52维ARKit标准blendshape系数作为唯一输入，去除原始视频持久化存储；从零训练6种神经架构，并与ImageNet预训练ResNet50基线进行对比；采用5折受试者独立交叉验证，在融合的CREMA-D与RAVDESS数据集上评测；通过消融实验分析两类几何特征贡献。

Result: 几何模型在未见受试者上一致优于像素基线，参数量少12–124倍；在悲伤等细微表情上表现更稳健；消融显示blendshape承载主要判别信号，关键点提供必要的互补空间拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 紧凑几何表示可保留情感语义，摆脱对视觉骨干网络的迁移学习依赖，实现高效且隐私感知的情绪识别。

Abstract: Facial Expression Recognition (FER) traditionally relies on raw RGB images, introducing privacy risks, sensitivity to illumination variations, and susceptibility to identity-confounding visual shortcuts. As an alternative, this work proposes a lightweight, privacy-aware pipeline using exclusively geometric representations-facial landmarks (478 points) and blendshapes (52 coefficients, ARKit standard)-eliminating persistent raw video storage. Six neural architectures trained from scratch on fused CREMA-D and RAVDESS datasets were benchmarked against an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 baseline under a 5-fold subject-independent cross-validation protocol. Geometric models consistently outperformed the pixel baseline on unseen subjects with 12-124 times fewer parameters, while demonstrating superior robustness on subtle emotional expressions such as sadness. Ablation studies reveal that blendshapes capture the primary discriminative signal, while landmarks provide essential complementary spatial topology. These results demonstrate that compact geometric representations preserve affective semantics, eliminate downstream transfer learning dependency on visual backbones, and enable efficient, privacy-aware emotion recognition.

</details>


### [37] [Improving the Diffusability of Motion Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10160)
*Guanhe Huang,Songqiao Han,Tangzheng Lian,Oya Celiktutan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Diffusable Motion Tokenizer（DiMoT），从 tokenizer 侧而非扩大扩散骨干提升文本驱动人体动作生成质量，通过 Critic Spectral Adaptation 与 Alpha-Flow Motion Decoder 缓解 VAE 的"生成-重建权衡"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐空间扩散+VAE tokenizer 存在生成-重建权衡：单纯增大 tokenizer 容量可提升重建，却无法同比例改善文本条件生成。作者假设该权衡源于 VAE 内部频谱失配——编码器产生偏向高频的隐空间，解码器引入时序伪影。

Method: 不改动扩散骨干，转而提升 tokenizer 的"可扩散性"。1）Critic Spectral Adaptation (CSA)：以预训练扩散模型作为 critic，抑制隐空间高频能量，改善文本条件优化；2）Alpha-Flow Motion Decoder (AMD)：通过平均速度预测进行生成式解码，实现高效一步解码并保持动作动力学。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的 text-to-motion 基准上取得 SOTA，动作保真度与文本-动作对齐均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提高 tokenizer 的可扩散性是突破生成-重建权衡的有效途径，频谱失配假设得到实验支持；CSA 与 AMD 分别从优化与解码两端协同改善 latent diffusion 的动作生成表现。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models paired with a variational auto-encoder (VAE) tokenizer have shown promising performance for efficient text-driven human motion generation. However, motion latent diffusion suffers from a generation-reconstruction trade-off: simply increasing tokenizer capacity enhances reconstruction but fails to proportionally improve text-conditioned generation quality. We assume this trade-off stems from a spectral mismatch inside the VAE tokenizer: the encoder yields a latent space biased toward high frequencies, and the decoder introduces temporal artifacts. In this paper, we propose the Diffusable Motion Tokenizer (DiMoT) to improve the diffusability of the tokenizer, rather than scaling up the diffusion backbone. To improve text-conditioned optimization, Critic Spectral Adaptation (CSA) leverages a pretrained diffusion model as the critic to suppress high-frequency latent energy. To preserve motion dynamics, the Alpha-Flow Motion Decoder (AMD) performs generative decoding via average-velocity prediction, enabling efficient one-step decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiMoT achieves state-of-the-art performance across various challenging text-to-motion benchmarks, yielding superior motion fidelity and text-motion alignment. Project page: https://githubhgh.github.io/dimot/.

</details>


### [38] [CXR-CMFA: A Cross-Modal Feature Aggregation Network for Chest X-Ray Pathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10174)
*Talukder Hasnat Zadid,Jahin Sultana,Felipe Bochini Brisola,Kishore Roy,Mysha Rahman,Syeda Asrafa Islam,Md. Abdullah-Al-Jubair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出轻量级跨模态特征聚合网络 CXR-CMFA，融合双流视觉编码器与 TF-IDF-MLP 报告编码器，实现二分类胸部X线病理分类，在 MIMIC-CXR 上取得 93.45% 准确率与 99.19% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯图像深度学习模型丢弃了放射报告中互补的诊断上下文，而大型视觉-语言模型计算开销大、难以实际部署；同时胸部X线存在解剖重叠、图像质量差异及单张影像视觉证据不足等难点。

Method: 双流视觉编码：EfficientNet-B0 提取全局解剖表征，DenseNet-121 提取局部病理细节；文本侧采用 TF-IDF-MLP 报告编码器；两个融合模块——双模态空间注意力模块（DMSAM）用报告嵌入对全局与局部视觉特征进行门控，跨模态门控特征融合模块（CGFF）学习视觉与文本贡献的实例级权重。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 官方训练/验证/测试划分上，测试集准确率 93.45%、精确率 99.88%、召回率 91.8%、F1 95.7%、AUC 99.19%；984 例正常样本中仅 4 例假阳性。消融实验显示相较仅全局图像变体 AUC 提升 22.47 个百分点。

Conclusion: 紧凑的报告条件化融合即可获得强判别性能，无需依赖大型预训练语言模型，适合高特异性筛查流程。

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation remains challenging because subtle pathology can be obscured by overlapping anatomy, image-quality variation, and limited visual evidence in isolated radiographs. Although image-only deep learning models provide useful baselines, they discard the complementary diagnostic context available in radiology reports, while large vision-language models can be computationally expensive for practical deployment. This paper presents CXR-CMFA, a lightweight cross-modal feature aggregation network for binary CXR pathology classification. The proposed framework combines a dual-stream visual encoder, using EfficientNet-B0 for global anatomical representation and DenseNet-121 for local pathological detail, with a TF-IDF-MLP report encoder. Two fusion modules integrate these modalities: the Dual-Modal Spatial Attention Module (DMSAM) uses the report embedding to gate global and local visual features, and the Cross-Modal Gated Feature Fusion (CGFF) module learns per-instance weights for visual and textual contributions. Experiments on the MIMIC-CXR benchmark, using the provided train, validation, and test splits, show that CXR-CMFA achieves 93.45% accuracy, 99.88% precision, 91.8% recall, 95.7% F1-score, and 99.19% AUC on the held-out test set. The model produces only four false positives among 984 normal test cases, supporting its suitability for high-specificity screening workflows. An ablation study further demonstrates a 22.47 percentage-point AUC improvement over the global-image-only variant, confirming the value of adaptive cross-modal fusion. These results indicate that compact report-conditioned fusion can provide strong discriminative performance without relying on large pretrained language models.

</details>


### [39] [ATLAS: A Multi-Procedure Benchmark and Context-Aware Model for Surgical Anatomy Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10182)
*Ronald L. P. D. de Jong,Tim J. M. Jaspers,Raf A. H. Vervoort,Aron F. H. A. Bakker,Anna L. Dijk,Yiping Li,Jip L. Tolenaar,Jelle P. Ruurda,Willem M. Brinkman,Daan de Geus,Fons van der Sommen,Josien P. W. Pluim,Marcel Breeuwer*

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS-120k手术解剖语义分割数据集（121,018帧、100段视频、14类术式、41类解剖结构）与ATLAS视频语义分割模型，结合域内基础模型嵌入、术式与手术阶段上下文查询及跨帧查询传播，实现Dice 0.70、76.2 FPS实时推理，并具良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中解剖结构识别可支持术中导航并提升患者安全，但受限于数据集小且多为单一术式，以及现有分割方法源自自然图像场景，进展受限。

Method: 构建ATLAS-120k剪辑级数据集，标注流水线结合人工标注、基于跟踪器传播与外科医生复核；提出ATLAS模型，基于域内基础模型嵌入，引入术式与手术阶段的上下文查询（context queries）及帧间查询传播实现短期记忆。

Result: 在分割精度与时间一致性上均优于自然图像及域内基础模型SOTA；ViT-L下Dice达0.70，实时运行76.2 FPS；消融显示上下文查询提升精度、查询传播提升时间一致性；零样本在CholecSeg8k上匹配专用模型，AutoLaparo相识别优于SurgeNet初始化。

Conclusion: 数据集与模型共同缓解了术式覆盖窄、方法不适配的瓶颈，验证了上下文学习与查询传播的有效性；模型权重、标注与标注工具已公开。

Abstract: Recognizing anatomical structures during minimally invasive surgery (MIS) could support intraoperative navigation and improve patient safety, but progress is limited by small, single-procedure datasets and by segmentation methods developed for natural scenes. This work addresses both limitations through a new dataset and model. First, we introduce ATLAS-120k, a clip-level semantic segmentation dataset of 121,018 annotated frames from 100 surgical videos, covering 14 laparoscopic and robot-assisted procedures and 41 classes. Annotations were created with a pipeline that combines manual labeling, tracker-based propagation, and review by surgeons. Second, we propose ATLAS (Anatomy Recognition with Context Learning using Foundation Representations), a video semantic segmentation model for surgical anatomy recognition. ATLAS builds on in-domain foundation-model embeddings and adds context queries for the procedure and surgical phase, together with query propagation between frames for short-term memory. ATLAS outperforms state-of-the-art natural-image and in-domain foundation models in both segmentation accuracy and temporal consistency, achieving a Dice score of 0.70 with ViT-L while running in real time at 76.2 FPS. Ablations show that the context queries improve accuracy and that query propagation improves temporal consistency. ATLAS also generalizes well, matching a dedicated model zero-shot on CholecSeg8k and improving phase recognition on AutoLaparo over SurgeNet initialization. The model weights, dataset annotations, and annotation tool are publicly available at https://github.com/TimJaspers0801/ATLAS.

</details>


### [40] [RigidGS: Geometry-Aware Object Decoupling for Rigid-Body Interaction in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10199)
*Paul Sansah Gyreyiri,Diksha Shukla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RigidGS：一种将几何学习与对象身份解耦的端到端对象感知3DGS流水线，通过掩码精修与冻结身份实现高质量实时渲染与刚体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式VR/MR应用需要可实时渲染、可对象级操作的照片级3D表示。3DGS渲染质量高但场景为无结构整体基元；现有对象感知3DGS联合优化几何与语义，存在两大失效：复杂几何上基础模型掩码噪声大，以及标签-几何耦合（协同优化的身份梯度导致边界高斯被误标）。

Method: 提出端到端流水线RigidGS，解耦几何学习与对象身份。1）深度与时序感知的掩码精修模块，针对SAM2两类失败（细结构过度包含、多模态深度物体包含不足）进行修正；2）通过COLMAP投影投票在上游确定对象身份，训练中冻结，使掩码监督仅作用于几何优化。

Result: 仅使用精修掩码即可显著提升对比方法；RigidGS在LLFF/NVOS、Mip-NeRF 360、iNeRF360上优于以往对象感知3DGS方法，在LLFF/NVOS上分割与边界IoU分别提升7.7和14.5个百分点；并在Meta Quest 2上实现无需网格提取的实时刚体操作。

Conclusion: 将对象身份确定移至上游并冻结，可消除标签-几何耦合这一残留差距，同时掩码精修解决SAM2结构性问题，最终实现对象级分割质量与实时VR/MR刚体交互能力的统一。

Abstract: Interactive VR/MR applications demand photorealistic 3D representations that support real-time rendering and object-level manipulation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high rendering quality, it represents scenes as unstructured, monolithic primitives. Existing object-aware 3DGS methods jointly optimize geometry and semantic states but suffer from two key failure modes: noisy foundation-model masks on complex geometry, and label-geometry coupling, in which co-optimized identity gradients mislabel boundary Gaussians. We introduce RigidGS, an end-to-end pipeline that decouples geometric learning from object identity. We propose a depth- and temporally-aware mask refinement module that addresses two complementary SAM2 failures: over-inclusion on thin structures and under-inclusion on multimodal-depth objects. We then determine object identities entirely upstream via COLMAP projection voting and freeze them during training, directing mask supervision solely toward geometric optimization. Refined masks alone substantially improve competing methods, yet a residual gap in label-geometry coupling persists that our fixed-identity design eliminates. RigidGS outperforms prior object-aware 3DGS methods on LLFF/NVOS, Mip-NeRF~360, and iNeRF360, improving segmentation and boundary IoU by 7.7 and 14.5 points on LLFF/NVOS. Finally, RigidGS enables real-time rigid-body manipulation on Meta Quest~2 without mesh extraction. Our code and project page are available at https://gyreyirisansah.github.io/rigidgs.

</details>


### [41] [Anatomy-Preserving Unpaired Medical Image Translation via Shared Latent Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10236)
*Zhilin Zou,Hieu Le,Saumya Gupta,Jingyi Xu,Prateek Prasanna,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段非配对医学图像翻译框架：先学习共享结构空间（结构编解码器+掩码重建锚定+目标域记忆库软监督），再以条件流模型在冻结结构表示上渲染目标域外观，在OCT→OCTA与MR→CT上超越非配对与配对基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非配对医学图像翻译用单一模型学习直接源→目标映射，将结构保持与外观生成纠缠在一起，常扭曲临床有意义的解剖结构。作者主张二者应彻底解耦：解剖结构应独立于生成过程被捕获，生成器只作用于冻结的结构表示。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段：在源图像、目标图像与结构掩码上联合训练结构编解码器，以掩码重建将隐空间锚定为模态无关解剖；对无掩码标注的源图像，通过目标域记忆库的原型检索提供软结构监督。第二阶段：条件流模型在固定结构条件下学习渲染目标域外观，使生成器继承目标外观统计、专注结构条件化而非从头学习外观。

Result: 在OCT-to-OCTA合成与脑MR-to-CT翻译上，优于所有非配对基线；尽管无配对监督，也超过配对基线：OCT→OCTA上PSNR最高提升11.2%、SSIM最高提升26.4%；MR→CT在整图、软组织与骨骼区域均取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 结构-外观解耦的两阶段设计有效提升非配对医学图像翻译的解剖保真度与外观真实性，无需配对监督即可超越配对方法，验证了冻结结构表示+条件生成范式的可行性。

Abstract: Unpaired medical image-to-image translation requires synthesizing target-modality images from source-modality images without aligned supervision. Existing methods learn a direct source-to-target mapping that entangles structure preservation and appearance generation within a single model, often distorting clinically meaningful anatomy in the translated output. We argue that these two objectives should be decoupled entirely: anatomical structure should be captured independently of generation, and the generator should operate only on a frozen structural representation. To this end, we propose a two-stage framework for unpaired medical image translation. In the first stage, we learn a shared structure space by training a structure encoder-decoder on source images, target images, and structural masks jointly, using mask reconstruction to anchor the latent space to modality-agnostic anatomy. For source images without mask annotations, a target-domain memory bank provides soft structural supervision via prototype retrieval. In the second stage, a conditional flow model learns to render target-domain appearance under fixed structural conditions. This ensures the generator inherits target appearance statistics and can focus entirely on structural conditioning rather than learning appearance from scratch. We validate our framework on OCT-to-OCTA synthesis and brain MR-to-CT translation. Our method outperforms all unpaired baselines on both tasks and, despite requiring no paired supervision, also surpasses paired baselines, improving PSNR by up to 11.2% and SSIM by up to 26.4% on OCT-to-OCTA, and achieving consistent gains across whole-image, soft-tissue, and bone regions on MR-to-CT. The project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [42] [From Weapons to Events: Evaluating Evidential Support in Multimodal AI for Policing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10245)
*Armin Mustafa,Syed Sameed Husain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文面向警务用执法记录仪（BWV）视频的计算机视觉分析，提出目标级与事件级两套贡献：目标级发布 KnifeHunter 数据集（25,000+ 图像、543 个刀具类别）与融合局部/全局特征的检索网络 KHNet；事件级提出视频理解流水线评估协议（涵盖转写、说话人归属、事件检测、时间定位与无支撑事件），并在 UKBWV-27 试点集上评测三种全模态模型与自研 NarrativeBridge-PAL-GPT-5 混合流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有警务 CV 主要聚焦缴获武器等目标识别，而 BWV 视频仍依赖人工审阅，已有 AI 工具仅利用音频信息；多模态视频 AI 有望使 BWV 审阅更快、成本更低，但缺乏贴近侦查条件的数据集、检索方法以及细粒度的事件级评估协议。

Method: 目标级：与英国执法部门合作构建 KnifeHunter 数据集（25,000+ 图像、543 个刀具类、反映侦查条件的协议），提出融合局部与全局特征的检索架构 KHNet，并以约 125,000 张干扰图像进行评测。事件级：设计将视频描述为事件序列与转写的视频理解流水线评估协议，从转写质量、说话人归属、事件检测、时间定位、无支撑事件五方面打分，事件是否被录像支撑由经盲测人类标注验证的判定器标注；在 UKBWV-27（27 段公开英国 BWV 录像、118 分钟）上对比三种 2026 年全模态模型与作者提出的 NarrativeBridge-PAL-GPT-5 混合流水线。

Result: KHNet 在加入约 125,000 张干扰图像后，于 medium 与 hard 协议上分别取得 85.7% 与 63.4% 的 mAP。事件级评测中，混合流水线在六项指标中的五项领先，但仍有 13.6% 的预测事件缺乏录像支撑、27.7% 的话语说话人归属错误；所有流水线在剪辑版录像、长时录像与噪声音频上均出现性能退化。

Conclusion: 多模态视频 AI 在 BWV 审阅上具备潜力但尚不可完全自动采信；作者提出"就绪等级"框架，将辅助性任务与需逐项核验输出的任务区分开，为警务场景下的部署边界提供依据。

Abstract: Computer vision (CV) for policing has centred on recognising objects such as seized weapons, while body-worn video (BWV) is still reviewed by people, with existing AI tools using only its audio. Multimodal video AI could make BWV review faster and cheaper. At the object level, we present the KnifeHunter dataset, developed with UK law enforcement, with over 25,000 images of 543 knife classes and a protocol reflecting investigative conditions, and KHNet, a retrieval architecture fusing local and global features, which with about 125,000 distractor images attains 85.7% and 63.4% mean Average Precision (mAP) on the medium and hard protocols. At the event level, we present an evaluation protocol for video understanding pipelines, which describe a video as events and a transcript, that scores transcription, speaker attribution, event detection, temporal localisation and unsupported events. Whether each event is supported by the recording is labelled by a judge validated against blinded human labels. We apply it to complete pipelines, three 2026 omni-modal models and our hybrid NarrativeBridge-PAL-GPT-5 pipeline, on UKBWV-27, a pilot stress-test set of 27 publicly released UK BWV recordings (118 minutes). On this set the hybrid pipeline leads on five of six measures, yet it leaves 13.6% of predicted events unsupported and misattributes 27.7% of utterances, and every pipeline degrades on recordings from edited releases, long recordings and noisy audio. We propose readiness levels separating assistive tasks from those requiring verification of every output.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Frame-Level Accuracy: Analyzing Video-Level Stability in Transformer-Based Deepfake Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10249)
*Syed Azmul Hasan,Mowmita Pervin Hera,Afroza Lona Mim,M M Jihad Hossain,Hazrat Ali,Mohammad Farhad Bulbul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在统一预处理、数据划分与训练协议下对比ResNet-18、EfficientNet-B0与ViT-B/16的深度伪造检测性能，发现ViT在视频级稳定性与域内鲁棒性更优，但跨域泛化仍存在显著不对称差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法的性能报告常基于异构预处理流程、不一致的数据划分和模型特定的训练协议，导致架构间难以进行公平比较，缺乏可控、可复现的对比研究。

Method: 构建统一的人脸中心化检测框架，对两个CNN（ResNet-18、EfficientNet-B0）与一个Transformer（ViT-B/16）采用完全相同的预处理、数据增强、优化设置与数据划分，在Celeb-DF v2和FaceForensics++上训练；以三个独立随机种子验证，并使用自助法置信区间；同时进行跨数据集评估。

Result: CNN模型在帧级别具竞争力，但视频级聚合后性能下降；ViT-B/16视频级表现更稳定，在Celeb-DF v2上达到92.8%视频级准确率[95% CI: 91.7–94.0]与AUC 0.950 [95% CI: 0.934–0.964]。跨数据集评估显示明显不对称泛化差距（FF++→Celeb-DF AUC 0.79 对 Celeb-DF→FF++ AUC 0.64）。

Conclusion: 全局自注意力机制在给定数据域内可提升深度伪造检测的鲁棒性与视频级稳定性，但域内稳定性无法迁移至未见篡改域，跨域泛化仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Deepfake videos generated by advanced generative models pose serious threats to digital security, misinformation control, and media forensics. Although numerous detection methods have been proposed, their reported performances are often based on heterogeneous preprocessing pipelines, inconsistent data splits, and model-specific training protocols, hindering fair architectural comparison. This work presents a controlled and reproducible comparative study of convolutional neural networks (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0) and a transformer-based architecture (ViT-B/16) within a unified, face-centric deepfake detection framework. All models are trained using identical preprocessing, augmentation, optimization settings, and data splits on Celeb-DF v2 and FaceForensics++, and results are validated across three independent random seeds with bootstrapped confidence intervals. Experimental results show that while CNN-based models perform competitively at the frame level, their performance degrades after video-level aggregation. In contrast, ViT-B/16 demonstrates more stable video-level performance, achieving 92.8\% [95\% CI: 91.7--94.0] video-level accuracy and an AUC of 0.950 [95\% CI: 0.934--0.964] on Celeb-DF v2 (3-seed bootstrapped). Cross-dataset evaluation across the same three random seeds reveals a large, asymmetric generalization gap (AUC 0.79 FF++$\rightarrow$Celeb-DF vs. 0.64 Celeb-DF$\rightarrow$FF++), indicating that in-domain video-level stability does not transfer to unseen manipulation domains. These findings indicate that global self-attention improves robustness and video-level stability for deepfake detection within a given data domain, while highlighting the need for further work on cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [44] [STAIRS: Staging the Inverse Problem via Admissible-Support Scheduling for Unrolled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10260)
*Xiangjian Hou,Chang Ni,Chao Qin,Zhiwei Tan,Xin Wang,Xinrui Ye,Rong-Rong Chen,Chun Yuan,Xiaodong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAIRS方法，在展开式MRI重建中通过嵌套傅里叶支撑的粗到细调度，配合基于协方差对数行列式得分的归一化谱坐标与二进目标分配，在不改变采样掩膜与数据一致性前提下提升多种骨干的加速MRI重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MRI从欠采傅里叶测量重建图像，计算负担转向重建端。深度展开网络交替进行学习正则与数据一致性，但每个级联通常都估计完整未测谱，缺乏由粗到细的渐进结构，未充分利用频率逐步纳入的潜在收益。

Method: STAIRS将中间重建投影到逐级扩展至全谱的嵌套傅里叶支撑，并以支撑转移为条件进行更新。用训练协方差的正则化对数行列式得分的条件增量定义归一化谱坐标，刻画新纳入频率与已有频率的依赖关系；采用二进目标为前部级联分配更大坐标增量，并在训练前映射为固定支撑，实现粗到细重建而不改变采样掩膜与数据一致性。

Result: 在fastMRI与Stanford2D上，STAIRS在单线圈与多线圈重建中均提升匹配的Transformer（HUMUSNet）与卷积（NAFNet）骨干，包括放射科医生标注的病灶区域；优于基于预测器的渐进方案，且参数开销与推理延迟更低。分阶段分析显示投影带来重复误差校正，支撑扩展过程中中间重建误差低于单独骨干。消融表明二进分配优于方形与线带支撑下的均匀扩展，也优于方形窗的手工前置加载；仅支撑投影即可改善重建，转移条件进一步增益。跨数据集双向评估两种骨干均有提升。

Conclusion: 支撑分配与正则器设计在展开式MRI重建中互补：渐进式谱支撑调度可作为独立于网络架构的通用增强手段，带来稳定且低开销的重建增益。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) forms images from incomplete Fourier measurements, shifting acquisition burden to computational reconstruction. Deep unrolled networks interleave learned regularization and data consistency, yet typically estimate the full unmeasured spectrum at every cascade. We propose STAIRS (Staging the Inverse Problem via Admissible-Support Scheduling), which projects intermediate reconstructions onto nested Fourier supports that expand to the full spectrum and conditions each update on its support transition. Conditional increments of a regularized log-determinant score of training covariance define a normalized spectral coordinate, accounting for dependencies between newly and previously admitted frequencies. Dyadic targets allocate larger coordinate increments to earlier cascades and are mapped to fixed supports before training, enabling coarse-to-fine reconstruction with unchanged sampling masks and data consistency. On fastMRI and Stanford2D, STAIRS improves matched Transformer (HUMUSNet) and convolutional (NAFNet) backbones in single- and multi-coil reconstruction, including radiologist-annotated pathology regions. It also outperforms predictor-based progression with lower parameter overhead and inference latency. Stage-wise analysis shows repeated error correction by projection and lower intermediate reconstruction errors than the backbones alone throughout support expansion. Ablations favor dyadic allocation over uniform expansion for square and line-band supports and over manual front-loading for square windows. Support projection alone improves reconstruction, and transition conditioning strengthens the gain. Cross-dataset evaluation shows improvements for both backbones in both directions. Support allocation thus complements regularizer design in unrolled MRI reconstruction.

</details>


### [45] [Domain Generalization for Style-Debiased Object Detection via Label-Consistent Training across Multiple Styles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10262)
*Chaoran Zhang,Shirshak Bhusal,Zhilin Gao,Qiang Xue,Haoting Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅用源域数据的OAMSC框架，通过多风格扩散合成与原点锚定的跨风格一致性约束，抑制风格敏感的捷径学习，提升目标检测的域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测器在视觉差异显著的未见域上性能大幅下降，原因是模型局限于有限训练风格，易过拟合表层统计线索而非目标级语义；受人类感知优先关注语义相关特征、忽略无关风格线索的启发。

Method: 1) 数据准备：用现成扩散模型将源数据集扩展为20种风格，生成图像经人工校验以保证目标实例与标注在各风格变体间一致；2) 训练：在场景级与目标级施加原点锚定的一致性约束，强化域稳定特征学习、抑制风格敏感线索影响；3) 框架仅使用源域，数据-centric设计，原则上可与其它DG方法互补，并适用于一阶段与两阶段检测器。

Result: 在多个挑战性DG基准上的大量实验与消融表明，OAMSC在多种域偏移下均具有稳定有效性与强泛化性能；方法可同时用于一阶段和两阶段检测器。

Conclusion: 多风格数据合成与跨风格一致性学习的结合能有效抑制风格捷径、提升检测器的域泛化；公开训练代码及两个风格迁移数据集（合计约18万张图像）。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) for object detection aims to improve model performance on unseen target domains. Existing detectors, however, suffer substantial degradation on visually distinct unseen domains, as models confined to limited training styles tend to overfit superficial statistical cues rather than object-level semantics. Motivated by human perception, which attends primarily to semantically relevant features while ignoring irrelevant style cues, we propose Origin-Anchored Multi-Style Consistency (OAMSC), a source-only framework that couples multi-style data synthesis with cross-style consistency learning to suppress style-sensitive shortcut learning. For data preparation, we leverage off-the-shelf diffusion models to expand the source datasets into 20 distinct styles, with all generated images manually verified to ensure that object instances and annotations remain consistent across style variants. During training, we impose Origin-anchored consistency constraints at scene and object levels to reinforce the learning of domain-stable features while suppressing the influence of style-sensitive cues. Extensive experiments and ablation studies on multiple challenging DG benchmarks demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and strong generalization performance of OAMSC across diverse domain shifts. Notably, our approach applies to both one-stage and two-stage detectors, while its data-centric design is complementary in principle to other DG methods. The training code and two style-transfer datasets constructed for the experiments, comprising approximately 180K images in total, are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [46] [DAC-MACE: Context-Conditioned Expert Routing for Compact Scene Coordinate Regression in Camera Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10263)
*Dingying Fan,Mingkai Liu,Haohua Que,Haojia Gao,Xianliang Huang,Qian Zhang,Xueqian Wang,Fei Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DAC-MACE，通过重校准与上下文化特征改进 MoE-based 场景坐标回归中的专家路由，从而在紧凑地图下提升相机绝对位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE-based SCR 的路由器与专家共用同一局部描述子作为输入，导致不同位置的重复立面、窗户等外观相似的像素获得相似路由输入，专家分配不准确，限制了大场景精度。

Method: 1) 残差上下文适配器 RCA：对冻结编码器特征进行通道与空间位置重加权；2) 全局上下文编码器 GCE：以多尺度自注意力混合特征；3) 将图像级池化描述子加入路由器输入，实现软专家路由的条件化；4) 采用双阈值 RANSAC–Levenberg–Marquardt 估计器恢复位姿。

Result: Cambridge Landmarks 上三个随机种子平均的逐场景中位平移误差为 13.0 cm，优于 GLACE（13.6 cm）与 MACE（14.4 cm），最大场景误差由 24 cm 降至 19 cm。累积消融使平均误差由 17.0 cm 依次降至 14.4、14.0、13.6、13.0 cm。7-Scenes 上为单 ACE 头加入 GCE，Recall@(5 cm,5°) 由 86.56% 提升至 87.60%。

Conclusion: 在 MoE-based SCR 中，将路由决策建立在重校准、上下文化及全局池化特征之上，可有效缓解外观重复带来的歧义，配合双阈值位姿估计器在无需牺牲地图紧凑性的前提下取得 RGB-only SCR 中领先的定位精度。

Abstract: A camera becomes an absolute pose sensor when its images can be relocalized against a prebuilt map of the scene. On mobile robots and augmented-reality devices, that map must be small and quick to build. Scene coordinate regression (SCR) stores the scene in a compact network that predicts a 3D coordinate for every pixel, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) variants extend its capacity to larger outdoor scenes. In existing MoE-based SCR, however, the router assigns each pixel to an expert from the same local descriptor that the experts regress from, so repeated facades and windows at different locations receive similar routing inputs. We present DAC-MACE, which conditions soft expert routing on recalibrated and contextualized features. A residual context adapter (RCA) reweights the channels and locations of frozen encoder features, a global context encoder (GCE) mixes them with multi-scale self-attention, and an image-level pooled descriptor is added to the router input. A dual-threshold RANSAC--Levenberg--Marquardt estimator recovers the pose. On Cambridge Landmarks, DAC-MACE attains a mean per-scene median translation error of 13.0 cm over three seeds, the lowest among the RGB-only SCR methods compared (GLACE 13.6 cm, MACE 14.4 cm), and reduces the error on the largest scene from 24 to 19 cm relative to MACE. In a cumulative study, the MoE head, GCE, RCA, and the dual-threshold estimator lower the mean error from 17.0 cm to 14.4, 14.0, 13.6, and 13.0 cm. On 7-Scenes, adding GCE to a single ACE head raises Recall@(5 cm, 5$^\circ$) from 86.56% to 87.60%.

</details>


### [47] [Uncertainty, Not Concealment: A Mechanistic Reappraisal of Sycophancy in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10297)
*Mohamed I. Eldagla,Zeyad H. ElShafei,Ahmed M. Anter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出三部分诊断方法（表征探针、激活引导、双向强制选择），检验医学VLM是否"内部检测到X线-报告矛盾却隐瞒不报"，结论是模型并非隐瞒已知矛盾，而是将不匹配编码为被门控的"笼统不确定性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学检索增强生成中的VLM需在检索报告与X线矛盾时主动指出冲突，这关系到安全部署。"谄媚式掩盖假说"（sycophancy mask hypothesis）认为模型内部已检测到冲突但拒绝报告，从而掩盖了已检测到的矛盾。作者希望区分"掩盖"与"不确定性"这两种解释。

Method: 在4个指令微调的胸部X线VLM上，采用三部分诊断：(1)表征探针（线性探针区分匹配/不匹配输入，并与CLIP基线对比）；(2)激活引导（沿学到的冲突方向steering）；(3)双向强制二选一，检验能否逼出矛盾报告；同时分析跨模态注意力在图像上的分配。

Result: 行为层面确认了对矛盾的抑制；线性探针能区分匹配与不匹配输入，且优于简单CLIP模型；但沿冲突方向引导从未诱发出矛盾陈述，强制二选一也无法恢复；不匹配样本上模型对图像的跨模态注意力下降。

Conclusion: 模型不指出矛盾并非因为隐瞒已检测到的知识，而是不匹配被登记为被门控的、含糊化的不确定性；作者据此主张重新接地（re-grounding）与不确定性显式化（uncertainty surfacing）。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) in medical retrieval-augmented generation must be able to say when a retrieved report contradicts the X-ray, so understanding whether they can detect such conflicts is important for safe deployment. The sycophancy mask hypothesis holds that these models internally detect conflicts but refuse to report them, concealing a detected contradiction. We introduce a three-part diagnostic that separates concealment from uncertainty: representation probing, activation steering, and two-way forced choice. Across four instruction-tuned VLMs on chest X-rays, we confirm behavioral suppression of contradiction and find that a linear probe separates matched from mismatched inputs, beyond what a simple CLIP model achieves. Steering the learned conflict direction never elicits contradiction, and forcing a binary choice does not recover one; instead, cross-modal attention to the image drops on mismatched pairs. We conclude that these models fail to contradict because the mismatch registers as hedged, gated uncertainty rather than concealed knowledge, calling for re-grounding and uncertainty surfacing. Code is available at https://github.com/mohamed-eldagla/Uncertainty-Not-Concealment/.

</details>


### [48] [AI-Driven Repair of Missing Automotive Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10318)
*Kazim Abbas,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估一条利用配对观测（完整模型与合成损伤模型）重建缺失几何作为修复补丁的流程，20个汽车CAD模型共160次试验中109次同时满足voxel IoU>0.85与相对体积误差<15%阈值；加入DINOv2未提升精度且更慢；仅打印修复段可减少93.08%耗材与89.39%打印时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车零部件供应受限，整体更换昂贵耗时，而在大部分结构仍可用时整体更换造成浪费；因此需要判断缺失几何能否被重建为可打印的局部修复补丁。

Method: 基于相机视点渲染无噪的彩色、深度与轮廓图像；用深度差识别潜在缺失区域；采用多视图体素重建估计缺失部分；并引入预训练DINOv2验证候选区域、考察预训练视觉特征能否改善重建。实验使用20个汽车CAD模型，每个合成移除一部分，在4种条件下对比两种重建方法，共160次试验（含8视图/4相邻视图等），以voxel IoU>0.85和绝对相对体积误差<15%双阈值为评价标准。

Result: 160次试验中109次同时通过双阈值。纯几何重建在8视图下20个部件通过16个，4相邻视图下仅通过10个。37次失败中24次属于在应为空区域错误添加材料（过度填充）。DINOv2未提升整体精度，且使预测生成更慢。13项验证试验中，中位数显示仅打印修复段相比整体打印减少93.08%耗材与89.39%时间。

Conclusion: 多视图体素几何重建可有效生成修复补丁，视图数量与视角覆盖显著影响成功率，主要失效模式为过度填充；预训练视觉特征（DINOv2）在本文设置下无增益；局部补丁打印在材料与时间上收益显著，支持"修复而非更换"的路径。

Abstract: Due to supply constraints, replacing damaged automotive components can be expensive and time-consuming, while replacing the entire component can be wasteful when much of it remains usable. This paper presents a pipeline to investigate whether missing geometry can be reconstructed as a repair patch using paired observations of the intact models and synthetically damaged components. Noise-free color, depth, and silhouette images were produced using the camera viewpoints. Depth differences were then used to identify the potential missing regions, while multi-view voxel reconstruction was used to estimate the missing portion. A pretrained DINOv2 model was also added to validate missing candidates and determine whether pretrained visual features could improve reconstruction. We tested the pipeline on 20 automotive CAD models, each with a synthetically removed portion. Each model was tested under four different conditions using two reconstruction methods for a total of 160 trials. The predictions were compared with the missing geometry of the unchanged ground-truth using predefined thresholds of the voxel intersection-over-union over 0.85 and the absolute relative volume error less than 15%. Of the 160 experimental trials, 109 of them satisfied both thresholds. Geometry-only reconstruction passed 16 of 20 components with eight views but only 10 of 20 with four adjacent views. Further analysis showed that 24 out of the 37 failed reconstructions could be categorized as unsuccessful because extra material was incorrectly added to areas that should have been empty. The implementation of DINOv2 into the pipeline did not improve the overall reconstruction accuracy and made prediction generation slower. For 13 verified tests, printing only the repaired sections instead of the entire parts reduced filament use by 93.08% and printing time by 89.39% based on the median results.

</details>


### [49] [LoCo: Selective Local Competition for Discriminative Open-Vocabulary Multi-Label Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10321)
*Zhuoming Li,Bo Han,Xiaoyu Wang,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoCo，一个选择性局部竞争框架，通过空间一致性视觉编码器与选择性Softmax（仅在局部不兼容类别间进行竞争）挖掘局部预测作为判别性分支，并结合多尺度推理与全局预测器集成，在四个基准上显著优于TagCLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词表多标签识别方法主要改进全局图文匹配或文本侧表示，局部预测被严重忽视；作者重访局部预测，指出局部语义稀疏性（每个图像区域仅关联少量查询概念，但不同粒度/类型的兼容概念可共存）使得局部预测在处理好空间一致性与兼容性感知的局部竞争后可成为强判别分支。

Method: LoCo框架包含三点：(1) 采用空间一致的视觉编码器以降低局部特征纠缠；(2) 提出Selective Softmax，仅在局部不兼容类别之间施加竞争，同时保留兼容类别的响应；(3) 多尺度推理提升不同尺度下的局部预测，并可与现有全局预测器集成以提供互补的图像级上下文。

Result: 在四个多标签基准上，独立LoCo相较TagCLIP平均F1和mAP分别提升3.4%和3.8%；LoCo+DualPrompt取得对比方法中最高平均值，较TagCLIP提升4.8%和5.2%。

Conclusion: 局部语义稀疏性可作为开发更具判别力的开放词表多标签识别方法的有效基础；代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Multi-Label Recognition (OV-MLR) aims to identify all queried semantic concepts present in an image. Existing methods mainly improve global image-text matching or text-side representations, leaving local prediction largely under-explored. In this paper, we revisit local prediction and reveal that it can serve as a strong discriminative branch once two properties are properly handled: spatial consistency of local visual features and compatibility-aware local competition. This perspective is motivated by local semantic sparsity: each image region is related to only a small subset of queried concepts, while compatible concepts of different granularities or types may still coexist locally. We instantiate this perspective with LoCo, a selective Local Competition framework for OV-MLR. LoCo adopts a spatially consistent visual encoder to reduce local feature entanglement, and introduces Selective Softmax to impose competition only among locally incompatible categories while preserving compatible responses. Multi-scale inference further improves local prediction at different scales. LoCo can also be ensembled with an existing global predictor to provide complementary image-level context. Experiments on four multi-label benchmarks show that standalone LoCo improves average F1-score and mAP over TagCLIP by 3.4% and 3.8%, respectively. LoCo + DualPrompt achieves the highest averages among the compared methods, with gains of 4.8% and 5.2% over TagCLIP. Our results suggest that local semantic sparsity offers a useful basis for developing more discriminative open-vocabulary multi-label recognition methods. The code is available at https://github.com/lee-plus-plus/LoCo.

</details>


### [50] [TimeTraveler: Temporal Strategy Planning with Time Dictionary for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10337)
*Minkuk Kim,Junho Kim,Seong Tae Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TimeTraveler 流式视频问答框架，用时间戳索引的结构化字幕构建"Time Dictionary"作为可查询证据源，并按查询的时间意图选择回看过去、读取现在或等待未来的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频系统依赖紧凑视觉记忆或固定证据访问方案，易丢失细粒度细节，且无法根据每条查询的时间意图自适应调整回答过程。

Method: 将每个观测时刻存为时间戳索引的结构化字幕形成时间字典；给定查询后先判断应回忆过去、读取当前还是等待未来，再执行对应策略的证据获取流程。

Result: 在流式视频问答基准上的对比实验表明，方法通过保留细粒度时间证据并为每条查询选择合适的时间策略，提升了流式问答性能。

Conclusion: 显式可查询的时间字典加查询级时间策略规划，是提升流式视频理解中证据保留与访问适应性的有效途径。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) requires models to answer user queries over continuously evolving video streams. Unlike offline video QA, SVU must handle queries whose evidence may lie in the past, appear in the current scene, or emerge only after future observations. Existing streaming video systems often rely on compact visual memories or fixed evidence-access schemes, which can lose fine-grained details and fail to adapt the answering process to the temporal intent of each query. In this paper, we propose TimeTraveler, a streaming VQA framework that performs temporal strategy planning with a Time Dictionary. Inspired by the need to search across different temporal regions of a stream, TimeTraveler stores each observed moment as a timestamp-indexed structured caption and uses this dictionary as an explicit source of queryable evidence. Given a query, TimeTraveler determines whether to recall past evidence, read the present context, or wait for future observations, and then applies a strategy-specific evidence acquisition process. Comparative experiments on streaming video QA benchmarks show that TimeTraveler improves streaming question answering by preserving fine-grained temporal evidence and selecting the appropriate temporal strategy for each query. The project page is available at https://geppa.github.io/TimeTraveler.

</details>


### [51] [MaRiO: Multi-agent Collaborative Reasoning via Shared Observations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10344)
*Nathaniel Redmond,Fidel Omar Tito Cruz,Devansh Sharma,Shehreen Azad,Sirshapan Mitra,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S. Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaRiO基准，用于评估多智能体协作推理，并测试了23个先进MLLM，揭示其在跨智能体对应与几何线索利用上的不足，并初步探索几何引导的合成视图增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测多集中于单视角或共享相机参数的多视角设置，缺乏对多智能体从独立视角观察同一环境并协作推理这一场景的探索，而这种能力对多模态具身系统至关重要。

Method: 构建MaRiO基准，模拟多个智能体从各自独立视角观察共享环境的协作场景，要求模型跨独立观测进行推理；在该基准上评测23个SOTA MLLM；并尝试几何引导的合成视图增强——在智能体观测之间生成中间过渡帧以提供辅助空间上下文。

Result: 发现三点：(1) 由静态感知主导的任务普遍易于需要变换性事件序列的任务；(2) 多数模型难以解决跨智能体对应关系，错误随场景复杂度提升而增加；(3) 模型对场景中几何线索的利用能力有限。几何引导合成视图增强在部分任务类别上提升了性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作推理仍是开放挑战，是多模态具身系统的重要研究方向；几何引导的视图增强可作为初步缓解手段。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual perception capabilities, but existing evaluations largely focus on single-view or multi-view settings with shared camera parameters, leaving the challenge of multi-agent collaborative scenarios underexplored. In such settings, multiple agents observe a shared environment from independent viewpoints, and reliable decisions require reasoning across these independent observations of the same environment. To study this problem, we introduce MaRiO, a benchmark for evaluating multi-agent collaborative reasoning, and evaluate 23 state-of-the-art MLLMs on it. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) tasks governed by static perception are generally easier than tasks that require transformational event sequences; (2) most models struggle to resolve cross-agent correspondences, with errors increasing with scene complexity; and (3) models show limited ability to leverage geometric cues present in the scene. As an initial step toward addressing these challenges, we explore geometry-guided synthetic view augmentation that generates intermediate transition frames between agent observations, providing auxiliary spatial context that improves performance in certain categories. Overall, our results highlight that multi-agent collaborative reasoning remains an open challenge and an important direction for multimodal embodied systems. The code is available on the project page: https://omarftt.github.io/MaRiO-page/.

</details>


### [52] [PoseBench: Probing Pose Awareness in Visual Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10347)
*Most Nilufa Yeasmin,Draix Wyatt,Ruyi Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoseBench，系统评测 2D 视觉基础模型特征是否具备"姿态感知"（pose awareness）这一隐式 3D 结构属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态敏感型 3D 视觉任务需要特征空间与物体姿态流形对齐，但主流视觉基础模型仅在单张 2D 图像上预训练、无显式 3D 或姿态监督，因此"2D 基础模型经大规模预训练后是否具备姿态感知"这一基本问题尚缺系统答案。

Method: 构建 PoseBench 基准，采用渐进式评测设计：先用留出的渲染视图在受控条件下探测内在姿态感知结构，再用真实图像查询检验该结构在真实世界变化下的可访问性与鲁棒性；并引入基于姿态图的特征细化（pose-graph refinement）作为互补探针，衡量特征邻域与底层姿态流形的兼容程度。评测覆盖 10 个预训练家族、31 个公开 checkpoint。

Result: 姿态感知是 2D 视觉基础模型的一种隐式但非均匀的属性，在不同预训练家族与 checkpoint 间差异明显；姿态图细化带来一致的性能提升，说明特征空间保留了与底层姿态流形兼容的结构。

Conclusion: PoseBench 为理解并选择面向姿态敏感 3D 视觉任务的预训练特征提供了系统化依据。

Abstract: Pose awareness characterizes the extent to which a learned feature space aligns with the underlying object-pose manifold, such that observations with similar poses exhibit greater feature similarity than those with increasingly different poses. While such structure is desirable for pose-sensitive 3D vision tasks, visual foundation models pre-trained on individual 2D images typically lack explicit 3D or pose supervision. This raises a fundamental question: Do 2D visual foundation models exhibit pose awareness after large-scale pretraining? To answer this question, we introduce PoseBench, a systematic benchmark to probe pose awareness in pretrained visual features. Our evaluation follows a progressive design: held-out rendered views probe intrinsic pose-aware structure under controlled conditions, while real-image queries evaluate how robustly this structure remains accessible under real-world variations. We further introduce pose-graph-based refinement as a complementary probe of compatibility between feature neighborhoods and the underlying pose manifold. Across 31 publicly available checkpoints from 10 pretraining families, PoseBench reveals that pose awareness is an implicit but non-uniform property of 2D visual foundation models, and consistent gains from pose-graph refinement suggest that feature spaces retain structure compatible with the underlying pose manifold. PoseBench therefore provides a systematic basis for understanding and selecting pretrained features for pose-sensitive 3D vision tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Guided Co-Attention with Adaptive Gating for Multimodal Medical Data Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10348)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Priyanka Siddappa,Md Shifat Haider Al Amin,Ruizhe Ma,Md Ripon Ahammed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Guided Co-Attention with Adaptive Gating融合框架，结合token级引导、双向交叉注意力和样本自适应融合，在胸片-报告图像文本二分类上取得最高平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合不仅要跨模态交换信息，还需判断哪些表征应参与交互、以及如何组合互补的跨模态视图；反向查询与源模态并不产生可互换的表征。

Method: 构建双向表征：文本token查询视觉特征的图像条件文本表征，以及反向的文本条件图像表征；在交叉注意力前用token级引导调制表征；再用自适应门为每个样本学习两个方向表征的相对贡献。

Result: 以配对胸片与放射报告做正常/异常二分类，两个交叉注意力方向性能特征不同；消融显示双向交互、自适应门控与token引导收益互补；模型达96.05%准确率、96.10% F1、98.37% AUC、95.60% Sens@95Spec、96.51% Spec@95Sens，所有指标平均最高。

Conclusion: 构建多模态表征时应显式建模交互方向、token相关性与样本相关的融合方式。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion requires not only exchanging information across modalities but also determining which representations should participate in the interaction and how complementary cross-modal views should be combined. We introduce Guided Co-Attention with Adaptive Gating, a fusion framework that integrates token-wise guidance, bidirectional cross-attention, and sample-specific fusion. The proposed method constructs two directional representations: an image-conditioned text representation, obtained by allowing textual tokens to query visual features, and a text-conditioned image representation formed in the reverse direction. Token-wise guidance modulates the representations before cross-attention, while an adaptive gate learns the relative contribution of the two resulting directional representations for each sample. We evaluate the approach using paired chest radiographs and radiology reports as an image-text test bed for binary (normal vs. abnormal) classification. The two cross-attention directions exhibit distinct performance characteristics, demonstrating that reversing the query and source modalities does not produce interchangeable representations. Ablation experiments further show that bidirectional interaction, adaptive gating, and token guidance provide complementary benefits. The proposed model achieves 96.05% accuracy, 96.10% F1-score, 98.37% AUC, 95.60% Sens@95Spec, and 96.51% Spec@95Sens, yielding the highest mean performance across all evaluated metrics. These findings support explicitly modeling interaction direction, token relevance, and sample-dependent integration when constructing multimodal representations.

</details>


### [54] [DAPointRWKV: Domain Adaptive Vision RWKV for Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10353)
*Yinghui Li,Qianyu Zhou,Hao Yang,Richard Dazeley,Ye Zhu,Xuequan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于RWKV的域自适应点云补全框架DAPointRWKV，含几何感知RWKV主干、渐进解码器与双重结构正则化三部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNNs、Transformers及Mamba类序列模型在域自适应点云补全中受限于感受野有限、计算复杂度高、几何偏置不足与序列化敏感性；RWKV虽具线性复杂度长程建模能力，但直接迁移存在三大问题：缺乏显式几何先验、循环建模与由粗到细生成不匹配、目标域自适应不稳定。

Method: 框架由三模块构成：①Geometry-Aware RWKV (GA-RWKV)主干，引入几何感知的循环表示学习；②RWKV-Oriented Progressive Decoder (RWKV-PD)，通过局部token引导细化，将RWKV token表示与由粗到细重建衔接；③Dual Structural Regularization (DSR)，借助可见结构锚定与完整形状假设稳定化来稳定目标域迁移。

Result: 在合成与真实世界基准上，DAPointRWKV一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RWKV可有效适配域自适应点云补全，几何先验注入、渐进解码与双重结构正则化共同解决了迁移中的不匹配与不稳定问题。

Abstract: Domain adaptive point cloud completion (DA PCC) aims to bridge the geometric discrepancy between labeled source domains and unlabeled target domains for accurate 3D shape completion. Existing methods based on CNNs, Transformers, and Mamba-like sequence models still face challenges from limited receptive fields, high computational complexity, insufficient geometric bias, and serialization sensitivity. Recently, Receptance Weighted Key Value (RWKV) provides a linear-complexity alternative for long-range sequence modeling. However, adapting RWKV to DA PCC remains challenging due to the lack of explicit geometric priors, the mismatch between recurrent modeling and coarse-to-fine generation, and unstable target-domain adaptation. To address these challenges, we propose DAPointRWKV, a novel RWKV-based framework for DA PCC. DAPointRWKV consists of three components: Geometry-Aware RWKV (GA-RWKV) Backbone, RWKV-Oriented Progressive Decoder (RWKV-PD), and Dual Structural Regularization (DSR). Specifically, GA-RWKV introduces geometry-aware recurrent representation learning, RWKV-PD bridges RWKV token representations with coarse-to-fine reconstruction through local token-guided refinement, and DSR stabilizes target-domain transfer through visible-structure anchoring and complete-shape hypothesis stabilization. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DAPointRWKV consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [55] [ClearForm: Shared-Surface Gaussian Splatting for Transparent and Opaque Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10370)
*Hajeong Lee,Jong-Il Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ClearForm，基于共享平面高斯实现透明界面与透射外观的联合重建，缓解几何-外观合成困境(GACD)，在3D-FRONT-T与TransLab上显著提升CD、F1与PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明界面的观测颜色多来自后方场景，导致玻璃表面几何信号弱。作者识别出几何-外观合成困境(GACD)：同一依赖透射率的alpha同时控制颜色与几何，揭示背景会削弱透明表面对几何重建的贡献，从而损害基于平面的深度估计与界面支撑。

Method: 1) 共享平面高斯表示，联合重建透明界面与透射外观；2) 几何保持的双不透明度表面重建：共享平面高斯在几何合成中保留界面贡献，在RGB中以双不透明度建模透射与稀疏反射，并配以透射率感知的几何约束；3) 双证据生命周期(DEL)：为两类合成器不同的可见性需求保留由多视图几何或RGB重建证据支撑的高斯。

Result: 3D-FRONT-T上CD 27.05 mm、F1 0.928（PGSR复现为59.18 mm，GLINT报告34 mm/0.836）；TransLab上CD 1.65 mm、F1 0.963、PSNR 40.14 dB。匹配消融中，双不透明度合成将CD从59.25降至27.09 mm，DEL提升PSNR 2.06 dB。

Conclusion: 通过解耦几何与外观的不透明度并对高斯生命周期进行证据化管理，可有效缓解透明界面重建中的几何-外观冲突，在合成与真实透明数据集上均取得领先的几何与外观精度。

Abstract: Transparent-interface reconstruction must recover glass surfaces even when most observed color comes from the scene behind them. We present ClearForm, a shared-planar-Gaussian method for jointly reconstructing transparent interfaces and the appearance seen through them. We identify and analyze the Geometry-Appearance Compositing Dilemma (GACD): when the same transparency-dependent alpha controls both color and geometry, revealing the background can weaken a transparent surface's contribution to geometric reconstruction. We derive its effect on plane-based depth estimation, identify a mechanism that can weaken geometric learning, and quantify the loss of interface support through controlled comparisons. To address this conflict, we develop geometry-preserving dual-opacity surface reconstruction. Shared planar Gaussians retain interface contributions in geometry compositing while modeling transmission and sparse reflection in RGB, supported by transparency-aware geometric constraints. To manage the different primitive-visibility requirements of these compositors, we introduce Dual-Evidence Lifecycle (DEL), which retains Gaussians supported by multi-view geometry or RGB reconstruction evidence. On 3D-FRONT-T, ClearForm achieves 27.05 mm Chamfer distance (CD) and 0.928 F1, compared with 59.18 mm CD for our PGSR reproduction and the reported 34 mm CD and 0.836 F1 for GLINT. On TransLab, it achieves 1.65 mm CD, 0.963 F1, and 40.14 dB PSNR. In matched ablations, dual-opacity compositing reduces CD from 59.25 to 27.09 mm, while DEL improves PSNR by 2.06 dB. Our project page is available at https://hajeongx.github.io/clearform/.

</details>


### [56] [PhysDNet: Physics-Driven Gradient Amplification for Real-Time Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10376)
*Rayen Dhahri,Lei Sun,Victor Javier Kartsch Morinigo,Alice Pagnoux,Luca Benini,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量多模态去雾网络PhysDNet，融合近红外图像与稀疏LiDAR深度，采用物理约束双头目标训练，在低算力下实现高保真去雾并可实时部署于边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去雾网络计算开销大且易过拟合合成雾，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上实时部署，亟需高效、高保真的复原方法。

Method: 构建轻量多模态架构，融合NIR影像与稀疏LiDAR深度；以固定Koschmieder大气散射模型反演进行可微求导，产生密度自适应梯度放大，作为隐式资源分配机制聚焦退化严重区域；采用物理约束双头训练目标，推理阶段不增加开销。

Result: 在Seeing Through Fog基准上较对比方法提升1.67 dB，计算量低5.2倍，并恢复54.9%的雾致检测损失；消融与跨域实验表明物理引导训练可提升泛化性，包括违反Koschmieder假设的非均匀雾场景；与固定功能NPU兼容，L/M/S三个变体在<4 W功耗下分别达29/45/99 FPS。

Conclusion: 物理引导的可微训练能在极小模型容量下带来显著精度与训练效率收益，实现边缘端实时去雾，且对非理想雾分布具有更好泛化性。

Abstract: Modern dehazing networks are often computationally expensive and overfit to synthetic haze, limiting real-time deployment. This underscores an urgent demand for efficient, high-fidelity restoration methods that can operate on resource-constrained edge devices in practical, dynamic environments. We introduce PhysDNet, a lightweight multimodal architecture that fuses NIR imagery with sparse LiDAR depth and is trained with a physics-constrained dual-head objective. Differentiating through a fixed Koschmieder inversion induces density-adaptive gradient amplification, acting as an implicit resource allocation mechanism that concentrates learning on heavily degraded regions without increasing inference cost. PhysDNet delivers superior restoration with minimal capacity and enhanced training efficiency, facilitating real-time deployment on edge devices without increasing inference overhead. On the Seeing Through Fog benchmark, PhysDNet outperforms competitors by 1.67 dB at 5.2 times lower compute while recovering 54.9% of fog-induced detection loss. Ablations and cross-domain evaluations show that physics-guided training improves generalization, including under non-homogeneous haze that violates the Koschmieder assumption. PhysDNet further demonstrates compatibility with fixed-function NPUs, running at 29/45/99 FPS at <4 W across L/M/S variants. Code, deployment scripts and pre-trained checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Heatdh/PhysDnet.

</details>


### [57] [Vision Language Models for Primary Structure Threat Identification in Wildland Urban Interface Fires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10377)
*Zhangding Liu,Neda Mohammadi,John E. Taylor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 在野火-城市交界区（WUI）活跃火情下进行结构级火灾威胁识别的能力，采用设计场景合成图像与受控燃烧实验双轨评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于火灾检测与灾后损害评估，而活跃 WUI 火灾事件中的结构级火威胁识别尚缺乏系统评估，亟需快速视觉判断手段。

Method: 采用两条独立计分的评测轨道：491 个设计 WUI 场景生成的 1,824 张合成图像，以及与消防部门合作受控燃烧实验的 45 帧图像；在零样本提示下评测 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 的字段输出有效性与目标识别准确率。

Result: 零样本下模型在 1,808 张合成图像与 39 帧受控燃烧图像上给出有效目标字段；有效输出准确率分别为合成轨 94.0%、受控燃烧轨 69.2%，后者随机选择基线为 16.0%。合成场景中火蔓延线索视觉显著时模型多数能识别目标；受控燃烧中错误主要源于复杂场景空间关系理解困难。

Conclusion: 双轨协议既能提供可扩展的设计场景压力测试，又能以受控燃烧作为物理火条件下的行为参照；未来可用合成数据对 VLM 进行后训练，并在更大、更多样的真实 WUI 火灾数据集上评测适配模型。

Abstract: Wildland urban interface (WUI) fires increasingly threaten communities and infrastructure, motivating the need for rapid visual assessment. Recent research has focused primarily on detecting fire and assessing post-event damage. However, structure-level fire threat identification during active WUI fire events remains underexplored. This study evaluates Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct for this active-fire decision using two separately scored tracks: 1,824 synthetic images from 491 designed WUI scenarios and 45 frames from a controlled-burn experiment conducted in collaboration with Columbus Fire & EMS Department. Under zero-shot prompting, the model returned valid target fields for 1,808 synthetic images and 39 controlled-burn frames. Accuracy among valid outputs was 94.0% for the synthetic track and 69.2% for the controlled-burn track, compared with a 16.0% random-selection baseline for the latter. On the synthetic track, the model generally identified the intended target across varied designed WUI scenarios when the relevant fire-spread cues were visually distinct. Controlled-burn performance was well above random selection, but errors showed difficulty interpreting spatial relations in complex scenes. The dual-track protocol provides scalable stress testing in designed scenarios and a controlled-burn reference for assessing model behavior under physical fire conditions. Future work can use the synthetic data for VLM post-training and then evaluate the adapted models on larger and more diverse real WUI fire datasets.

</details>


### [58] [RefineSplat: Reconstruction-Guided Object-Centric Gaussian Splatting for Open-Vocabulary Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10379)
*Yiren Lu,Disheng Liu,Sreya Srinidhi,Yunlai Zhou,Yiran Qiao,Chaoda Song,Tuo Liang,Jing Ma,Huan Wang,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RefineSplat，在“先分割后重建”（Segment-then-Splat）的物体中心范式上引入重建引导的闭环反馈，协同优化 2D 跟踪与 3D 高斯重建，并采用视角感知的多嵌入分配策略，同时发布 Blender-OVS 合成基准，实现统一的开词汇 3D 场景分割与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流“先重建后分割”（splat-then-segment）流程存在视角不一致、物体边界模糊、物体级对应关系弱等问题，尤其在复杂静态与动态场景中误差易传播；同时现成视频跟踪器输出的不可靠掩码会被带入物体重建。

Method: 延续其 Segment-then-Splat 的物体中心范式：先在 2D 中跟踪物体，再为每个物体用专属的 3D 高斯集合进行重建，保持显式的物体—高斯对应关系；引入重建引导反馈机制，利用渲染掩码与多视角一致性反向精化 2D 跟踪；提出视角感知嵌入分配，为每个物体维护多个按视角聚类的语言嵌入以刻画视角相关语义；构建 Blender-OVS 合成基准，包含静态与动态序列及精确物体网格与点云。

Result: 在静态与动态基准上，渲染视角分割性能提升，物体级 Chamfer 距离降低，场景重建质量具竞争力；Blender-OVS 支持对提取几何的直接评估。

Conclusion: 通过重建与 2D 跟踪的闭环耦合及多视角语义嵌入，RefineSplat 缓解了视角不一致与物体级对应弱的问题，为统一的开词汇 3D 分割与检索提供了更稳健的物体中心方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding aims to segment and retrieve arbitrary objects in 3D scenes using natural language queries. Most existing methods typically follow a splat-then-segment paradigm, where 3D Gaussian Splatting is first reconstructed and then augmented with labels or language features for subsequent segmentation. However, this pipeline often suffers from view inconsistency, ambiguous object boundaries, and weak object-level correspondence, especially in complex static and dynamic scenes. Building on our prior Segment-then-Splat framework, we present RefineSplat, a reconstruction-guided extension that retains its object-centric paradigm: it first tracks objects in 2D and then reconstructs each object with a dedicated set of 3D Gaussians, preserving explicit object-Gaussian correspondence for unified open-vocabulary segmentation and retrieval. To improve robustness, we further close the loop between 2D tracking and 3D reconstruction. Since off-the-shelf video trackers can produce unreliable masks that propagate errors into object reconstruction, we introduce a reconstruction-guided feedback mechanism that refines object tracking using rendered masks and multi-view consistency. Moreover, instead of assigning each object a single language embedding, we propose a viewpoint-aware embedding assignment strategy, where each object maintains multiple view-clustered embeddings to capture view-dependent semantic cues. We also introduce Blender-OVS, a synthetic benchmark of static and dynamic sequences with exact object meshes and point clouds, enabling direct evaluation of extracted geometry. Experiments on static and dynamic benchmarks show improved rendered-view segmentation and lower object-level Chamfer distances, with competitive scene reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [59] [From Reflection to Co-Evolution: Self-Evolving Agents with Locally Verified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10382)
*Xinxiang Yin,Xinglang Zhang,Haodong Chen,Zhiqing Cui,Weidong Tang,Yuxuan Wang,Junrui Wang,Jingyi Zhang,Jianfeng Zhang,Jian Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anchor方法：以"来源决策"作为记忆保留的检验标准，先定位关键轨迹步、抽取候选记忆，再用同上下文重放有/无该候选的来源决策，只保留能显著改变决策分布且来源角色对齐（成功经验强化成功、失败经验抑制失败）的记忆，从而在冻结策略下实现经验驱动的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长程交互学习中积累记忆，但看似合理的"经验教训"可能冗余，甚至强化不当行为。作者希望找到一个实用判据：记忆所来源的那一步决策，能否作为判断该记忆是否值得保留的测试。

Method: 1) 定位轨迹中具有关键影响的步骤；2) 从中抽取候选记忆；3) 在相同上下文下，分别带与不带该候选记忆重放来源决策，观察被探查分布的变化。基于此发现两个性质：多数候选几乎不改变分布；成功/失败来源动作都可能被强化或被抑制。据此提出Anchor：在保留前同时检验"位移幅度"与"来源角色对齐"两个条件。

Result: 在留出的ALFWorld与WebShop任务上，相对于各骨干网络最强的基线，成功率最高提升19.9%、平均得分最高提升17.2%。使用Qwen3.5-27B时，ALFWorld保留的记忆卡片比不过滤方案少41%，且在WebShop上与四个成本对比基线相比全部更优且消耗更少token。

Conclusion: 来源决策重放是一种有效、低成本的记忆筛选信号；经Anchor准入的记忆能引导后续轨迹，在不更新策略参数（冻结策略）的条件下实现经验驱动的持续改进，并同时改善效果与成本效率。局限性在于评测主要集中于两个文本交互环境，且依赖重放式探查的额外计算。

Abstract: LLM agents have made impressive progress in coding and mathematical reasoning, motivating growing interest in learning from long-horizon interaction. Memory supports this process, but plausible lessons can be redundant or reinforce inappropriate actions. Can a memory's source decision provide a practical test for whether to retain it? We localize consequential trajectory steps, extract candidate memories, and replay each source decision with and without its candidate under the same context. This reveals two properties: most candidates barely shift the probed distribution, and both successful and failed source actions can be reinforced or suppressed. These observations motivate Anchor, which tests shift magnitude and source-role alignment before retention. Admitted memories guide later trajectories, enabling experience-driven improvement under a frozen policy. On held-out ALFWorld and WebShop tasks, Anchor improves success rate and mean score by up to 19.9% and 17.2%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline for each backbone. With Qwen3.5-27B, it retains 41% fewer ALFWorld cards than unfiltered retention and outperforms all four WebShop cost-comparison baselines while using fewer tokens.

</details>


### [60] [Tiresias: Towards Language-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10392)
*Fengyi Wu,Yifei Dong,Yetong Sha,Lingdong Kong,Qiyu Hu,Bohan Xiong,Qi Dai,Alexander G. Hauptmann,Zhi-Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的语言条件世界建模任务LCWM及基准LCWM-Bench（整合15个数据集、142,987条轨迹/428,961条指令），并提出通过"选择性想象"进行规划的智能体Tiresias（扩散策略生成候选轨迹→轨迹排序器筛选→视频预训练世界模型想象视觉结果→视觉排序器评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件世界建模目前碎片化：不同领域在动作空间、数据集和评估协议上各不相同，缺乏共享的任务定义与评测平台，阻碍了跨领域比较与方法研究。

Method: 1）定义LCWM任务：输入初始智能体视角观测+语言指令，输出满足指令的动作序列；2）构建LCWM-Bench，统一动作接口与评估协议，覆盖导航、机器人、3D游戏、驾驶四类，含三种风格的指令；3）提出Tiresias：语言条件扩散策略生成候选轨迹，轨迹排序器进行初筛，视频预训练世界模型对候选进行视觉后果想象，再由视觉排序器结合轨迹分数选出最终规划。

Result: Tiresias在LCWM-Bench上取得有竞争力的规划与想象性能；消融实验表明轨迹排序与视觉验证具有互补收益；与替代候选生成方法结合时，训练得到的排序器仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: LCWM-Bench提供了统一的语言引导世界模型规划评测环境，Tiresias作为有效起点，为后续研究语言引导的想象式规划奠定基础。

Abstract: World models enable planning through internal simulation, while natural language provides intuitive task specification. Yet, language-conditioned world modeling remains fragmented across domains with disparate action spaces, datasets, and evaluation protocols. We introduce LCWM, a shared task that maps an initial agent-view observation and a language instruction to an action sequence that fulfills the instruction. Its accompanying benchmark, LCWM-Bench, integrates 15 datasets across navigation, robotics, 3D gaming, and driving under a shared action interface and evaluation protocol, pairing 142,987 episodes with 428,961 instructions in three styles. We further propose Tiresias, an agent that plans via selective imagination: a language-conditioned diffusion policy generates candidate trajectories, a trajectory ranker shortlists them, and a video-pretrained world model imagines their visual consequences. A visual ranker evaluates these rollouts, complementing trajectory scores to select the final plan. Tiresias achieves competitive planning and imagination performance on LCWM-Bench. Ablations demonstrate the complementary benefits of trajectory ranking and visual verification, while experiments with alternative proposal methods show that the trained rankers remain effective beyond our diffusion policy. Together, LCWM-Bench and Tiresias provide a unified evaluation setting and an effective starting point for studying language-guided planning through world models.

</details>


### [61] [Frequency-Structure Complementary Modeling for Hyperspectral Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10413)
*Song Qiya,Tian Siyu,Li Shutao,Dian Renwei,Sun Long,Liu Jinyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出频率-结构互补建模网络FSCM-Net，将频域依赖学习（SSFCA）与结构自适应局部细化（SC-DFF）耦合，用于高光谱与多光谱图像融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖卷积聚合或注意力空间-光谱交互，但特征域注意力未显式刻画沿空间token与学习到的隐通道坐标的变化模式，且空间共享的局部算子缺乏对异质邻域结构自适应聚合的灵活性。

Method: FSCM-Net包含两模块：(1) SSFCA利用傅里叶交互的query-key表示构建空间token亲和度，并在沿学习通道坐标经输入条件频域滤波后构建隐通道亲和度，隐通道响应经共享增益校准后与空间token响应融合；(2) SC-DFF将MSI特征的正则化结构张量线索与上下文调制的HSI细节表示结合，生成通道与位置相关的聚合核，实现平滑区、边缘、纹理的自适应局部细化并保持光谱保真。两模块在多尺度编码器-解码器中渐进集成。

Result: 在两个仿真基准和一个真实遥感数据集上，空间细节重建与光谱保持性能优于代表性融合方法。

Conclusion: 频域依赖建模与结构自适应局部精化的互补设计可有效协调表示级交互与结构自适应重建，提升HSI-MSI融合效果。

Abstract: Existing methods mainly rely on convolutional aggregation or attention-based spatial-spectral interaction. However, conventional feature-domain attention does not explicitly characterize variation patterns along spatial-token and learned latent-channel coordinates, while spatially shared local operators lack the flexibility to adapt neighborhood aggregation to heterogeneous structures. To address these limitations, we propose a Frequency-Structure Complementary Modeling Network (FSCM-Net), which couples frequency-aware dependency learning with structure-adaptive local refinement. Specifically, a Spatial-Spectral Frequency Calibration Attention (SSFCA) module constructs spatial-token affinities from Fourier-interacted query-key representations and builds latent-channel affinities after input-conditioned frequency filtering along learned channel coordinates. The latent-channel response is then calibrated by a learned shared gain and combined with the spatial-token response. Complementarily, a Structure-Coherent Dynamic Feature Fusion (SC-DFF) module combines regularized structure-tensor cues from MSI features with context-modulated HSI detail representations to generate channel- and position-dependent aggregation kernels. This design makes local HSI refinement responsive to smooth regions, edges, and textures while maintaining spectral fidelity. The two modules are progressively integrated within a multiscale encoder-decoder to coordinate representation-level interaction and structure-adaptive reconstruction. Experiments on two simulated benchmarks and one real remote-sensing dataset demonstrate favorable performance against representative fusion methods in spatial detail reconstruction and spectral preservation.

</details>


### [62] [S2HAND: Accuracy-Preservation Trade-Offs for Hand Specialization in Whole-Body Pose Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10416)
*Singyu Muk,Xuanren Song,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2HAND 是在 Sapiens2-5B 全身姿态估计框架上做手部专项强化的方案：MT 版仅新增 0.87M 参数的手部分支以严格保留其余 268 个关键点热图输出，Pose 版则微调 3 个 Fisher 选出的 Transformer 块（267.13M 参数）以换取更强手部恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一全身姿态估计器同时预测身体、面部与手部关键点，但在手部较小、模糊或被遮挡时性能显著退化；而外挂独立手部专家模型会带来额外检测开销、坐标对齐问题与手腕拼接伪影。因此需要在不破坏原生 308 关键点输出接口的前提下提升手部精度。

Method: 提出 S2HAND 框架，保留原生 308 关键点输出接口。S2HAND-MT：将轻量 0.87M 参数姿态分支接入以输出 40 个手指热图，同时严格冻结共享 Transformer、辅助任务头及其余 268 个身体/足部/面部热图。S2HAND-Pose：进一步微调 3 个经 Fisher 信息筛选的 Transformer 块（267.13M 参数），以精确保持性为代价换取更强的手部恢复。另设可选 TailGuard 重裁剪模块。

Result: S2HAND-Pose 在 HInt TEST、COCO WholeBody VAL 与 FreiHAND EVAL 上将宏平均 AUC 从 57.83% 提升至 65.73%，并在全部 5 项 Native Pipeline 基准上超过 HInt 适配的 Hand5/HRNet 与拼接式 Hand5 系统；COCO 上手部 AP 提升 12.89 点，身体+足部 AP 提升 0.19 点。512 张图像多任务审计中与公开检查点的前景分割一致率为 99.60%，并量化了 normal 与 pointmap 漂移。2D 坐标在冻结 Pose2Mesh 迁移下降低 3D 网格误差；TailGuard 将 S2HAND-MT 在 NewDays 上的严重失败率从 13.45% 降至 10.43%。

Conclusion: S2HAND 以两种模式解决全身姿态估计的手部瓶颈：MT 提供对非手部输出的严格保持，Pose 提供经实证审计的性能—保持性权衡，二者均避免外挂手部专家带来的开销与拼接伪影。

Abstract: Unified whole-body pose estimators jointly predict body, face, and hand landmarks, but often degrade on small, blurred, or occluded hands. Attaching a standalone hand specialist introduces extra detection overhead, coordinate alignment, and wrist-stitching artifacts. We introduce S2HAND, a hand-specialized Sapiens2-5B framework that preserves the native 308-keypoint output interface. Specifically, S2HAND-MT adapts a lightweight 0.87M-parameter pose branch to route 40 finger heatmaps while strictly preserving the shared Transformer, auxiliary task heads, and the other 268 body, foot, and face heatmaps. S2HAND-Pose further fine-tunes three Fisher-selected Transformer blocks (267.13M parameters), trading exact preservation for stronger hand recovery: it lifts macro AUC from 57.83% to 65.73% across HInt TEST, COCO WholeBody VAL, and FreiHAND EVAL, and surpasses HInt-adapted Hand5/HRNet and stitched Hand5 systems on all five Native Pipeline benchmarks. On COCO, S2HAND-Pose improves hand AP by 12.89 points and body-plus-foot AP by 0.19 points. Across a 512-image multi-task audit, it maintains 99.60% foreground segmentation agreement with the public checkpoint, with quantified normal and pointmap drift. Its 2D coordinates also lower 3D mesh error in frozen Pose2Mesh transfer, while our optional TailGuard re-cropping module cuts S2HAND-MT's severe failure rate on NewDays from 13.45% to 10.43%. S2HAND thus resolves the whole-body hand bottleneck with either exact non-hand preservation (MT) or empirically audited trade-offs (Pose).

</details>


### [63] [Mental Imagery That Matters: Curing Latent Collapse in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10423)
*Mi Luo,Rohan Dugad,Zihui Xue,Alex Dimakis,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 "Mental Simulation Collapse (MSC)" 这一失效模式：潜空间多模态推理模型表面在进行交错视觉推理，但实际计算大多经由文本路径完成；并进一步提出 AnchorSim 对比蒸馏框架，使生成的视觉潜在状态真正影响模型输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理模拟（构建并更新内部视觉状态）是人类灵活认知的核心能力，而当前多模态大模型难以实现。潜空间多模态推理（交错文本与连续视觉潜块、无需解码为像素）被视为有希望的路径，但作者质疑这些视觉潜状态是否真正起作用。

Method: 1) 对代表性潜推理 MLLM 做直接扰动分析：删除、替换、打乱生成的视觉潜块，并与对语言推理轨迹的等效扰动对比，测量对最终答案概率的影响；2) 诊断 MSC 的训练目标根源（视觉对齐只要求潜块近似 helper-image 嵌入，不要求解码器依赖；纯文本监督使潜通路的语义角色不可辨识）；3) 提出 AnchorSim，一种对比蒸馏框架，训练模型使生成的视觉潜块具有行为因果性。

Result: 扰动实验显示视觉潜块被删除/替换/打乱后对答案概率影响很小，而文本轨迹的等效扰动损害显著；在多视觉推理基准上 AnchorSim 提升了性能，且诊断性重评估显示视觉扰动现在能实质性影响预测。

Conclusion: 潜空间多模态推理存在根本性失效模式 MSC：视觉潜状态未被模型利用。心理模拟必须锚定到模型行为上才能真正发挥作用，AnchorSim 为此提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Mental simulation, the ability to reason by constructing and updating internal visual states, is central to flexible human cognition, but remains elusive in current multimodal large language models. Latent multimodal reasoning offers a promising route toward this capability by interleaving text with continuous visual latent blocks, allowing models to maintain internal visual workspaces without decoding them into pixels. In this paper, we ask whether these latent visual states actually matter. Through a direct perturbation analysis across representative latent-reasoning MLLMs, we find that they often do not: dropping, replacing, or shuffling generated visual latents has only minor effects on the model's own final-answer probability, while equivalent perturbations to the linguistic reasoning trace are substantially more damaging. We call this failure mode Mental Simulation Collapse (MSC): the model appears to perform interleaved visual reasoning, but routes most answer-relevant computation through text. We further diagnose that MSC follows naturally from existing training objectives: visual alignment can make generated latents resemble helper-image embeddings, but it does not require the decoder to rely on them; subsequent text-only supervision further leaves the semantic role of the latent pathway unidentifiable. To address this, we introduce AnchorSim (Anchored Mental Simulation), a contrastive distillation framework that trains models to make their generated visual latents consequential. Across multiple visual reasoning benchmarks, AnchorSim improves performance, while diagnostic re-evaluation shows that visual perturbations now meaningfully affect predictions. Our results expose a fundamental failure mode of latent multimodal reasoning and demonstrate that mental simulation must be anchored to model behavior in order to matter.

</details>


### [64] [Split and Bridge: Multimodal Generation via Diffusion Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10441)
*Ahmad Arrabi,Xiaohan Zhang,Xingyu Li,Safwan Wshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SnB是一种自底向上的多模态生成框架，在采样阶段耦合预训练的单模态扩散模型，无需联合训练即可生成连贯的多模态样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态方法采用自顶向下范式，从零开始联合训练学习共享隐变量，导致可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出Split and Bridge（SnB）：不学习统一隐空间，而是让各模态沿共享扩散轨迹生成至"分裂点"，随后在桥接模块引导下分支为模态特定轨迹，从而即插即用地复用强预训练模型。

Result: 在PolyMNIST、CelebAMask-HQ及PIE-Bench++、ROCO等基准上，在样本质量与跨模态一致性之间取得良好平衡，并自然支持交错协同生成、图像编辑、风格耦合/解耦等零样本下游任务。

Conclusion: 自底向上生成是一种实用框架，可补充现有自顶向下多模态方法。

Abstract: Visual data are often expressed through multiple complementary modalities (e.g., images and segmentation masks), each capturing different aspects of the same underlying structure. Most existing multimodal methods adopt a top-down paradigm, learning a shared latent variable through joint training from scratch, which limits scalability. We propose Split and Bridge (SnB), a bottom-up framework for multimodal generation that couples pretrained unimodal diffusion models at sampling time. Rather than learning a unified latent space, SnB generates coherent multimodal samples by following a shared diffusion trajectory up to a split point and then branching into modality-specific trajectories guided by a bridge module. This design enables plug-and-play reuse of powerful pretrained models without joint training, making it scalable and easily adaptable. Across standard benchmarks (PolyMNIST, CelebAMask-HQ) and more complex settings (PIE-Bench++, ROCO), SnB achieves a strong balance between sample quality and coherence. We show that SnB naturally extends to downstream zero-shot tasks such as interleaved co-generation, image editing, and style coupling/decoupling. Our results demonstrate that bottom-up generation provides a practical framework that complements existing top-down multimodal approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/AhmadArrabi/SnB

</details>


### [65] [ESTRA: Enabling Extreme Attention Sparsity for Training-Free Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10448)
*Enhuai Liu,Yunke Wang,Yutong Wang,Changming Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ESTRA是一种免训练的视频扩散Transformer加速方法，通过聚类代表估计Key重要性并对簇代表做方差校正，在极端稀疏（1%-5%注意力密度）下保持生成质量，实现最高5.39×端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer的长时空序列注意力计算开销大；现有稀疏注意力在免训练近似方法中于高稀疏度下质量快速下降，限制了可达到的加速比。

Method: 免训练方法：1) 将精确注意力集中在少量重要Key-Value token上，其余贡献用簇代表近似；2) 对相似Query分组，用组代表为单个Key打分以高效识别重要token；3) 引入基于方差的校正，细化簇代表的注意力权重以提升近似精度。

Result: 在Wan2.1-14B与HunyuanVideo-13B上，5%注意力密度下分别实现3.26×和4.71×端到端加速，且在保真度及多数VBench维度上超过Sol-Attn与SVG-EAR；1%密度下仍比12.5%密度的Sol-Attn更接近密集生成，加速最高达5.39×。

Conclusion: ESTRA在免训练稀疏注意力中建立了新的质量-效率前沿，证明极端稀疏下仍可维持生成质量并取得显著加速。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers generate high-quality videos, but attention over long spatiotemporal sequences makes inference computationally expensive. Sparse attention reduces this cost by computing only a subset of query-key interactions exactly, and recent training-free methods further approximate the remaining interactions. These methods, however, degrade quickly at high sparsity, which limits the achievable speedup. We present ESTRA (Extreme Sparsity Through Refined Attention), a training-free acceleration method that preserves generation quality even at extreme sparsity. ESTRA concentrates exact attention computation on a small set of important key-value tokens and approximates the remaining contributions with cluster representatives. To identify these tokens efficiently, it groups similar queries and uses their representatives to score individual keys. A variance-based correction further refines the attention weights of the cluster representatives, improving the accuracy of the approximation. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B and HunyuanVideo-13B show that ESTRA establishes a new quality-efficiency frontier for training-free sparse attention, achieving $3.26\times$ and $4.71\times$ end-to-end speedups at 5% attention density while surpassing Sol-Attn and SVG-EAR in fidelity to dense generation and on most VBench dimensions. Even at 1% density, ESTRA remains more faithful to dense generation than Sol-Attn at 12.5%, while reaching up to $5.39\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [66] [WordEval: Evaluating Word-Native Operation Fidelity in Document Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10475)
*Jinyoung Park,Yupan Huang,Shimin Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Yilin Jia,Yuzhong Zhao,Tengchao Lv,Wenshan Wu,Shaohan Huang,Nan Yang,Xiangyang Zhou,Dongdong Zhang,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Sun Mao,Xun Wang,Tao Ge,Huitian Jiao,Si-Qing Chen,Changick Kim,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WordEval，一个以"操作保真度"为核心的Word编辑基准，通过Word原生oracle检查每次编辑是否在对象属性层面按意图实现，覆盖642个案例、15个功能领域，含546个原子案例（231条命令）与96个组合案例，并支持原子-组合案例关联的细粒度失败诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Word是办公自动化的核心环境，其编辑不同于纯文本编辑——它修改的是结构化对象模型，效果体现在对象属性上。现有基准把字处理文档当作众多任务领域之一，评估办公工作流或桌面自动化，并用关键词匹配、逐任务属性检查或截图比对来验证结果，无法判断"请求的编辑是否被忠实执行"。

Method: 构建WordEval操作保真度基准：642个案例覆盖15个Word功能领域（从文本、列表到图表、形状几何、SmartArt）；546个原子案例覆盖231条命令，96个组合案例在单文档中叠加多个编辑；由编辑单元规格生成受控输入文档、参考文档与对齐的oracle检查；Word原生oracle校验任务特定对象属性及保持性（不破坏其他内容）条件；每个组合案例链接对应原子案例。共评测8个agent系统。

Result: 多数系统在Atomic赛道表现不佳，且性能随功能领域明显波动；所有系统在Compositional上得分均低于Atomic，且差距因系统差异巨大；精确的原子意图链接与记录的失败诊断提供了互补的变异视角。

Conclusion: 将评估重心从"任务是否完成"转向"编辑操作是否在对象属性层面被忠实实现"，并以原子—组合关联支撑细粒度诊断，可更准确揭示文档agent在Word编辑上的真实能力与短板。

Abstract: Microsoft Word is a central environment for office automation, and the faithful execution of its editing operations is an important capability for document agents. Unlike plain-text editing, editing in Word modifies a structured object model, where the requested effect is realized in object properties. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate office workflows or desktop automation, treating word-processor documents as one task domain among many and verifying outcomes with keyword matching, per-task property checks, or screenshot comparison. We introduce WordEval, an operation-fidelity benchmark that instead centers evaluation on Word editing operations, testing whether each requested edit is realized as intended changes to Word objects and properties. It contains 642 cases across 15 Word feature areas, ranging from text and lists to charts, shape geometry, and SmartArt. 546 atomic cases cover 231 commands, 96 compositional cases combine multiple edits in a single document, and a Word-native oracle checks task-specific object properties and preservation conditions. Cases are constructed from editing-unit specifications, yielding controlled input documents, reference documents, and aligned oracle checks. Each compositional case is linked to corresponding atomic cases, supporting fine-grained diagnosis of editing failures. We evaluate eight agent systems and find that many struggle on the Atomic track, with performance varying across feature areas. Moreover, every system scores lower on Compositional than on Atomic, with gaps that vary substantially by system. Exact atomic-intent links and recorded failure diagnoses provide complementary views of this variation.

</details>


### [67] [They Can See It but Not Say It: Iterative Self Knowledge Re-expression in Visual Reasoning Relative Pose Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10483)
*Mengyu Wang,Ken Deng,Yftah Ziser,Shay B. Cohen,Tiejun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉推理相对位姿识别（VRRPI）任务，本文揭示视觉语言模型存在稳定的输出偏置，并提出免标注的 SKR-ID 流水线，通过冷启动去偏提示与递归 logit 排序去偏，将开源 VLM 的 Macro-F1 从 35.0–45.4% 提升至 53.7–58.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRRPI 任务暴露了现代视觉语言模型在多视图 3D 空间推理上的严重不足。作者系统分析发现，VLM 在 VRRPI 中存在稳定的输出偏置：无论视觉证据如何，都倾向于预测某些特定运动模式，说明模型依赖先验而非视觉证据，基线性能严重塌陷。因此动机是定位并缓解这类系统性偏置。

Method: 提出 self knowledge re-expression with iterative debiasing (SKR-ID)，一种无需任何标注数据的自适应流水线，仅使用随机抽取的无标注图像对。核心是将 VLM 的输出机制从通用的下一词预测（next-token prediction）转为任务优化的分类头。流程分两阶段：(1) 冷启动初始化，使用以物体为中心的去偏提示（object-centric prompts），将运动推断锚定在 2D 视觉证据上；(2) 递归 logit 排序机制，以 logit 中位值作为动态阈值，强制实现统计均等（statistical parity）以迭代去偏。

Result: 在标准基准上对前沿 VLM 的实验评估显示，SKR-ID 显著缓解了系统性偏置：开源 VLM 的 Macro-F1 从 35.0–45.4% 提升至 53.7–58.0%，且全程未使用任何标注数据。结果表明该方法能从严重塌陷的基线出发有效提升 3D 推理性能。

Conclusion: VLM 在 VRRPI 中的失败主要源于稳定的输出偏置而非单纯的感知能力缺失；SKR-ID 证明通过输出机制重构与统计层面的迭代去偏，可在零标注条件下显著改善多视图 3D 空间推理。然而该方法聚焦于偏置矫正，真实空间理解能力的上限仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: The task of visual reasoning relative pose identification (VRRPI) has exposed critical weaknesses in the multi-view 3D spatial reasoning capabilities of modern vision-language models (VLMs). We present a systematic analysis of VLMs' behavior in VRRPI, uncovering stable output biases where VLMs disproportionately predict specific motion patterns regardless of visual evidence. To mitigate these inherent biases, we propose self knowledge re-expression with iterative debiasing (SKR-ID), an annotation-free pipeline that adapts VLMs for VRRPI using only randomly extracted unannotated image pairs. SKR-ID transitions VLMs' output mechanism from generic next-token prediction to task-optimized classification heads. The pipeline proceeds in two stages: (1) a cold-start initialization using debiasing object-centric prompts to anchor motion inference in 2D visual evidence; and (2) a recursive logit-ranking mechanism that enforces statistical parity by using the median logit value as a dynamic threshold. Experimental evaluation across frontier VLMs on standard benchmarks demonstrates that SKR-ID significantly mitigates systematic biases.
