Our method improves open-source VLMs' Macro-F1 scores from 35.0-45.4% to 53.7-58.0%, without using any annotated data. These results show that SKR-ID can effectively improve 3D reasoning performance from a severely collapsed baseline. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/astudentuser/Codes_of_SKR-ID.

</details>


### [68] [Can General-Purpose Generative AI Supply Training Data for Medical Vision Models? A Multimodal Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10484)
*Jiachuan Peng,Kyle Lam,Jierui Qu,Lin Li,Haining Zheng,Bang Zheng,Aniruddha Agarwal,Zaki Almallah,Min Chen,Vicente Grau,Hagai Rossman,Eran Segal,Jianing Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估通用图像生成与编辑模型 Nano Banana 生成的合成医学图像作为训练、亚组增强与模型审计数据源的可行性，覆盖四种影像模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的多样化、精细标注医学影像数据集仍严重不足，是视觉医学 AI 的主要瓶颈；需验证通用生成模型能否提供可用的合成数据。

Method: 回顾性研究，涵盖四种影像模态。胸片、眼底照相与 CT 模型仅用合成图像训练，并在真实数据上外部验证，与真实数据训练的模型对比；颈动脉超声模型基于 Human Phenotype Project 私有队列（40-75 岁），分别在有无合成数据增强下训练。由临床医生对图像真实性打分，评估指标包括 AUROC、AUPR、校准与决策曲线分析。

Result: 纯合成数据模型在肺炎（n=14863）、视网膜脱离（n=1149）、肾结石（n=4122）上的 AUROC 分别为 0.877（95% CI 0.843-0.910）、0.963（0.948-0.975）、0.711（0.600-0.823），与真实数据模型相当，但校准与净获益并不始终更优。合成-真实混合训练的颈动脉超声模型输出序数血管年龄指数，将 633 名外部受试者分层为 BMI 与血糖存在差异的亚组（均 p<0.01）。在眼底图中编辑加入中度网格状图案后，中心性浆液性脉络膜视网膜病变的真实类概率由 0.996 降至 0.822，可用于检验捷径学习。经临床医生指导的编辑使低质量 CT 图像真实性评分由 3.10/5 提升至 4.30/5。

Conclusion: 合成数据可在模型训练、亚组代表性与受控编辑驱动的模型测试中补充真实数据，但其临床价值应通过校准、决策曲线分析与临床医生评审综合判断，而非仅看判别性能。

Abstract: Background: Well-annotated, diverse medical imaging datasets remain a major constraint for vision-based medical AI. We evaluated Nano Banana, a general-purpose image generation and editing model, as a source of synthetic medical images for model training, subgroup augmentation, and model auditing. Methods: In this retrospective study across four imaging modalities, chest radiography, fundus photography, and CT models were trained only on synthetic images and externally validated on real-world data against real-data-trained models. Carotid ultrasound models were trained on a private Human Phenotype Project cohort (aged 40-75 years) with and without synthetic augmentation. Clinicians rated image plausibility. Evaluation used AUROC, AUPR, calibration, and decision curve analysis. Findings: Synthetic-only models achieved AUROCs of 0.877 (95% CI 0.843-0.910) for pneumonia (n=14863), 0.963 (0.948-0.975) for retinal detachment (n=1149), and 0.711 (0.600-0.823) for renal calculus (n=4122), comparable to real-data-trained models, but their calibration and net benefit were not consistently more favorable. With mixed synthetic-real training, a carotid ultrasound model produced an ordinal vascular age index that stratified 633 external subjects into subgroups differing in BMI and glucose (both p<0.01), linking vascular aging to cardiometabolic health. To stress-test shortcut learning, editing a moderate tessellated pattern into fundus photographs reduced the true-class probability for central serous chorioretinopathy from 0.996 to 0.822. Clinician-guided editing raised low-quality CT plausibility from 3.10/5 to 4.30/5. Interpretation: Synthetic data can complement real data in model training, subgroup representation, and model testing via controlled editing, but its clinical value should be assessed with calibration, decision curve analysis, and clinician review, not discrimination alone.

</details>


### [69] [Decoupling Direction and Magnitude: Language-Steered Flow Matching for Super-Resolution in the Dark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10485)
*Jiaxin Gao,Ziyu Yue,Yaohua Liu,Danchen Cui,Zhihui Zhao,Zhixun Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeLang-SR，一个一步流匹配框架，将"退化语言"作为语义先验引导速度场方向，并用连续空间更新强度场作为局部欧拉步长控制幅度，实现暗光图像超分辨率的空间自适应增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光超分存在极端空间异质性：严重欠曝与噪声区需强生成式增强以重建细节，而较好曝光区需保守更新以避免幻觉纹理。标准扩散/流匹配模型采用全局均匀积分轨迹，难以适配这种空间变化的退化。

Method: 显式解耦"修复方向"与"修复幅度"。(1)方向：将可测量的物理统计量转译为结构化"退化语言"，借助预训练文本到图像模型的语义先验，与图像特定视觉token共同引导速度场趋向修复相关流形；(2)幅度：引入连续空间更新强度场，作为局部变化的欧拉步长场，在重退化阴影区施加更强积分步，在可靠区域施加保守更新。整体为一步流匹配框架。

Result: 在配对暗光图像与真实暗光图像上的实验、消融实验及诊断分析表明，DeLang-SR能够以有效且可解释的一步方式实现暗光超分辨率。代码与预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将退化信息语义化并解耦方向与幅度，DeLang-SR克服了全局均匀积分的局限，为暗光超分提供了一种高效、可解释的单步解决方案。

Abstract: Image super-resolution in the dark is fundamentally challenged by extreme spatial heterogeneity: severely underexposed and noisy regions demand aggressive generative enhancement to reconstruct missing details, while relatively well-exposed areas require conservative updates to prevent hallucinated textures. Standard diffusion and flow-matching models, however, rely on globally uniform integration trajectories, making them sub-optimal for such spatially varying degradations. In this paper, we propose DeLang-SR (Degradation-Language steered Super-Resolution), a one-step flow-matching framework that explicitly decouples the restoration direction and magnitude. Rather than treating low-light corruption as arbitrary latent features, DeLang-SR translates measurable physical statistics into structured degradation language, harnessing the semantic prior of pretrained text-to-image models. The degradation-language intent, complemented by image-specific visual tokens, steers the velocity field (direction) toward restoration-relevant manifolds. Simultaneously, a continuous spatial update-strength field acts as a locally varying Euler step-size field (magnitude), applying stronger integration steps in heavily degraded shadows while enforcing conservative updates in reliable regions. Experiments on paired and real dark images, together with ablations and diagnostic analyses, show that DeLang-SR provides an effective and interpretable one-step route to super-resolution in the dark. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/moriyaya/DeLang-SR.

</details>


### [70] [Disentangling Channel Semantics in Vision Transformers via Token Decorrelation and Composition-Aware Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10497)
*Daeun Kim,Hyesong Choi,Hyejin Park,Dongbo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuCa-ViT，通过Factorial Token Learning (FTL) 强制上下文token去相关、Channel Composition-Aware Modulation (CAM) 以FTL精炼的[CLS]为语义锚生成样本级调制信号，解决多通道ViT中上下文token向主导通道坍缩的问题，在JUMP-CP、CHAMMI、So2Sat 上均超越已有方法且提升幅度与通道冗余度正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道图像需同时建模全局共享空间结构与通道特异性变化。已有方法用memory token增强[CLS]，但在高通道冗余下上下文token会坍缩到少数主导通道，产生冗余表示与有偏的全局摘要，难以支撑composition-aware的通道解释。

Method: 两个耦合机制：(1) FTL，在上下文token间施加token级去相关约束，使[CLS]聚焦全局共享语义、memory token保留互补的通道特异线索；(2) CAM，以FTL精炼后的[CLS]作为语义锚，生成逐样本的调制信号，在统一backbone内实现随通道组合变化的通道解释，替代静态通道嵌入。

Result: 在JUMP-CP、CHAMMI、So2Sat三个基准上均优于现有多通道ViT；性能提升幅度与各数据集的通道间冗余度（ρ∈[0.19, 0.77]）一致，验证了驱动该设计的诊断分析。

Conclusion: token坍缩是高冗余多通道场景下的关键瓶颈，token级去相关与动态锚定调制是有效解法，且方法收益与冗余程度呈系统性关联。

Abstract: Multi-channel images encode heterogeneous channel semantics aligned over a shared spatial structure, which requires models to jointly capture globally shared structure and channel-specific variations. Recent multi-channel Vision Transformers attempt to address this challenge by augmenting the [CLS] token with memory tokens to organize multi-channel representations under varying channel compositions. However, we find that these context tokens collapse onto a few dominant channels under high inter-channel redundancy, producing redundant representations and a biased global summary that fails to reliably support composition-aware channel interpretation. We propose MuCa-ViT (Multi-Channel Composition-Aware Vision Transformer), a unified framework that addresses these limitations through two coupled mechanisms. Factorial Token Learning (FTL) enforces token-level decorrelation among context tokens, encouraging the [CLS] token to capture globally shared semantics while memory tokens preserve complementary channel-specific cues. Channel Composition-Aware Modulation (CAM) uses the FTL-refined [CLS] as a semantic anchor to generate sample-wise modulation signals, which enable composition-aware interpretation of channels within a unified backbone rather than relying on static channel embeddings. Experiments on JUMP-CP, CHAMMI, and So2Sat show that MuCa-ViT outperforms prior multi-channel ViTs across all three benchmarks, and the magnitude of improvement aligns with the inter-channel redundancy of each dataset ($ρ\in [0.19, 0.77]$), validating the diagnostic analysis that motivates our design. Our code is available at https://github.com/kimdanni/MuCa-ViT .

</details>


### [71] [SRA: Spatial Reasoning Adapter via Evolving Social Interaction Graphs for Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10506)
*Jaewoo Jeong,Seonkyu Song,Hyeonwoo Park,Jegyeong Cho,Yujin Bae,Giwon Lee,Daehee Park,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Reasoning Adapter(SRA)，一种可插入现有扩散/流匹配预测器的模块化图去噪器，在每个反向步骤基于宿主当前的联合未来预测重建稀疏欧氏交互图并输出残差修正，在三种架构、四个数据集上一致提升ADE/FDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机多智能体轨迹预测器只在观测历史中读取一次交互信息，并在整个生成式采样过程中保持不变；随着联合未来逐渐清晰而涌现的互惠几何约束未被重新审视。

Method: SRA作为模块化图去噪器嵌入宿主（LED、MID、MoFlow）的扩散/流匹配生成核心，不改变其结构。每个反向步骤读取宿主的当前未来估计，在预测轨迹上构建稀疏欧氏交互图，对宿主解码路径施加残差修正。含三个组件：(1)由学习到的query-key打分加几何偏置检索的稀疏邻域；(2)在演化预测上重算的未来条件关系特征；(3)不确定性加权消息传递，让高置信智能体作为低置信智能体的锚点；三者耦合为单一机制。

Result: 在SDD、NBA、Soccer、Football四个基准上，对三种结构不同的宿主均提升ADE与FDE，平均增益ADE 5.8%、FDE 7.0%；累积消融显示三组件相互依赖，仅在组合时才能在所有宿主与所有体育数据集上同时提升。

Conclusion: 在模型自身演化的未来预测上做空间推理、并以该未来的可信度加权，是一种跨去噪架构普遍有效的通用增强方式，可即插即用而无需重设计生成核心。

Abstract: Predicting where multiple agents will move next is fundamental to autonomous driving, robotics, and any system that must share space with people safely. Modern stochastic predictors read off inter-agent interactions once from the observed past and keep that view fixed throughout the generative rollout, so the reciprocal geometric constraints that emerge as the joint future sharpens are not re-examined. We address this gap with SRA (Spatial Reasoning Adapter), a modular graph denoiser that is integrated into existing diffusion and flow-matching predictors without redesigning their generative cores. At every reverse step, SRA reads the host's current future estimate, builds a sparse Euclidean interaction graph on the predicted trajectories, and returns a residual correction to the host's decoding path. Our adapter consists of three core components: a sparse neighborhood retrieved with a learned query-key score and a geometric bias, a future-conditioned relational feature recomputed on the evolving prediction, and uncertainty-weighted message passing where confident agents act as anchors for uncertain ones. We instantiate SRA on three structurally different hosts (LED, MID, and MoFlow) across four multi-agent benchmarks: SDD, NBA, Soccer, and Football. SRA improves ADE for every host on every dataset, with average gains of 5.8% in ADE and 7.0% in FDE. A cumulative ablation shows that the three components act as one mechanism: only together do they improve every host on every sports dataset, indicating that spatial reasoning over the model's own evolving future, weighted by how far that future can be trusted, is broadly useful across denoising architectures. Code is available at https://github.com/Jaewoo97/SRA.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Optimization-Oriented Hypernetworks for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10509)
*Lulu Cai,Hanxiang Ren,Siyan Dong,Li Sun,Jiefeng Wu,Youyi Zheng,Yi Ma,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超网络，单次前向即可生成隐式神经表示（INR）的全部参数，无需测试时优化；核心是以注意力替代MLP来实现保持链式法则结构的超网络，从而消除随目标网络宽度四次方增长的参数开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INR超网络要么忽略产生INR的测试时优化所蕴含的链式法则结构，要么在保留该结构时用MLP实例化，导致参数量随目标网络宽度四次方增长（quartic in width）。作者指出这一代价源于MLP实例化本身的结构性缺陷，而非"保持链式法则结构"这一优化导向原则，因此需要新的实例化方式。

Method: 以注意力机制替换MLP主体，组合三项设计：（1）以输出神经元为中心的tokenization，使token轴与每层输出神经元轴对齐；（2）信息流一致性规则，依据每个交叉注意力模块输出应占据的表示来固定其角色；（3）token空间的迭代rollout，将目标INR单步优化所计算的五个量实现为五个交叉注意力模块，参数量与目标网络宽度无关。

Result: 在五个2D与3D INR生成基准上优于最先进的单次前向与优化导向基线；可扩展到MLP式优化导向范式耗尽GPU显存的目标网络宽度；在无真值INR权重监督的NeRF生成任务上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 参数四次方增长是MLP实例化的结构性代价而非优化导向原则的必然结果；用注意力实例化链式法则结构可在保持单次前向生成的同时实现宽度可扩展性，并展现跨任务（含NeRF生成）的强泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a hypernetwork that generates the parameters of an implicit neural representation (INR) for a given signal in a single forward pass, without test-time optimization. Existing hypernetworks for this problem either ignore the chain-rule structure of the test-time optimization that produces an INR or, when they preserve it, instantiate the structure with MLPs whose parameter count grows quartically in target-network width. Our central contribution is to show that this parameter-count cost is structural to the MLP instantiation, not to the optimization-oriented principle of preserving chain-rule structure, and to resolve it by replacing the MLP body with attention. The resulting hypernetwork combines three design choices: an output-neuron-centric tokenization that aligns the token axis with each layer's output-neuron axis; an information-flow consistency rule that fixes the role of every cross-attention module from the representation its output should occupy; and a token-space iterative rollout that realizes the five quantities one optimization step on the target INR computes as five cross-attention modules with parameter count independent of target-network width. Across five 2D and 3D INR generation benchmarks, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art single-pass and optimization-oriented baselines; scales to target-network widths at which the MLP-based optimization-oriented paradigm exhausts GPU memory; and produces the highest result on NeRF generation without ground-truth INR weights supervision.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Row Alignment: Virtual-Camera-Aware Online Stereo Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10518)
*Yizhao Peng,Rui Gong,Zaiwang Gu,Xudong Jiang,Jun Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VirtualRect 在线立体矫正方法，不仅对齐行，还通过估计虚拟立体相机几何并引入整图形变监督，保证矫正图像与相机参数一致，从而提升下游立体匹配与深度估计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中相机标定后的物理位移（机械漂移、振动、安装变化等）会破坏标定/矫正假设，导致深度估计退化；现有在线矫正方法只优化行对齐，虽必要但不充分，可能造成图像区域被裁切、大片空白、物体拉伸/挤压或尺度不均，进而损害立体匹配。

Method: 提出 VirtualRect：将在线矫正视为估计一个矫正后的"虚拟立体相机"，而非仅使同一场景点的左右视图落到同一行；同时结合对整幅图像形变行为的监督（image-wide supervision of image warp），使矫正后图像与相机参数来源于同一几何，保证匹配所用图像与深度计算所用模型一致。

Result: 相比先前 SOTA 在线矫正器：垂直光流误差在 Carla-Flowguided 上降低 17%，在 Semi-Truck Highway 上降低 7%；在 Carla-Flowguided 上，对三种下游立体匹配器的深度 MAE 降低 30–41%。

Conclusion: 行对齐只是在线立体矫正的必要条件而非充分条件；通过虚拟相机几何建模与整图形变监督，VirtualRect 在保持几何一致性的同时显著改善下游立体匹配与深度估计，更适用于真实部署。

Abstract: Stereo matchers estimate depth by comparing the left and right images from a stereo camera pair, but they assume that the two cameras are accurately calibrated and rectified. In real deployments, small physical shifts after calibration, caused by camera motion, mechanical drift, vibration, or installation changes, can break this assumption and degrade depth estimation. Online stereo rectification is therefore needed to correct the image pair during operation and recover a stereo geometry where standard stereo matching can work reliably. Recent online rectification methods mainly optimize row alignment, making the left and right views of the same scene point fall on the same image row. We show that this target, while necessary, is insufficient for deployable stereo depth estimation. A rectifier may make a small set of matched points look well row-aligned, but still distort the full images in ways that hurt stereo matching: useful image regions may be cut off, large blank areas may appear, objects may be stretched or squeezed, or image scale may change unevenly across the view. We propose VirtualRect, an online stereo rectification method designed for reliable real-world deployment. Rather than treating rectification as simply making the left and right image rows line up, this method estimates a rectified virtual stereo camera and combines image-wide supervision of image warp behavior. This makes the rectified images and the camera parameters come from the same geometry, so the images used for matching and the model used for depth computation remain consistent. Experiments on multiple datasets show that, compared with the prior state-of-the-art online rectifier, VirtualRect reduces vertical flow error by 17% on Carla-Flowguided and 7% on Semi-Truck Highway, and lowers depth MAE by 30-41% on Carla-Flowguided across three downstream stereo matchers.

</details>


### [74] [Action Transfer Across Character Body Plans in Latent-Action World Models: An Empirical Study of Animated Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10523)
*Amarnath R*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究跨具身（不同身体结构）2D角色的潜在动作迁移，提出剔除"屏幕运动混淆"的迁移评分，并按"比较正确动作与同一来源错误动作"的度量设定，在自建合成基准、Sprites及DeformingThings4D上评估五种训练目标。结果显示：固定相机下"仅复制源屏幕运动"的基线即可得0.71，与多数学习模型相当；跟随相机下最优模型对未见动物科仅0.46、对人类仅0.20，但线性探针识别动作类别可达66%–86%平衡准确率（随机17%），说明动作信息被编码但正确动作只弥补了部分预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动作世界模型可从无标签视频中学动作并跨具身共享，但不同身体结构的2D角色间迁移研究不足。关键在于：猫、团块、人都会"跳"，但各自身体运动方式不同。传统迁移指标把身体运动与屏幕运动混在一起，甚至会给"完全忽略动作、只复制源画面位移"的模型打满分，导致评测失效。

Method: 提出新的迁移评分：将正确动作与来自同一源角色的错误动作进行对比，从而剥离屏幕运动捷径。在三个测试集上比较五种训练目标：(1) 新构建的合成基准，含六种体型规划的2D卡通角色；(2) 标准Sprites基准；(3) 从DeformingThings4D中精选的431段动物与人类动画。并设置固定相机与跟随相机两种条件，另用线性探针检验潜在动作的可解码性。

Result: 固定相机下，仅复制源屏幕运动的基线得分0.71，与大多数学习模型相当，印证指标此前受混淆污染。跟随相机下，最佳模型对未见动物科仅0.46、对人类仅0.20。线性探针在未见动物科上的动作分类平衡准确率为66%–86%（随机水平17%）。

Conclusion: 潜在动作确实保留"执行何种动作"的语义信息，但正确动作对陌生身体只弥补部分预测误差，跨具身动作迁移仍远未解决；评测必须消除屏幕运动捷径，否则会高估模型能力。

Abstract: Latent-action world models learn actions from video without labels, and recent methods aim to share these actions across bodies. Transfer between 2D characters with different body structures remains underexplored. In this paper, we study whether an action learned from one character can make a character with a different body perform the same action. A cat, a blob and a human can all jump, but each moves its body in its own way. The usual transfer gain mixes body motion with screen movement and can assign a perfect score to a model that ignores its action. To address this, we introduce a transfer score that compares the correct action with wrong ones from the same source. We test five training objectives on a new synthetic benchmark of 2D cartoon characters with six body plans, the standard Sprites benchmark, and 431 selected animal and human animations from DeformingThings4D. With a fixed camera, a baseline that only copies the source's screen movement scores 0.71, as high as most learned models. In contrast, with a camera that follows the body, the best model reaches only 0.46 for an unseen animal family and 0.20 for humans. With this camera, a linear probe identifies the action class of unseen animal families with 66% to 86% balanced accuracy (chance 17%). The learned actions therefore retain information about which action is performed, but the correct action closes only part of the prediction error gap for a new character.

</details>


### [75] [PrivMark: Pixel-Space Steering against Privacy Leakage in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10528)
*Ruofan Xing,Arman Akbari,Weikai Lin,Yuhao Zhu,Xuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrivMark：在图像层面将两个弱对齐的引导向量方向编码为单一有界像素扰动，抑制VLM对年龄、性别、种族等软生物特征的泄露，无需运行时访问模型内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在文本输出中常泄露年龄、性别、种族等软生物特征，使上传图像的人口统计信息暴露给任意下游API用户。现有防御不适配该场景：人脸去标识化保留分布式软生物线索；面向分类器的对抗扰动无法迁移到开放式生成；更强的VLM专用方法需重训练或运行时激活访问，下游API不可得。

Method: 分析VLM激活，在隐状态空间中识别两个弱对齐的引导向量方向：一个对应开放式描述下的偶发属性披露，另一个对应直接生物特征探测时的顺从而非拒答；将两方向编码为单个有界像素扰动，使受保护图像本身即可实现隐私保护。同时构建BioleakBench：1,600个VQA对，覆盖自由描述与直接探测两种协议下的生物隐私属性。

Result: 在三个白盒源模型上，生物特征抑制率最高达85.9%，同时保留75%的干净OK-VQA效用，隐私-效用权衡至多达最强纯像素基线的1.4倍；同一一次性扰动可一致迁移至七个未见过的VLM（含闭源API）。

Conclusion: 图像级、单次生成的有界像素扰动可有效抑制VLM软生物特征泄露，并具备跨模型与跨API的迁移性，为下游用户场景提供实用隐私防御。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) routinely disclose soft-biometric attributes such as age, gender, and race in their text outputs, exposing demographic information from uploaded images to any downstream API user. Existing privacy defenses do not fit this setting: face de-identification leaves distributed soft-biometric cues intact, classifier-targeted adversarial perturbations do not transfer to open-ended generation, and stronger VLM-specific methods require retraining or runtime activation access unavailable to downstream APIs. We propose PrivMark, a framework that suppresses biometric leakage at the image level. By analyzing VLM activations, we identify two weakly aligned steering vector directions in hidden-state space, each tied to a distinct leakage behavior --- one corresponding to incidental attribute disclosure under open-ended descriptions, the other to compliance with direct biometric probes instead of refusal --- and encode both directions into a single bounded pixel perturbation, so the protected image alone enforces privacy with no invasive access to model internals at deployment. To support evaluation, we assemble BioleakBench, $1{,}600$ VQA pairs covering biometric privacy attributes under both free-form description and direct-probe protocols. Across three white-box source models, PrivMark achieves up to $85.9\%$ biometric suppression while retaining $75\%$ of clean OK-VQA utility, up to $1.4\times$ the privacy-utility tradeoff of the strongest pixel-only baseline. The same one-shot perturbations transfer consistently to seven unseen VLMs including closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [76] [CompositionEvidence: Controlling Geometric Evidence for Cinematic Composition Reasoning with Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10559)
*Yichen He,Xin Huang,Yuxi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CompositionEvidence：从冻结的检测、深度、线条专家中提取九个可复现的几何特征并统一为24维表示，通过免训练门控文本通道与轻量软提示通道注入 VLM，在 ShotBench 构图基准上分别提升 6.7 与 11.5 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影构图问题依赖帧内几何信息，而 VLM 对此类几何的利用并不稳定；外部感知工具虽能显式提供几何，但朴素提示注入反而会覆盖模型原有的有效视觉预测（49.5%→46.3%，几何受污染时降至33.6%）。因此需要研究"如何把外部几何证据可靠地交给模型"。

Method: 基于冻结的检测、深度、线条专家抽取9个可复现构图特征，构成共享的24维表示，并以两种接口注入：(1) 免训练通道——在问题之后追加带门控的中性证据文本；(2) 最小训练通道——用7.37M参数的投影器将该表示映射为4个软 token，VLM 主干保持冻结。

Result: ShotBench 上 Qwen2.5-VL-7B：免训练通道达56.2%（+6.7，McNemar 精确检验 p=0.0018），证据受污染时仍有48.4%；软 token 通道达61.0%（+11.5）。在9个 VLM（含5个通过官方 API 访问的商业模型）上，零样本准确率区间为43.8%–63.0%，文本通道带来的变化范围为 +6.7 至 −6.9 点，且基线最高者反而出现退化。

Conclusion: 显式几何证据的增益并非普适：其效果取决于测量可靠性（噪声/损坏会抵消收益）以及接收模型对证据的整合方式（基线最强的模型可能被干扰而退化）。外部工具应当以规范化的共享表示、配合门控或可学习接口注入，而非直接拼接原始提示。

Abstract: Cinematic composition questions depend on in-frame geometry that vision-language models (VLMs) use inconsistently. External perception tools can expose this geometry, but naive prompt injection can overwrite useful visual predictions. On ShotBench composition, pre-question geometry reduces Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 49.5% to 46.3%; corrupted measurements lower it to 33.6%. CompositionEvidence derives nine reproducible features from frozen detection, depth, and line experts and supplies their shared 24-dimensional representation through two interfaces. A training-free channel appends gated neutral evidence after the question, reaching 56.2% (+6.7, exact McNemar p=0.0018) and 48.4% under corrupted evidence. A minimal-training channel maps the same representation to four soft tokens with a 7.37M-parameter projector while leaving the VLM frozen; it reaches 61.0% (+11.5). Across nine VLMs, including five commercial models queried through official provider APIs, zero-shot accuracy ranges from 43.8% to 63.0%, and the text channel changes it by +6.7 to -6.9 points; the highest baseline is among the regressions. The effect of explicit geometry depends on measurement reliability and on how the receiving model integrates it.

</details>


### [77] [ZARA: Zero-shot Animatable and Relightable 3D Avatar reconstruction from a single image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10562)
*Yixiao Chen,Dongdong Weng,Chengfeng Zhao,Tao Gong,Zhihe Zhao,Zhe Zhu,Yue Liu,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZARA：零样本前馈框架，从单张图像同时重建可动画、可重光照的3D高斯化身及其内禀材质属性，无需逐人优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模人体重建模型外观与光照纠缠、不可重光照；而现有可重光照化身方法通常需逐人优化，成本高、泛化差。

Method: 将预训练大规模人体重建模型与Material Transformer耦合，继承几何先验并学习材质感知高斯表示；提出Gaussian-aligned material feature fusion，将多尺度材质特征注入每个高斯基元并与几何token融合；以伪材质标签作为条件输入与监督目标；引入MatFace正则化，在面部伪材质监督不可靠时保持反照率预测的面部结构与颜色。

Result: 数十秒内完成可动画、可重光照人体重建，无需逐人优化或后处理；在重光照质量与材质泛化能力上优于现有基线。

Conclusion: ZARA实现了单图零样本可重光照化身重建，恢复PBR属性并支持化身的直接材质编辑。

Abstract: Recent large human reconstruction models create remarkable animatable 3D avatars from a single image, but their appearance remains entangled with the illumination and cannot be relighted. In contrast, existing 3D relightable-avatar reconstruction methods typically require person-specific optimization. We present ZARA, a zero-shot feed-forward framework that reconstructs an animatable 3D Gaussian avatar together with its intrinsic attributes from a single image. ZARA couples a pretrained large-scale human reconstruction model with a Material Transformer to inherit geometry priors while learning material-aware Gaussian representations. To preserve localized details, we introduce Gaussian-aligned material feature fusion strategy, which injects multi-scale material features onto individual Gaussians and combines them with geometry tokens. As most human-avatar datasets lack ground-truth material maps, we use pseudo-material labels as conditioning inputs and supervision targets during training. We further introduce MatFace regularization to preserve facial structure and color in albedo prediction under unreliable facial pseudo-material supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZARA reconstructs animatable and relightable humans within tens of seconds without person-specific optimization or post-processing, outperforming existing baselines in both relighting quality and materials generalization ability. As the ability of recovering the physically-based-rendering attributes of the human, ZARA also enables direct material editing of the avatar's appearance.

</details>


### [78] [Understanding the Effect of Visual Attention in CNNs: A Decision-Space Alignment Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10571)
*Niladri B. Puhan,K. Kamala Priya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出决策空间框架，将乘性注意力建模为中间特征扰动，并用一阶近似预测其对目标-竞争类分类间隔的影响；引入决策对齐分数（DAS）量化扰动与局部间隔梯度的方向一致性。在CIFAR-100上用SimAM和CBAM在五种CNN骨干网络上验证，结果显示预测与实际间隔变化在多数末层注意力配置下吻合良好，DAS与实际间隔变化正相关，且相关性随注意力插入位置后移而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉注意力通过调制中间特征增强CNN，但其对下游决策的影响机制尚不清楚，缺少从决策层面刻画注意力作用的理论与量化工具。

Method: 将乘性注意力视为中间特征扰动，基于一阶展开推导其对目标类与竞争类之间分类间隔的影响预测；定义决策对齐分数（DAS）度量扰动方向与局部间隔梯度的对齐程度；在CIFAR-100上以SimAM、CBAM两种注意力模块，跨ResNet-18/20/50/56与MobileNetV2五种骨干，分析预测一致性、DAS与间隔变化的相关性以及下游激活状态变化，并进行单注意力位置消融。

Result: 多数末层注意力配置下预测间隔变化与实际间隔变化一致性良好；DAS与实际间隔变化呈正相关；单注意力实验中预测一致性与DAS相关性依赖注意力插入位置，越靠后的阶段关联越强。

Conclusion: 注意力对决策的影响可由特征扰动的一阶决策空间分析有效近似，DAS可作为刻画注意力决策效应的可解释量化指标，且注意力位置显著影响其决策效应强度。

Abstract: Visual Attention enhances convolutional neural networks (CNNs) by modulating intermediate features, yet their effects on downstream decisions remain less understood. We present a decision-space framework that models multiplicative attention as an intermediate feature perturbation and derives a first-order prediction of its effect on the target-versus-competitor classification margin. We further introduce the Decision Alignment Score (DAS) to quantify the directional alignment between the perturbation and the local margin gradient. We evaluate the framework on CIFAR-100 using SimAM and CBAM across five popular CNN backbones, ResNet-18, ResNet-20, ResNet-50, ResNet-56, and MobileNetV2. The analysis examines prediction agreement, DAS margin correlations, and downstream activation-state changes. Across most final-attention configurations, predicted and actual margin changes show good agreement, while DAS exhibits positive correlations with actual margin changes. Single-attention experiments further show that prediction agreement and DAS margin correlations depend on attention placement, with stronger associations at later stages.

</details>


### [79] [ROLECUE: Diagnosing Cross-Lingual Role-Binding Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10575)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Evan Maksud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROLEUCE诊断基准，用192张双人动作图搭配德语、土耳其语、日语、英语、韩语、孟加拉语的规范语序与受事前置句子，分离测试VLMs的图像角色识别、文本角色解析、图-句联合匹配与顺从倾向，发现多语言"组件-联合"性能落差与"接受偏差"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs能识别图像事件与解析句子，但尚不清楚它们何时能把视觉的Agent-Patient结构绑定到语言语义角色上，尤其是在依赖形态、语序或构式来标示角色的多语言场景中；聚合式基准掩盖了组件能力与联合推理之间的差异。

Method: 构建受控诊断集：192张经校验的双人动作图像，配以六种语言（德语、土耳其语、日语、英语、韩语、孟加拉语）的规范语序与受事前置语序句子；评测分为四类任务——纯图像角色识别、纯文本角色解析、图文直接匹配、回答顺从行为，并额外做外部模块化组合（用模型自身组件预测拼装）；在八个近期开源权重VLM上评估，配合bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 呈现结构化谱系：部分模型在视觉或语言组件测试上失败，部分基本解决该诊断，部分塌缩为恒定的语义肯定（接受偏差），若干模型出现组件-联合落差——可靠的纯图像与纯文本角色预测在直接联合推理中不复存在；最大非退化落差达44.9个百分点，bootstrap区间确认并非点估计假象。

Conclusion: 多语言VLM存在被聚合基准掩盖的组件-联合性能落差与接受偏差；在解读联合多模态错误前，必须先做组件级诊断。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can recognize events in images and parse sentences, but it remains unclear when they bind visual Agent-Patient structure to linguistic semantic roles across languages whose role cues depend on morphology, word order or construction. We introduce ROLECUE, a controlled diagnostic that pairs 192 validated two-person action images with German, Turkish, Japanese, English, Korean and Bangla sentences in canonical and patient-first orders. The evaluation separates image-only role recognition, text-only role parsing, direct image-sentence matching, response-compliance behavior and an external modular composition of the model's own component predictions. Across eight recent open-weight VLMs, ROLECUE reveals a structured spectrum: some models fail visual or linguistic component tests, some largely solve the diagnostic, some collapse to constant semantic assent and several show component-joint gaps in which reliable image-only and text-only role predictions do not survive direct joint inference. The largest non-degenerate gaps reach 44.9 percentage points, with final bootstrap intervals confirming that the effect is not a point-estimate artifact. These results reveal multilingual component-joint performance gaps and acceptance biases hidden by aggregate VLM benchmarks and show why component diagnostics are necessary before interpreting joint multimodal errors.

</details>


### [80] [MotionGraphicsBench: Evaluating Motion Graphics Generation with Programmatic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10592)
*Jiaju Ma,Ashvini Kumar Jindal,Chuhua Wang,Sumithra Bhakthavatsalam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MotionGraphicsBench：含100个专家标注实例的"静态SVG+运动编辑提示"基准，用确定性可读验证程序替代参考动画或VLM评判，衡量模型对空间/外观/时序组合指令的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能由静态图与自然语言生成动效，但缺乏对"空间+外观+时序"复合指令遵循能力的可靠评估手段。现有做法依赖与参考动画比对或VLM打分，难以容纳多种合法实现，且判定噪声大。

Method: 输入完整静态SVG与运动编辑提示，将动效生成与图形生成解耦，贴近设计师工作流；100个实例源自真实Lottie设计，覆盖多样时空动画行为组合。每个实例配有确定性、人类可读的验证程序，可接受不同合法实现并逐条报告各提示要求的通过/失败；程序由多智能体流水线结合人工标注动画与合成测试用例构建。

Result: 在180个动画的平衡测试集上，验证程序与人工判定原始一致率93.3%，GPT-6 Astra VLM基线为83.3%，且在批准与拒绝两类召回上均优于基线。32个LLM、12家提供商的47种配置中，pass@1为6.0%–67.7%；15个模型在最高推理强度、每实例32次尝试下，pass@32提升至38.0%–95.0%。

Conclusion: 基于程序化验证的评估在一致性上显著优于VLM评判，并显示现有模型在复合动效指令遵循上仍有明显差距；典型失败包括未匹配参考姿态、未遵循运动轨迹、相关事件缺乏协调。基准已开源。

Abstract: Large language models can generate motion graphics from static images and natural language requests, but it remains difficult to evaluate how well models can follow requests that combine spatial, appearance, and temporal requirements. We introduce MotionGraphicsBench, an expert-curated benchmark of 100 instances for animating an input static SVG with a motion edit prompt. By supplying all required visual elements, our editing task reflects a designer-like workflow and isolates motion generation from graphic generation. Derived from real-world Lottie motion designs, our benchmark contains diverse combinations of spatio-temporal animation behaviors. Each benchmark instance pairs its model input with a deterministic, human-readable verification program that accepts different valid implementations and reports which prompt requirements pass or fail, rather than comparing against a reference animation or using a VLM judge. We construct these programs with a multi-agent pipeline using human-labeled animations and synthesized test cases. On a balanced test set of 180 animations, our programs achieve 93.3% raw agreement with human judgments, compared with 83.3% for a GPT-6 Astra VLM baseline, and outperform the baseline in both approval and rejection recall. Across 47 configurations from 32 LLMs and 12 providers, pass@1 ranges from 6.0% to 67.7%. Across 15 models evaluated at their highest available reasoning effort with 32 attempts per instance, additional attempts improve every model, but pass@32 still ranges from 38.0% to 95.0%. Our analysis of verification programs reveals that generated animations often fail to match reference poses, follow requested motion trajectories, or coordinate related events. We release our benchmark at https://huggingface.co/datasets/figma-ai/motion-graphics-bench.

</details>


### [81] [The RenAIssance: Caption-Guided Diffusion Models for Historical-Art Visual Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10599)
*Zhanyu Tuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出The RenAIssance可扩展流水线：用BLIP为DEArt历史欧洲艺术藏品生成场景级描述，构建约1.5万图文对，训练Stable Diffusion v1.5的紧凑LoRA适配器，将生成分布推向历史艺术风格，同时保留基础模型对新概念的渲染能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI可将文化藏品扩展为可提示驱动的视觉内容，但历史艺术品普遍缺乏配对文本描述，难以直接适配现代图像生成器，因此需要可规模化的图文配对与风格适配方案。

Method: 流水线化：以BLIP自动生成场景级caption，得到约15,000个DEArt图文对；在此基础上训练面向SD v1.5的紧凑LoRA适配器，将可复用的风格表示与基础生成器解耦。

Result: 在历史与当代、不同长度的提示下，各设置下相对DEArt参考分布的FID均有改善，CLIP文图相似度仅小幅下降；定性结果显示同一适配器既能生成符合时代的主体，也能以连贯的历史视觉语言表现当代概念。

Conclusion: 通过解耦可复用风格表示与基础生成器，该方法为可控历史艺术媒体创作、交互式创作与富媒体体验提供了实用构件。

Abstract: Generative AI can expand cultural collections into prompt-responsive visual media, but historical artworks rarely provide the paired descriptions needed to adapt modern image generators. We present The RenAIssance, a scalable pipeline that turns a collection of historical European artworks into a reusable text-to-image media resource. The pipeline generates scene-level captions with BLIP and uses the resulting approximately 15,000 image--text pairs from DEArt to train a compact LoRA adapter for Stable Diffusion v1.5. The adapter shifts generated images toward the historical-art distribution while retaining the base model's ability to render novel concepts. On historical and contemporary prompts of different lengths, it improves FID to the DEArt reference distribution in every setting with only a modest decrease in CLIP text--image similarity. Qualitative results show that the same adapter can render both period-appropriate subjects and contemporary concepts in a coherent historical visual language. By decoupling a reusable style representation from the base generator, the approach offers a practical building block for controllable historical-art media creation, interactive authoring, and rich-media experiences.

</details>


### [82] [Reason First, Retrieve What Matters: Hypothesis-Guided Keyframe Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10609)
*Yisong Hu,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Jiangbin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HypoKFS：一种免训练、即插即用的框架，将关键帧选择与 MLLM 迭代推理耦合，用初始假设空间驱动判别性帧选择，仅在必要时进行额外视觉计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需在有限帧预算下定位稀疏、时间分散的证据。现有方法先用密集采样候选集做全局图文匹配检索查询相关帧，但当少量均匀采样帧已足够时带来无谓计算；且仅依赖查询相关性会忽略区分竞争答案的证据，一次性选择也无法纠错。

Method: HypoKFS 先在稀疏采样帧上推理，构建初始假设空间；由答案分布判断是否需要补充选帧。需要时，用保留的假设引导选择判别性帧，再重新推理并精化假设，迭代进行，从而把额外视觉计算集中到未解决的假设上，避免全局帧匹配。

Result: 在 Video-MME、LongVideoBench 和 MLVU 上，多种 MLLM 主干均取得一致提升；准确率最高提升 6.7 个百分点，在仅 1.3% 的参考帧-假设匹配开销下于 Video-MME 达到 72.9% 准确率。

Conclusion: 以假设驱动的按需选帧替代一次性全局匹配，可在极低计算开销下有效提升长视频理解性能，且具备免训练、即插即用的通用性。

Abstract: Long video understanding requires models to identify and reason over sparse, temporally distributed evidence within limited frame budgets. Existing keyframe selection methods typically retrieve query-relevant frames from a densely sampled candidate set before inference. However, global image-text matching incurs unnecessary computation when a small set of uniformly sampled frames already provides sufficient evidence. Query relevance alone may also overlook evidence that distinguishes competing answers, while one-shot selection offers no opportunity to correct earlier selection errors. We propose HypoKFS, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that couples keyframe selection with iterative inference by multimodal large language models (MLLMs). HypoKFS first reasons over sparsely sampled frames to construct an initial hypothesis space. The resulting answer distribution determines whether additional frame selection is needed. When needed, the retained hypotheses guide the selection of discriminative frames, followed by renewed inference and hypothesis refinement. This iterative process directs additional visual computation toward unresolved hypotheses while avoiding unnecessary global frame matching. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple MLLM backbones. HypoKFS improves accuracy by up to 6.7 percentage points and achieves 72.9% accuracy on Video-MME at only 1.3% of the reference frame-hypothesis matching cost.

</details>


### [83] [EndoSCOP-V: A Multi-Turn Video Understanding Evaluation Framework for Multimodal Models in Endoscopy Reporting and Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10626)
*Abhishek Tyagi,Tanmay Jain,Vilas Biradar,Krithi Krishna Koduri,Misba Hul Bashir Masoodi,Swetha Rapolu,Hardik Rughwani,Rachana Kotha,Anjan Kumar Kaipa,Anudeep Katrevula,Rakesh Garlapati,Aniruddha Pratap Singh,Rajendra Patel,Abhay Tyagi,Sundeep Lakhtakia,Mohan Ramchandani,Rakesh Kalapala,D. Nageshwar Reddy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出EndoSCOP-V，一个面向结构化内镜报告与临床推理的视频多轮评测框架，包含1,307段真实内镜/结肠镜视频（63.2小时，平均约174秒）及20,274个由临床医生填写的结构化电子病历确定性生成的问答轮次，覆盖9任务×30子任务、54种WEO MST 3.0疾病；在15位认证内镜医师78%严格准确率的基线下，最优27B级开源LMM仅达59.8%，前沿专有模型在内镜相关严格准确率上落后医师组23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内镜多模态基准仅测试静态图像或单一疾病视频，忽略了真实临床报告所需的时序与临床推理链（定位、形态、严重程度、干预决策），因而无法反映模型在长时程视频中的真实报告能力。

Method: 构建EndoSCOP-V：1,307段真实内镜/结肠镜视频（63.2h，97%为1080p，平均约174s），问答由临床医生结构化报告EHR确定性实例化（无LLM改写、无合成标注），源自1,033个经医生审阅的模板（每病种约18个），覆盖9任务×30子任务分类体系与54种WEO MST 3.0疾病，22%病例含共病（最多6种）。采用马尔可夫式上下文剪枝oracle-forcing协议逐轮评测，每轮前注入金标准答案以隔离单轮推理。以15名认证内镜医师在1,500题上的表现为基线，评测14个开放权重LMM及3个前沿专有模型（组间抽查）。

Result: 医师组严格准确率基线78%。14个开源LMM中最佳的27B级模型达59.8%，剔除格式平凡性疾病否定题后降至53.4%；3个前沿专有模型中最佳者在内镜严格准确率上落后医师组23个百分点。子任务分析显示，模型在定量测量、多变量分级及长时程视频中的干预推理上存在持续差距。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了临床对齐、细粒度且可复现的内镜视频多轮推理基准，揭示当前LMM在长时程内镜视频的结构化报告与临床推理上仍显著落后于专家，尤其在定量测量、多变量分级与干预决策等能力上存在系统性短板。

Abstract: Endoscopic diagnosis unfolds over native video, where a single positive finding during traversal of the gastrointestinal tract sets off a multi-minute chain of reasoning over location, morphology, severity and intervention. Existing endoscopy multimodal benchmarks test only static images or single-disease videos, which miss the temporal and clinical reasoning that real-world reporting demands. We introduce EndoSCOP-V, a video-grounded multi-turn evaluation framework for structured endoscopy reporting and clinical reasoning. It contains 1,307 real-world endoscopy and colonoscopy videos (63.2 h; 97% 1080p) averaging about 174 s. Its 20,274 question-answer turns are instantiated deterministically from clinician-filled structured endoscopy-reporting EHRs, so no per-case annotation is synthetic or LLM-rephrased. They come from 1,033 clinician-reviewed templates, about 18 per disease, that span a clinically aligned 9-task x 30-sub-task reporting taxonomy over 54 World Endoscopy Organization (WEO) MST 3.0 diseases. 22% of procedures carry co-existing pathologies, up to six per case. A Markovian context-pruned oracle-forcing protocol evaluates the dialogue turn by turn, injecting the gold answer before each subsequent turn so that per-turn reasoning is isolated from cascading dialogue errors. A panel of 15 board-certified endoscopists sets a 78% strict-accuracy baseline on 1,500 questions. Across 14 open-weight LMMs, the best 27B-class model reaches 59.8% strict accuracy, falling to 53.4% once format-trivial disease-rejection items are excluded. On a between-subjects spot-check, the best of 3 frontier proprietary models trails the clinician panel by 23 pp on strict NDR accuracy. Sub-task analysis reveals consistent gaps in quantitative measurement, multi-variable grading and intervention reasoning on long-horizon video. Data: Hugging Face (EndoSCOP-V). Code: GitHub (abhi25t/EndoSCOP-V).

</details>


### [84] [Latent-WAM++: Shaping World and Action Representations for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10632)
*Yichen Zhang,Yupeng Zheng,Linbo Wang,Pengxuan Yang,Zebin Xing,Junli Wang,Shiwei Li,Kangwei Liu,Fan Ren,Qichao Zhang,Yifeng Pan,Ji Tao,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以表征为中心的世界动作模型 Latent-WAM++，将每类中间表征的监督方式与其在规划中的作用相匹配，在 NAVSIM v2 达 90.1 EPDMS 并在 HUGSIM 零样本评测取得最佳性能，训练成本较 Latent-WAM 降低 88.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 主要训练预测未来状态或最终动作，连接"预测—规划"的中间世界表征与动作表征仅受到间接约束，其监督信号与其在规划中的实际作用不匹配，导致表征质量与规划性能受限。

Method: 三部分设计：(1) 世界表征构建（World Representation Construction）融合场景-自车未来预测、基于 SIGReg 的分布稳定化与冻结几何先验，无需 EMA 目标编码器即获得时序可预测、非退化、几何感知的世界表征；(2) 轨迹 MeanFlow 规划器直接预测多模态轨迹提案，采用改进的 MeanFlow 速度空间目标优化，支持一步采样；(3) 视觉-动作表征对齐，将输出前动作特征与由当前视觉语义生成、带 stop-gradient 的目标对齐。

Result: NAVSIM v2 上达 90.1 EPDMS（SOTA）；HUGSIM 零样本评测取得 47.0 平均 RC 与 30.1 平均 HD-Score；规划器端到端训练耗时 190 GPU-hours，较 Latent-WAM 的 1,600 GPU-hours 降低 88.1%。

Conclusion: 将表征监督与规划角色对齐，可在不依赖 EMA 目标编码器的情况下提升世界表征质量，并使一步采样的多模态规划器取得 SOTA 精度与显著降低的训练成本，验证了"表征为中心"WAM 设计的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Latent-WAM++, a representation-centered World Action Model (WAM) for end-to-end autonomous driving. Existing WAMs are primarily trained to predict future states or final actions, leaving the intermediate world and action representations that connect prediction to planning only indirectly constrained. Latent-WAM++ matches the supervision of each representation to its role in planning. World Representation Construction combines scene-ego future prediction, SIGReg-based distribution stabilization, and frozen geometric priors to learn a temporally predictive, non-degenerate, and geometry-aware world representation without an EMA target encoder. Trajectory MeanFlow Planner directly predicts multimodal trajectory proposals while being optimized with an Improved MeanFlow velocity-space objective, enabling one-step sampling. Visual-Action Representation Alignment further aligns the resulting pre-output action features with a stop-gradient target derived from current visual semantics. Latent-WAM++ achieves state-of-the-art performance on NAVSIM v2 and under zero-shot evaluation on HUGSIM, reaching 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and 47.0 average RC and 30.1 average HD-Score on HUGSIM. Training the planner end to end costs 190 GPU-hours for Latent-WAM++ versus 1,600 for Latent-WAM, a reduction of 88.1%.

</details>


### [85] [VAComp: View-Aware Full Projector Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10637)
*Kai Zhou,Qingyue Deng,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAComp是首个面向移动观察者的视角感知全投影补偿框架，结合2D高斯视角条件几何校正与学习式光度补偿，实现单次前向推理的实时/交互式补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全投影补偿方法假设相机视角静止：视角相关方法在新视角下需重训练或重标定；NeRF/GS类视角无关方法依赖SfM标定ProCams，存在全局尺度未知问题，且视角感知跟踪需标定与投影仪朝向相反的相机，带来额外标定难题。

Method: 提出VAComp框架：以度量标定为参考恢复SfM全局尺度；采用双棋盘（分别朝向RGB相机与深度相机）标定反向相机；设计两种非迭代混合方法VAComp-SD与VAComp-SI，将2D高斯视角条件几何校正与学习式光度补偿结合，单次前向传播预测补偿图像，无需逐帧迭代优化。

Result: 真实场景实验表明两种方法均取得有竞争力的补偿质量，VAComp-SD支持实时更新，VAComp-SI达到交互级速率；可使观察者看到视觉上替代真实物体的虚拟物体，或观察到视角一致的外观与材质修改。

Conclusion: VAComp在裸眼设置下实现了移动观察者的视角感知全投影补偿，通过尺度恢复与反向相机标定解决标定难题，并以非迭代混合设计兼顾补偿质量与运行效率。

Abstract: Full projector compensation adjusts a projector's input image to compensate for both geometric and photometric distortions introduced by the projection surface. Existing methods generally treat the viewing condition as a static camera viewpoint. When applied to a moving viewer, existing methods face two key challenges: (1) view-dependent methods require model retraining or recalibration to compensate for novel viewpoints; and (2) NeRF/GS-based view-agnostic methods use structure from motion (SfM) to calibrate the projector-camera systems (ProCams) with an unknown global scale, while view-aware tracking requires calibrating a camera oriented opposite to the projector, which introduces an additional calibration challenge. To address these challenges, we introduce VAComp, the first view-aware framework for full projector compensation for a moving viewer in a naked-eye setting. Using a projector, VAComp enables the viewer to perceive a virtual object that visually replaces the real object or to observe view-consistent modifications of the real object's appearance and material properties. To resolve the SfM scale ambiguity, we use metric calibration as a reference to recover the global scale; to calibrate the oppositely facing camera, we use two checkerboards, one facing the RGB camera and the other facing the depth camera. To balance compensation quality and runtime efficiency, we develop two non-iterative hybrid methods, VAComp-SD and VAComp-SI. Both combine 2D Gaussian-based view-conditioned geometric correction with learned photometric compensation, predicting each compensation image through a single forward pass without per-frame iterative optimization. Real-world experiments show that both methods achieve competitive compensation quality, with VAComp-SD enabling real-time updates and VAComp-SI operating at interactive rates. Project page: https://realkaizhou.github.io/VAComp/.

</details>


### [86] [VisProve: Learning Typed Visual Provenance for Scientific Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10642)
*Jingxuan Wei,Zhengyang Wang,Zheng Guo,Xiaoming Wu,Fuqiang Wang,Xin Wang,Bihui Yu,Yining Wang,Junnan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出类型化视觉溯源任务，构建ReViE数据集（3,941题，标注Quotation/Compression/Inference三类支撑关系），并提出VisProve模型，通过可微空间证据读取与证据-答案关系反馈提升细粒度溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉引用只表明答案"在何处被支持"，却不刻画被引内容"如何"贡献于答案；科学文档问答中模型可能直接读取数值、压缩多个观察或组合证据进行推断，缺乏对支撑关系的显式建模。

Method: 构建ReViE数据集，覆盖完整科学文档，提供页面、章节、区域三级标注及三类支撑关系标签；提出VisProve，在联合答案与溯源生成中引入可微空间证据读取（Spatial Evidence Reading）与证据-答案关系反馈（Evidence-Answer Relation Feedback），通过门控范数有界残差将局部视觉证据及其估计支撑关系作为下一词预测的条件。

Result: 在文档不相交的ReViE测试集上，相同骨干下相较联合监督微调：Typed-F1由3.52提升至5.48，Section-F1由45.99提升至53.24，Page-F1基本持平。

Conclusion: 将视觉定位与支撑关系建模耦合主要改善细粒度溯源质量，而对粗粒度页面级定位影响有限；Typed-F1绝对数值偏低表明答案生成与精确视觉证据归因之间仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: Visual citations show where an answer is supported, but not how the cited content contributes to it. In scientific document question answering, a model may read a value directly, compress several observations, or combine evidence to infer a conclusion. We study typed visual provenance, where a system generates an answer together with source regions and explicit support relations. We introduce ReViE, a dataset of 3,941 questions over complete scientific documents, with page, section, and region annotations labeled as Quotation, Compression, or Inference. We further propose VisProve, which augments joint answer and provenance generation with differentiable Spatial Evidence Reading and Evidence-Answer Relation Feedback. VisProve conditions next-token prediction on localized visual evidence and its estimated support relation through a gated norm-bounded residual. On the document-disjoint ReViE test set, VisProve improves Typed-F1 from 3.52 to 5.48 and Section-F1 from 45.99 to 53.24 over joint supervised fine-tuning with the same backbone, while Page-F1 changes only marginally. These results suggest that coupling visual grounding with support relation modeling mainly improves fine-grained provenance, while the low absolute Typed-F1 reveals a substantial gap between answer generation and precise visual evidence attribution.

</details>


### [87] [Agent2BIM: A Systematic Evaluation of Reality-Capture Configurations and Prior Models for As-Built Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10644)
*Boyu Wang,Borja García de Soto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent2BIM，一种基于智能体的Scan-to-BIM框架，并在实验室与MEP场景中系统评估七种采集配置对重建精度与连通性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能从实景采集数据生成竣工BIM，但何种证据足以支撑准确重建尚不明确，缺乏对采集配置的系统性对比证据。

Method: 构建Agent2BIM智能体框架，在实验室与MEP场景中评测七种输入配置（360°全景、手机视频、手机LiDAR、地面激光扫描TLS、TLS加位姿全景、以及结合先验IFC模型的影像），并以TLS验证的参考模型为基准，评估构件检测、几何精度与MEP连通性。

Result: 实验室场景构件召回率84%–100%，但仅9%的全景重建构件位于参考位置20 cm以内，手机LiDAR为86%；MEP场景中无尺度输入仅恢复11%–43%构件，度量输入为75%–93%；先验IFC模型可改善MEP重建，但会传播误差。

Conclusion: 视觉证据与度量证据在智能体式Scan-to-BIM中具有互补作用，准确重建需兼顾二者。

Abstract: AI agents can generate as-built Building Information Models (BIMs) from reality-capture data, but the evidence required for accurate reconstruction remains unclear. This paper presents Agent2BIM, an agent-based Scan-to-BIM framework, and evaluates seven capture configurations in a research laboratory and a mechanical, electrical, and plumbing (MEP) scene. Inputs include 360° panoramas, smartphone video, smartphone LiDAR, terrestrial laser scanning (TLS), TLS with posed panoramas, and imagery supplemented by prior IFC models. Outputs are compared against TLS-verified reference models for component detection, geometric accuracy, and MEP connectivity. In the laboratory, component recall ranged from 84% to 100%, but only 9% of components reconstructed from panoramas were within 20 cm of their reference positions, compared with 86% using smartphone LiDAR. In the MEP scene, scale-free inputs recovered 11 - 43% of components, versus 75 - 93% for metric inputs. Prior IFC models improved MEP reconstruction but could propagate inaccuracies. The results highlight the complementary roles of visual and metric evidence in agent-based Scan-to-BIM.

</details>


### [88] [SHIN: Scalable Hierarchy for INcremental 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10652)
*Reagan Koo,Yeong-Gyu Kim,Eun-Seok Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHIN是一种可扩展的3DGS分层表示，由一个可复用的Base和若干可叠加增强层构成，按字节预算自适应分配高斯添加与球谐保留，实现单一累积表示覆盖多个质量-体积工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实时高质量新视角合成，但不同设备与网络条件的部署预算各异；多数紧凑方法只针对单一工作点，预算变化即需另存一份表示，缺乏复用性。

Method: 构建可扩展分层结构：高层工作点以增量方式扩展而非替换已解码表示。用多尺度位置-尺度敏感性推导统一的全局高斯排序；每阶段预算在“新增高斯”与“保留SH频带”之间按校准后的梯度得分/字节进行划分；并可进行轻量不透明度更新以调整合成，而传输的几何与SH系数保持不变。

Result: 在三个基准上，SHIN逐步提升同一累积表示的质量，并在与多码率及渐进式基线的所有重叠比较中取得最优PSNR-体积效率。

Conclusion: 通过统一高斯排序与按字节的预算划分，可将单一分层3DGS表示复用于多预算场景，在保持增量可解码的同时获得优于多码率/渐进基线的率失真效率。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides high-quality real-time novel-view synthesis, but deployment budgets vary across devices and network conditions. Most compact methods target one operating point, so another budget requires a separate representation. SHIN is a scalable 3DGS hierarchy with a reusable Base and additive enhancement layers, so each higher-quality operating point extends rather than replaces the decoded representation. Constructing this hierarchy requires deciding which Gaussians each stage adds and how much spherical-harmonic (SH) appearance each retains, because these choices remain fixed in later prefixes. SHIN derives one shared Gaussian order from multi-scale position-scale sensitivity and divides each stage budget between adding Gaussians and retaining SH bands using calibrated gradient-based scores per byte. Lightweight opacity updates adjust compositing while transmitted geometry and SH coefficients remain unchanged. Across three benchmarks, SHIN progressively improves one cumulative representation and achieves the best PSNR-size efficiency in every overlapping comparison with the multi-rate and progressive baselines.

</details>


### [89] [FG-DSA: Frequency-Guided Depthwise Separable Attention for Efficient Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10653)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FG-DSA框架，用2D FFT高通滤波将频域先验显式注入深度可分离空间注意力，以线性复杂度帮助轻量网络恢复清晰边界，在语义分割和扩散生成U-Net中均带来稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限场景下的密集预测与生成任务中，反复下采样会显著损失空间细节、劣化边界；现有注意力机制虽缓解语义歧义，但难以保留高频特征，或以极高计算代价换取。

Method: FG-DSA采用二维FFT高通滤波模块，显式地将频域先验注入空间注意力机制，并结合深度可分离注意力，使轻量网络在线性复杂度下恢复锐利边界过渡；同时可作为一种模块嵌入扩散生成U-Net。

Result: 实验表明FG-DSA能有效恢复细粒度目标边界，在语义分割任务上取得一致的性能提升；作为扩散U-Net中的模块使用时，仅增加极少的参数与FLOPs即可显著提升生成图像的细节质量。

Conclusion: 频域先验与深度可分离注意力的结合是高效保留高频信息、改善边界质量的有效途径，且具备从密集预测向好生成任务迁移的通用性；文中"FGA-DSA"应为笔误。

Abstract: Dense prediction and generative modeling in resource-constrained scenarios remain challenging, as repeated downsampling substantially reduces spatial detail and degrades boundaries. Although recent attention mechanisms have alleviated the problem of semantic ambiguity, they often fail to preserve high-frequency features or do so at the expense of a very high computational cost. In this paper, we propose the Frequency Guided Depthwise Separable Attention for Efficient Dense Prediction (FG-DSA), a novel framework for efficient dense visual understanding and generation. FG-DSA employs a 2D FFT high-pass filtering module to explicitly inject frequency domain priors into the spatial attention mechanism, allowing lightweight networks to recover sharp boundary transitions with linear complexity. A range of experiments show that FG-DSA is effective at recovering fine object boundaries and leads to a consistent performance gain in the task of semantic segmentation. Importantly, we show that FGA-DSA can be used as a module in diffusion-based generative U-Nets, leading to a significant improvement in the detail of the generated images with very little additional parameter and Floating Point Operation (FLOP) cost.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamics-Informed Action Learning for Goal-Conditioned Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10660)
*Yinuo Jiang,Guangzhi Wang,Yiran Wang,Junfu Pu,Cheng Cheng,Honghao Chen,Cheng Cheng,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIAL 通过训练阶段的 rollout alignment 与多步逆动力学监督，并在测试时用逆动力学头自回归耦合前向模型生成动作序列以初始化 CEM 提案分布，弥合潜空间世界模型中“动力学学习”与“规划使用”之间的鸿沟，在四个视觉控制基准上优于现有潜空间世界模型与规划基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间世界模型可在紧凑表征空间规划动作序列实现视觉控制，但动力学学习方式与规划使用方式存在两处脱节：(1) teacher-forced 训练未让动力学模型接触规划时递归预测的潜状态，导致训练-测试分布不匹配；(2) 基于采样的规划器通常以与目标无关的提案初始化动作搜索，未利用世界模型已学到的动作条件动力学。

Method: 训练端：(a) rollout alignment，使前向模型暴露于自身自回归潜预测；(b) 多步逆动力学监督，训练轻量逆动力学头从潜状态转移推断动作；(c) 将逆动力学头参数固定，应用于模型生成的转移，促使预测 rollout 保留动作相关信息。测试端：自回归耦合逆动力学与前向动力学，朝向目标构造 dynamics-informed 动作序列，用于初始化 CEM 提案分布的均值，随后由标准 CEM 采样与更新过程精化。

Result: 在四个视觉控制基准的标准与长时程场景中，DIAL 均优于现有潜空间世界模型与规划基线。

Conclusion: 通过在训练中引入自回归 rollout 对齐与逆动力学监督、在规划中以动力学知情动作序列初始化 CEM，可有效弥合潜动力学学习与规划使用间的差距，并带来标准及长时程任务上的性能提升。

Abstract: Latent world models enable visual control by planning action sequences in compact representation spaces, but a gap remains between how latent dynamics are learned and how they are used for planning. First, teacher-forced training does not expose the dynamics model to the recursively predicted latent states encountered during planning. Second, sampling-based planners typically initialize action search from goal-agnostic proposals, without exploiting the action-conditioned dynamics learned by the world model. We introduce Dynamics-Informed Action Learning (DIAL) to bridge these two gaps. During training, rollout alignment exposes the forward model to its own autoregressive latent predictions, while multi-step inverse supervision trains a lightweight inverse dynamics head to infer actions from latent-state transitions. We further apply the inverse head to model-generated transitions with its parameters held fixed, encouraging predicted rollouts to retain action-relevant information. At test time, DIAL autoregressively couples the inverse and forward dynamics to construct a dynamics-informed action sequence toward the goal, which initializes the mean of the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) proposal distribution. The subsequent CEM search then refines this initialization using the standard sampling and updating procedure. Experiments across four visual control benchmarks demonstrate that DIAL outperforms existing latent world models and planning baselines in both standard and long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [91] [Physically Guided TeX Generation for Low-Light Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10665)
*Daoheng Li,Jianjun Qian,Mengkai Yan,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TeViT-Gen：从配对可见光-红外图像生成温度、发射率与纹理(TeX)物理图的框架，含无需像素级物理标签的TeXMetric评价与TeX Tune任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光下可见光对比度低、细节缺失，红外图缺色彩纹理，故需红外-可见光融合；现有融合方法只优化外观或任务特征，未显式解耦热观测背后的耦合物理因素，热退化下缺乏物理解释性；高光谱热测量可物理分解但需专用硬件，且像素级物理标注成本高。

Method: TeX Generation构建互补多视角特征，用独立分支门控将线索分别路由至Temperature、Emissivity、Texture三张图；TeXMetric结合辐射重建、通道结构与材质一致性在无像素级物理标签下评估生成图；TeX Tune冻结生成器，联合优化调整模块与任务网络实现任务引导的性能提升。

Result: 在FLIR、LLVIP、M3FD、MSRS数据集上低光任务性能具竞争力；HADAR实验考察TeXMetric分数与下游检测性能的关联。

Conclusion: 以物理引导方式从常规可见光-红外图像生成可解释的TeX物理图，无需专用高光谱硬件与像素级物理标注，兼顾物理可解释性与下游任务性能。

Abstract: Low-light conditions reduce visible contrast and obscure details, while infrared images lack color and texture, motivating the fusion of infrared and visible information for visual tasks. Existing fusion methods primarily optimize image appearance or task-specific features without explicitly disentangling the coupled factors underlying thermal observations, limiting their physical interpretation under thermal degeneracy. Hyperspectral thermal measurements enable physical decomposition, but they require specialized imaging hardware, while pixel-level physical annotations for supervision or evaluation incur additional costs. We propose TeX-Gen, a physically guided framework for TeX generation from paired visible and infrared images in low-light visual tasks. TeX Generation constructs complementary multi-view features and employs independent branch-specific gates to route cues into the Temperature, Emissivity, and Texture maps. TeXMetric integrates radiometric reconstruction, channel structure, and material consistency to assess the generated maps without requiring pixel-level physical labels. TeX Tune improves performance through task-guided adjustments, jointly optimizing the adjustment modules and task network while keeping the generator frozen. Experiments show that TeX-Gen achieves competitive task performance in low-light environments on the FLIR, LLVIP, M3FD, and MSRS datasets. Experiments on HADAR also examine the association between TeXMetric scores and downstream detection performance.

</details>


### [92] [Heterogeneous Asymmetric Contrastive Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10672)
*Qi Wang,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出异构非对称对比蒸馏（HACD），将异构教师（如Transformer）的logit映射到共享单位超球面上的中心化、温度软化概率方向，以对比学习方式传递样本间关系结构，而非逐样本模仿logit值；在CIFAR-100的12对异构组合中11对、ImageNet-1K的15对中14对取得最佳或次佳精度，平均较vanilla KD提升8.82与1.33个百分点，且无需真值标签、无额外可学习参数、每样本仅需一次教师前向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨架构（如Transformer教师→CNN学生）知识蒸馏困难，因两类网络学到的表征不同。Logit虽共享标签空间，但异构教师的logit存在偏移、尺度与锐度差异，且非目标类语义组织性较弱。传统logit蒸馏逐样本匹配数值，本质是让学生模仿另一架构的预测表达方式，不合理。

Method: 提出HACD：将教师与学生logit映射为单位超球面上的中心化、温度软化概率方向，消除架构相关偏移，并由温度控制输出锐度进入表征的程度，同时保留有信息量的非目标类；每个学生输出需在batch内识别其对应的教师目标，教师目标互为负样本；在中等增强视图上计算目标，并用中等与强两种学生视图与其对比。

Result: CIFAR-100上12对异构组合中11对、ImageNet-1K上15对中14对达到最佳或次佳精度（无真值标签）；相对报告中的vanilla KD结果平均提升8.82与1.33个百分点。方法不增加可学习参数，每样本仅需一次教师前向。

Conclusion: 异构教师应传递其预测的关系结构而非逐样本数值；将logit转为超球面概率方向并以非对称对比目标蒸馏，是一种无参数、低开销且在多架构组合上稳定有效的异构KD方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) between architectural families, e.g., from a Transformer teacher to a CNN student, is difficult because the two networks learn different representations. Logits share a label space, but heterogeneous teachers differ in logit offset, scale, and sharpness, and their non-target classes are less semantically organized. Conventional logit distillation matches these values sample by sample, asking the student to imitate how another architecture expresses its predictions. We argue that a heterogeneous teacher should pass on the relational structure of its predictions, i.e., how it arranges samples relative to one another, and propose Heterogeneous Asymmetric Contrastive Distillation (HACD) to transfer this structure. HACD maps teacher and student logits to centered, temperature-softened probability directions on a shared unit hypersphere, which removes architecture-dependent offsets and lets the temperature control how sharply peaked outputs enter the representation while keeping informative non-target classes. Each student output must then identify its own teacher target within the batch. Because every teacher target also acts as a negative for the others, HACD computes the targets on a moderately augmented view and contrasts both a moderate and a strong student view against them. HACD adds no learnable parameters and needs one teacher pass per sample. It achieves the best or second-best accuracy on 11 of 12 heterogeneous pairs on CIFAR-100, without ground-truth labels, and on 14 of 15 pairs on ImageNet-1K, improving over the reported vanilla KD results by 8.82 and 1.33 percentage points on average. Checkpoints are available at https://github.com/BTAEYANG/HACD-Heterogeneous-Asymmetric-Contrastive-Distillation; the code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [93] [MGE: A Membership Guided Data Extraction Attack against Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10678)
*Yuchang Mei,Longqiang Wang,Haoyang Jiang,Mingyang Yi,Shaolei Zhang,Keman Huang,Wuqiong Pan,Ju Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出成员引导提取框架MGE，将扩散模型训练数据提取建模为条件生成，借助成员推断分类器生成条件信号，并结合对抗梯度与并行分支解码，从预训练扩散模型中成功提取数百张训练图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像生成上表现优异，但其记忆特性带来训练数据泄漏的潜在安全风险。需要显式构造数据提取攻击方法以揭示并量化该隐私风险，从而推动扩散模型的隐私保护研究。

Method: 将数据提取形式化为条件生成问题，条件信号来自成员推断（membership inference）分类器，形成成员引导提取（MGE）框架；为提升成功率与效率，引入对抗梯度（adversarial gradient）与并行分支解码（parallel branch decoding）策略。

Result: 在图像生成基准上的实验表明，MGE能够从预训练扩散模型中提取出数百张训练图像，验证了训练数据泄漏风险的现实性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的记忆导致的训练数据泄漏风险确实存在且可被有效利用，应更加重视扩散模型的隐私保护设计。

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have achieved remarkable performance in image generation. However, their inherent memory properties introduce a potential safety risk of training data leakage, i.e., data extraction attack. To illustrate such risk, we propose a membership guided extraction (MGE) framework to effectively extract training data from a pre-trained diffusion model. Our core idea is to formulate the data extraction as a conditional generation, where the condition signal is from a membership inference classifier. To improve the success rate and efficiency of MGE, we propose adversarial gradient and parallel branch decoding. Our experiments on image generation benchmarks demonstrate that our MGE can extract hundreds of images, which shows that more attention should be paid to privacy-preserving of diffusion model.

</details>


### [94] [JoVox: Joint Hierarchical Flow Matching for Lightweight Voxel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10690)
*Xiangjun Tang,Jianqi Chen,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoVox 提出一种离散流匹配（discrete flow matching）框架，学习体素层次中所有层级的联合分布，并配合两级预测与重访式解掩码策略，在保持轻量的同时生成高保真体素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体素生成器难以兼顾高保真与轻量：高保真模型计算量大，轻量模型保真度低。层次化方法虽保持体素轻量特性，但其粗层级（决定所有更细单元出现位置）仅利用极少形状信息生成且从不被修正，导致结果往往"看似合理但错误"。

Method: 核心思路是让更细单元参与粗单元的决策。为此提出 JoVox：基于离散流匹配学习体素层次所有层级的联合分布，而非各层独立分布，从而在预测粗单元时邻近的细单元已生成。框架内进一步引入：(1) 两级预测，一次输出细两级的单元而非细一级，使网络处理的层数减半并提升保真度；(2) 重访式解掩码策略（unmasking），在细单元生成后修正早期决策，纠正掩码扩散模型中的不一致。

Result: 在无条件与图像条件体素生成任务上均优于基线。与当前 SOTA 方法 TRELLIS.2 相比：训练吞吐提升 8 倍，单次前向 FLOPs 仅为 1/15，训练 GPU 从 32 张降至 4 张，且质量更高，Chamfer 距离降低 11%。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模层次分布并允许细层信息回馈粗层决策，JoVox 证明可在显著降低计算成本（更少 GPU、更低 FLOPs、更高吞吐）的前提下实现更高保真度的体素生成，为轻量高保真三维生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity voxels while keeping their lightweight nature remains an open problem in computer vision. Current generators are either high-fidelity but computationally heavy, or lightweight but of lower fidelity. Hierarchical methods keep the lightweight nature of voxels. However, their coarse levels, which decide where all finer cells can appear, are generated with the least information about the shape and are never revisited, so they are often plausible but wrong. Our key insight is to let finer cells take part in deciding coarse cells. To this end, we propose JoVox, a discrete flow matching framework that learns the joint distribution of all levels of the voxel hierarchy, instead of a separate distribution for each level, so that coarse cells can be predicted with finer cells already generated nearby. Within this framework, we further introduce a two-level prediction, which outputs the cells two levels finer at once instead of one level finer, halving the number of levels that the network processes and yielding voxels of higher fidelity. We also introduce an unmasking strategy that revisits early decisions once finer cells are generated and corrects inconsistencies in masked diffusion models. Our framework outperforms the baselines on both unconditional and image-conditioned voxel generation. Compared with the current state-of-the-art TRELLIS.2, we achieve 8x higher training throughput, 1/15 of the FLOPs in a forward pass, train on 4 vs. 32 GPUs, and obtain higher quality with an 11% lower Chamfer distance.

</details>


### [95] [Perception, Not Reasoning, Limits Visual Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10694)
*Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi,Syed Talal Wasim,Juergen Gall,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉心智理论的主要瓶颈是感知而非推理：用五条件解离协议与"感知贡献比"指标测得，真实场景状态脚手架可弥合视觉-文本差距，而自动解析器失败，60%错误源于"现实偏差"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在纯文本错误信念任务上接近人类水平，但同一问题以视觉呈现时性能骤降20多个百分点。主流共识将原因归为概念性缺陷并主张新增信念推理模块；本文认为主导瓶颈在于感知前端——模型不是无法推理，而是"看不见"关键场景状态。

Method: 设计五条件解离协议：保持推理任务不变，仅将感知保真度从纯视觉渐变到纯文本；用"感知贡献比"（Perceptual Contribution Ratio，视觉-文本差距中被某一保真度弥合的比例）量化。场景分两类：Heider–Simmel传统的抽象2D面板，以及MuMA-ToM与MindPower的自然3D游戏场景。评测7个VLM、约15.9万次API调用，对比真值脚手架与自动解析器（含前沿VLM）构建的脚手架。

Result: 真值脚手架在所有设置下显著缩小差距，在抽象面板与MuMA-ToM上达到或超过纯文本推理水平；自动解析器脚手架则失败。纯视觉条件下最常见错误为"现实偏差"，占错误信念失败的60%——模型报告物体实际所在位置，而非缺席智能体所相信的位置。

Conclusion: 错误源于感知前端未能追踪各时刻哪些智能体在场。在该协议下，结构化视觉场景状态抽取而非信念推断，是视觉心智理论的主导瓶颈；后续工作应优先改进感知层面的智能体存在性追踪与场景状态表征。

Abstract: Vision-language models pass text-based false-belief probes at near-human levels but drop by over twenty percentage points on abstract panels the moment the same problem is conveyed visually. The consensus diagnosis is conceptual, prescribing new belief-reasoning modules. We argue the dominant bottleneck is perceptual: not what the model cannot reason about, but what it cannot see. We propose a five-condition dissociation protocol that holds the reasoning task constant while varying perceptual fidelity from visual only to text only, and quantify the result with the Perceptual Contribution Ratio, the share of the visual-to-text gap a given fidelity closes. We test this on two kinds of scenes: abstract 2D panels in the Heider--Simmel tradition, and naturalistic 3D game scenes from MuMA-ToM and MindPower. The evaluation covers seven VLMs and roughly 159,000 API calls. Ground-truth scaffolds substantially close the gap in every setting, and on the abstract panels and MuMA-ToM they match or exceed the text-only reasoning. Whereas scaffolds built by automatic parsers, including frontier VLMs, fail. The most common error in the visual-only condition is \emph{reality bias}, which accounts for 60\% of false-belief failures. Models report where an object actually is, not where an absent agent believes it is. This points to a perceptual front-end that fails to track which agents were present at each moment. Under the evaluated protocol, structured visual scene-state extraction, rather than belief inference, is the dominant bottleneck for visual ToM.

</details>


### [96] [PLS-GS: Probabilistic Lidar Scaffolds for Compact, Surface-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10699)
*Krishnan Harikumar,Alejandro Fontan,Thierry Peynot,Emili Hernández,Niko Sünderhauf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLS-GS提出以LiDAR点云拟合的分层高斯混合模型(GMM)作为"概率脚手架"来约束3DGS图元分布，无需体素/八叉树等离散划分，在大规模室外场景中以更少图元获得更贴近实测表面的紧凑表示与更快渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS通常仅由图像优化，而机器人与建图场景往往同时提供相机与高精度LiDAR数据，可兼顾外观与几何结构；但现有方法未能有效利用该互补先验来约束图元贴近真实表面，且常依赖体素网格、八叉树等离散场景划分，模型冗余、不够紧凑。

Method: 对位置与颜色联合拟合分层高斯混合模型，构建连续、自适应的概率脚手架，替代离散划分；将每个可渲染3DGS图元关联到一个GMM分量，并在该分量协方差诱导的局部表面坐标系中加以约束；引入Mahalanobis支撑损失，限制渲染图元相对LiDAR表面的偏移。

Result: 在五个大型室外场景中，相较图像初始化与LiDAR初始化基线，PLS-GS的图元显著更贴近LiDAR实测，同时使用明显更少的图元，模型更紧凑、渲染更快；在同等容量下，图元更贴合LiDAR测量且渲染质量相当。

Conclusion: 利用LiDAR几何先验构建的概率脚手架能有效引导3DGS图元的空间分布，实现几何一致、紧凑高效的场景表示，为融合相机与LiDAR的机器人建图应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is typically optimized from images only, many robotics and mapping applications provide both camera and high-definition LiDAR data, combining rich visual appearance with accurate geometric structure. We introduce PLS-GS, a probabilistic LiDAR scaffold for Gaussian Splatting that exploits this complementary sensing setting to construct a compact 3DGS representation aligned to the measured surface. PLS-GS fits a Hierarchical Gaussian Mixture Model jointly over position and color, yielding a continuous and adaptive scaffold that eliminates the need for discrete scene partitioning such as voxel grids or octrees. The fitted mixture then guides the renderable 3DGS Gaussians: each rendering Gaussian is associated with a GMM component and constrained in the local surface frame induced by that component's covariance. A Mahalanobis support loss bounds the deviation of the rendering Gaussians from the corresponding LiDAR-derived surface. Across five large outdoor scenes, PLS-GS places primitives markedly closer to the LiDAR measurements than both image- and LiDAR-initialized baselines, while employing substantially fewer primitives, yielding a considerably more compact model and faster rendering. At matched capacity, PLS-GS places primitives closer to the LiDAR measurements, with comparable rendering quality. Code is available at https://github.com/k-harikumar/PLS-GS.

</details>


### [97] [Quadratic Bezier Splatting with Structural Initialization for Efficient Painting Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10713)
*Jinfan Liu,Xuhan Zhan,Mengqin Zhao,Guangyi Deng,Wuze Zhang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模型引导的笔画审查渲染框架：以二次Bezier泼溅解析建模连续曲线笔画并做可微alpha合成，结合前馈多尺度笔画解码器提供结构化初始化与笔画尺度分配、测试时笔画优化做高保真细化，并引入单笔画审查机制评估局部笔画质量，实现连贯布局、PSNR最高提升5.46 dB、表征紧凑度提升16%、渲染器前后向计算加速220.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于笔画的渲染多被优化为像素级重建任务，过度依赖全局误差下降：一方面缺乏结构化规划导致笔画退化为碎片化曲线，另一方面局部缺陷图元可被重叠图层掩盖而存活。

Method: 1) 二次Bezier泼溅（Quadratic Bezier Splatting）解析建模连续曲线笔画，配合高效可微alpha合成构建渲染器；2) 在该渲染器上构建前馈多尺度笔画解码器，学习笔画尺度分配以提供结构化初始化；3) 测试时笔画优化补足重建能力，用于高保真细化；4) 单笔画审查机制（single-stroke audit）显式评估局部笔画质量，防止缺陷图元通过逐层补偿在优化中存活。

Result: 相较现有方法获得连贯的布局结构；PSNR最高提升5.46 dB；表征紧凑度提升16%；渲染器前向与反向合计计算加速220.3倍。

Conclusion: 将笔画渲染从纯像素级误差最小化转向"结构化初始化+可微解析渲染+局部笔画级质量审查"的模型引导范式，可同时改善布局连贯性、重建保真度、表征紧凑性与计算效率。

Abstract: Stroke-based rendering is often optimized as a pixel-level reconstruction task. This over-reliance on global error reduction not only degrades strokes into fragmented curves due to a lack of structured planning, but also allows locally defective primitives to be concealed by overlapping layers. To address these issues, we introduce a model-guided framework for stroke-audited rendering. Our framework uses Quadratic Bezier Splatting to analytically model continuous curved strokes with efficient differentiable alpha compositing. Built on this renderer, a feedforward multi-scale stroke decoder provides a structural initialization with learned stroke-scale allocation, while test-time stroke optimization supplies the reconstruction capacity needed for high-fidelity refinement. We further propose a single-stroke audit mechanism that explicitly evaluates local stroke quality, preventing defective primitives from surviving optimization through layer-wise compensation. Compared with existing methods, our approach achieves coherent layouts, improves PSNR by up to 5.46 dB, yields a 16% more compact representation, and accelerates combined renderer forward and backward computation by a factor of 220.3.

</details>


### [98] [Permute-then-Adapt: Weak-to-Strong Contrastive Image-Text Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10714)
*Jinhao Li,Sarah Erfani,Lei Feng,Guangrui Li,James Bailey,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Permute-then-Adapt (PTA)框架，利用弱模型估计随机配对参考分布，以统计校准方式识别图像-文本对齐中被误判为负样本的"漏标正例"，并通过多正例对比目标训练，在目标识别、跨模态检索和数据效率上优于启发式软标签方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等图像-文本对齐模型以对比学习训练，将未配对样本视为严格负样本，这会惩罚语义相关对而引入噪声。现有解决方案依赖缺乏统计依据、且对数据集特定噪声分布不敏感的启发式相似度阈值。

Method: PTA借用高效弱模型识别漏标正例，并在条件置换参考下控制Type-I错误（至校准近似误差）。具体地，从弱教师模型的相似度分数估计随机配对参考分布，以由α确定的上尾为准则筛选辅助正例；随后用单一多正例对比目标训练——每个锚点最大化其分配给正例集的总概率质量。弱模型的离线校准开销可忽略。

Result: 在目标识别、跨模态检索和数据效率实验中，PTA较启发式软标签方法取得提升。代码开源：https://github.com/JinhaoLee/Permute-then-Adapt。

Conclusion: PTA以统计校准替代启发式阈值，将漏标正例选择建立在可控制的Type-I错误之上，训练开销低且在多任务上有效，是一种有原则的对比学习正例扩展方案。

Abstract: Image-text alignment models such as CLIP are typically trained with contrastive learning, where unpaired examples are treated as strict negatives. While this assumption introduces noise by penalising semantically related pairs, existing solutions often rely on heuristic similarity thresholds that lack statistical grounding and sensitivity to dataset-specific noise distributions. In this paper, we propose Permute-then-Adapt (PTA), a framework that leverages efficient weak models to identify missed positives while controlling Type-I error relative to a conditional permutation reference, up to calibration approximation error. Unlike standard distillation or arbitrary thresholding, PTA estimates a random-pairing reference distribution from the weak teacher's similarity scores and uses its upper tail, determined by $α$, to select auxiliary positives. The calibrated positive set is then trained against by a single multi-positive contrastive objective: each anchor maximises the total probability mass it assigns to its positive set. Offline calibration with the weak model adds negligible training overhead. Experiments show gains over heuristic soft-label approaches in object recognition, cross-modal retrieval, and data efficiency. Project repository: https://github.com/JinhaoLee/Permute-then-Adapt.

</details>


### [99] [Disentangled Stylized Image Generation via IP-Cross-Attention Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10717)
*Beibei Dong,Yueming Lyu,Yue Jiang,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于IP交叉注意力重排（IP-cross-attention rearrangement）的解耦风格化图像生成方法，并借鉴无分类器引导提出负内容引导（negative content guidance）以减少内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散风格化方法存在三方面问题：1）扩散生成为迭代过程，基于循环一致性学习隐式风格表示代价高且不适配扩散，即便LoRA类方法亦然；2）扩散潜空间中属性表征相互耦合，解耦风格困难，提取风格属性时易出现内容泄漏；3）风格难以用自然语言表达，文本提示或文本反转对风格的刻画不足。

Method: 基于实验观察，通过IP交叉注意力重排实现解耦的风格化图像生成；并受无分类器引导启发，提出负内容引导以抑制内容泄漏。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅声称可缓解内容泄漏并实现解耦风格化生成（缺少指标与实验数据）。

Conclusion: 将风格解耦从隐式表示学习转向注意力机制层面的重排，为扩散模型风格化生成提供了一条低成本、免训练式循环一致性的技术路径。

Abstract: With a solid theoretical foundation and pretraining on large-scale datasets, diffusion-based methods have achieved controllable generation leveraging text and image conditions, and generating excellent stylized images is possible using diffusion models. However, previous methods face significant challenges. First, because diffusion generation is iterative, the common approach of learning implicit style representations via cycle-consistency strategies is too costly and not well-suited to diffusion, even for LoRA-based methods. Second, decoupling style is difficult in the latent space of diffusion models. Representations of attributes are coupled in the latent space of diffusion models; thus, extracting style attributes with diffusion models usually suffers from the issue of content leakage. Furthermore, because style is hard to represent in natural language, capturing style using text prompts or textual inversion is deficient. In this paper, we propose a method for achieving disentangled stylized image generation through IP-cross-attention rearrangement, based on our experimental observations. We also propose negative content guidance, inspired by classifier-free guidance, to reduce content leakage in stylized image generation.

</details>


### [100] [Locating and Repairing Domain Shift in VLM Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10720)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Michael Alexander Riegler,Guandong Xu,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将 VLM 驾驶规划器的跨城市域偏移重新刻画为“模态×层”的二维张量,并提出 locate-then-repair 框架 MoLaRx,在多个模型与基准上取得最低跨城轨迹误差差距,且参数更少、延迟更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将 VLM 规划器的跨城市域偏移视为单一量,采用统一对齐或单点编辑处理。作者对此提出质疑:偏移在模态与层上是结构化的,匹配该结构的“先定位后修复”应优于所有统一目标基线。

Method: 通过 activation patching 分析发现两条规律:(F1) 模态轴——图像与文本 token 在各 VLM 中呈现不同偏移模式;(F2) 层轴——层间偏移划分依赖架构但对数据集稳定。据此提出 MoLaRx:用因果重要性分数(CIS)估计模态-层域偏移张量并划分功能区域,再由 CISR 对每个区域施加匹配算子(MMD / LoRA / 冻结),组合随架构自动适配。

Result: 在三种架构不同的 VLM(LFM2-VL-1.6B、Qwen2-VL-2B、PaliGemma2-3B)与两个跨城基准(nuScenes、Argoverse 2)的六组主表设置中,MoLaRx 均取得最低跨城轨迹误差差距,可训练参数减少 50-60%,推理延迟降低 8.4-10.9 ms;而统一 MMD 适配在六组设置中全部失效。

Conclusion: 域偏移是模态与层维度上的结构化现象,结构引导的选择性修复显著优于统一目标适配,验证了“只在哪里偏移,就在哪里修复”的原则及其效率优势。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted as end-to-end driving planners, yet they suffer from cross-city domain shift. Existing methods treat this shift as a single quantity addressed by uniform alignment or single-site editing. We challenge this view on two counts: (i) domain shift in VLM trajectory planners is inherently structured across modality and layer, and (ii) a locate-then-repair recipe matched to this structure outperforms every uniform-objective baseline. We analyse domain shift via activation patching and uncover two regularities. (F1) Modality axis. Image and text tokens exhibit distinct shift patterns in each VLM. (F2) Layer axis. The layer-wise shift partition is architecture-dependent and dataset-stable. These findings recast domain shift as a two-dimensional tensor indexed by modality (image vs. text tokens) and layer, motivating a simple principle: locate where shift occurs, and repair only there. We instantiate this principle in MoLaRx (Modality-Layer Repair), a locate-then-repair framework. The Causal Importance Score (CIS) estimates the domain shift as a modality-layer tensor and partitions the layers into functional zones. CIS-Guided Selective Repair (CISR) then applies a matched operator (Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) / low-rank adaptation (LoRA) / freezing) to repair each zone, with the composition adapting automatically to each architecture. We evaluate MoLaRx on three architecturally distinct VLMs (LFM2-VL-1.6B, Qwen2-VL-2B, and PaliGemma2-3B) across two cross-city benchmarks (nuScenes and Argoverse 2). MoLaRx attains the lowest cross-city trajectory-error gap among adaptation methods in all six main-table settings, while using 50-60% fewer trainable parameters and 8.4-10.9 ms lower inference latency. In contrast, uniform MMD-only adaptation collapses in all six settings, highlighting the importance of structure-guided repair.

</details>


### [101] [3D Segmentation in the Clinic: A Grand Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10728)
*Bram van Ginneken,Tobias Heimann,Martin Styner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在2007年MICCAI会议上举办的肝脏CT分割与脑MRI尾状核分割竞赛的组织设置，包括竞赛动机、训练与测试数据集及评分系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织该分割竞赛旨在推动自动与半自动分割方法的对比与评估，为分割算法提供统一的训练/测试数据和客观评分标准。

Method: 以2007年MICCAI会议研讨会的形式开展竞赛，设置两项分割任务（CT肝脏提取、脑MRI尾状核提取），描述各自的训练与测试数据集，并构建用于评价分割结果的评分系统。

Result: 论文呈现了竞赛的完整设置框架：竞赛动机、两任务的数据集说明以及统一的分割评价评分体系。

Conclusion: 该竞赛为医学图像分割方法提供了标准化的数据与量化评估平台，有助于推进自动/半自动分割技术的可比较研究。

Abstract: This paper describes the set-up of a segmentation competition for automatic and semi-automatic extraction of the liver from computed tomography scans and the caudate nucleus from brain MRI data.
