This competition was held in the form of a workshop at the 2007 Medical Image Computing and Computer Assisted Intervention conference. The rationale for organizing the competition is discussed, the training and test data sets for both segmentation tasks are described and the scoring system used to evaluate the segmentation is presented.

</details>


### [102] [LIFT: Local Metric Alignment and Foundation-guided Propagation for Few-shot Depth Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10729)
*Xiang Li,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LIFT框架，利用冻结的单目深度估计基础模型的深度先验与特征先验完成少样本深度补全，在NYUv2和KITTI-DC上取得少样本SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本深度补全需在标注成本与模型域适应之间取得平衡；而预训练MDE基础模型蕴含丰富的深度与特征先验，尚未被充分利用。

Method: 三步式框架：1）在粗网格上估计局部尺度与平移参数并插值为平滑稠密的参数场，得到空间自适应的度量深度初始化；2）以基础模型特征为引导，通过多尺度深度传播逐步精化；3）轻量残差解码器融合多层基础特征与传播修正，输出最终度量深度。

Result: 在NYUv2与KITTI-DC上实验表明该方法达到少样本场景下的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 冻结MDE基础模型的深度先验与特征先验可有效迁移至少样本深度补全，参数场初始化+多尺度传播+残差解码的组合范式有效。

Abstract: Few-shot depth completion aims to recover dense metric depth from sparse measurements using only a small number of target-domain annotations, offering a favorable balance between annotation cost and model adaptation. We propose LIFT, a framework that harnesses both depth and feature priors from a frozen pretrained monocular depth estimation (MDE) foundation model for few-shot depth completion. Specifically, LIFT first estimates local scale and shift parameters on a coarse grid and interpolates them into smooth, dense parameter fields, providing a spatially adaptive metric depth initialization. It then progressively refines this initialization through multi-scale depth propagation guided by foundation features. Finally, a lightweight residual decoder combines multi-level foundation features with propagation correction to refine the metric depth. Extensive experiments on NYUv2 and KITTI-DC demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art few-shot performance. The source code will soon be released.

</details>


### [103] [Tiny Evidence Region Localization with Selective Contrastive On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10734)
*Yujuan Pang,Wenhao Xu,Yinglei Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性正负样本在线策略自蒸馏（SPN-OPSD），同时利用证据区域与背景区域监督，并以 token 级选择性蒸馏提升多模态大模型在细粒度视觉任务中的抗干扰与证据关注能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在需要极小图像区域证据的细粒度视觉任务上仍受限；已有方法只用证据相关区域训练，忽视大范围背景区域的干扰影响，导致模型抗无关区域干扰能力不足。

Method: SPN-OPSD：在在线策略自蒸馏框架中同时构造证据视角与背景视角的正、负监督信号，使模型学会区分证据与无关背景；并采用选择性 token 级蒸馏，仅对信息量更高的 token 施加监督，从而提供更有效的指导。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-2B 上，V* Bench、ZoomBench、MME-RealWorld-Lite 准确率分别由 76.44%、45.92%、57.43% 提升至 84.82%、54.56%、59.56%；Qwen3.5-0.8B 上观察到类似增益。

Conclusion: 引入背景区域作为负向/对比监督并结合选择性 token 级蒸馏，可有效增强模型对无关区域的抗干扰能力和对证据区域的注意力，是提升细粒度视觉任务性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made progress in general visual tasks. However, limitations remain in fine-grained visual tasks requiring evidence from minute image regions. Existing methods primarily train models using evidence-related regions while overlooking the influence of extensive background regions, resulting in a limited ability to resist interference from irrelevant regions. We propose selective positive-negative on-policy self-distillation (SPN-OPSD), which considers both evidence and background regions. Supervision from both views improves the model's ability to resist interference from irrelevant regions. Furthermore, selective token-level distillation provides more informative supervision, improving the model's ability to attend to evidence regions in Fine-Grained Visual Tasks. On Qwen3.5-2B, SPN-OPSD improves accuracy on V* Bench, ZoomBench, and MME-RealWorld-Lite from 76.44%, 45.92%, and 57.43% to 84.82%, 54.56%, and 59.56%, respectively. Similar gains are also observed on Qwen3.5-0.8B.

</details>


### [104] [Writer-independent handedness identification from offline Bangla handwriting under controlled acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10744)
*Tanzim Hasan Prappo,Sayeed Hassan,Sayem Ali,Sukhendra Nath Dhar,M. Shahidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在控制采集条件下，基于墨迹梯度描述子可从离线孟加拉文手写中识别未见书写者的利手，平衡准确率77.4%，但需扣除笔画明暗等光度线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利手是法证文件检验与书写者刻画中的软生物特征，但既有离线手写研究报告的准确率常未将书写者、文本与图像采集因素分离，模型可能识别的是书写者身份或采集条件而非书写手；孟加拉文缺乏此类受控证据。

Method: 构建BanglaHand数据集（42名书写者，左右手各21人），每人书写6个共享提示文本与2段自选文本，采集与摄影流程受控；评估时留出每个测试书写者的全部图像，采用整书写者置换检验与光度控制。比较墨迹梯度描述子、冻结ImageNet嵌入与纯形状特征，并做事后分析考察笔画中心暗度（笔压）与光照漂移。

Result: 墨迹梯度描述子对未见书写者利手识别达77.4%平衡准确率（书写者bootstrap 95%区间67.7–86.5%，p=0.001）；冻结ImageNet嵌入为69.5–72.0%。事后分析显示等笔画宽度下右手书写者笔画中心更暗，符合更高笔压且不能由光照漂移解释。回归剔除墨密度与亮度后描述子仍达64.5%（p=0.027），而仅形状特征较弱（60.8%，p=0.090）。

Conclusion: 在受控采集下，离线孟加拉文手写中的利手可被识别；光度控制对正确解释此类线索至关重要，因为部分判别信号来自笔压相关的笔画明暗而非书写形状。

Abstract: Handedness is a soft-biometric cue for forensic document examination and writer profiling, yet reported accuracies from offline handwriting rarely keep writers, texts and image acquisition apart, so models may recognise the writer or capture conditions rather than the writing hand. Such controlled evidence is missing for Bangla. Using our BanglaHand dataset of 42 writers (21 left- and 21 right-handed), who each wrote six shared prompts and two own texts under controlled collection and photography, we evaluate handedness identification with all images of each test writer held out, whole-writer permutation tests and photometric controls. An ink-gradient descriptor identified the handedness of unseen writers with 77.4% balanced accuracy (95% writer-bootstrap interval 67.7-86.5%; p=0.001) and frozen ImageNet embeddings with 69.5-72.0%. Part of this signal is stroke darkness: in a post hoc analysis, right-handed writers' strokes were darker at their centres for equal stroke width, consistent with higher pen pressure and not explained by lighting drift. With ink density and brightness regressed out, the descriptor still reached 64.5% (p=0.027), whereas writing-only shape features were weaker (60.8%; p=0.090). Handedness is thus identifiable from offline Bangla handwriting under controlled acquisition, and photometric controls are essential to interpret such cues.

</details>


### [105] [RoadState: A Domain-Prior-Enhanced Vision-Language System for Industrial-Grade Lane-Level Map Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10746)
*Deguo Xia,Zihan Li,Jiaqi Wang,Dong Xie,Xiyan Liu,Liuhui Zhang,Jizhou Huang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang,Chen Zhu,Liwei Qian,Xin Pei,Shuanglong Li,Hanmeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生产级视觉语言系统 RoadState，将序列道路观测转为结构化车道级语义；核心模型 RoadState-VLM 以自回归状态估计建模，引入 Geometric Chain-of-Thought 图先验，实现 94.33% 平均准确率并在百度地图 360+ 城市部署，降低 30% 生产成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道级地图更新需在遮挡、模糊车道线、临时障碍与视角变化下保持时序一致、物理合理且可审计的道路语义，现有 VLM 道路场景理解与大规模生产线之间存在鸿沟。

Method: RoadState-VLM 将道路场景理解形式化为自回归状态估计：由前一时刻观测与符号状态、当前观测共同预测当前结构化道路状态；Geo-CoT 以语义依赖有向无环图作为领域先验提升互依道路状态维度的语义一致性与推理保真度；并采用级联路由流水线，常规样本交由轻量模型、复杂样本交由 RoadState-VLM，配合人在回路审计。

Result: 真实数据集上六类道路状态维度平均准确率 94.33%，优于最强微调 VLM 基线；消融实验验证 Geo-CoT 与时序转移上下文的互补有效性；2025 年 3 月部署以来支持 360+ 城市车道级更新，整体生产成本降低 30%，压力测试下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 领域先验增强推理、结构化状态接口、选择性路由与人工质检相结合，可弥合 VLM 道路场景理解与大规模车道级地图更新之间的差距。

Abstract: Lane-level map updating requires temporally consistent, physically valid, and auditable road-scene semantics despite occlusions, ambiguous lane markings, temporary obstacles, and viewpoint changes. We present RoadState, a production-deployed vision-language system that transforms sequential road-scene observations into structured lane-level semantics for Baidu Maps' map-updating pipeline. Its core model, RoadState-VLM, formulates road-scene understanding as autoregressive state estimation, predicting the current structured road state from the previous observation and symbolic state together with the current observation. We introduce Geometric Chain-of-Thought (Geo-CoT), which uses a directed acyclic graph of semantic dependencies as a domain prior to improve semantic consistency and reasoning fidelity across interdependent road-state dimensions. Extensive experiments on a real-world dataset show that RoadState-VLM achieves 94.33% mean accuracy across six road-state dimensions, outperforming the strongest fine-tuned VLM baseline. Ablation studies further demonstrate the complementary effectiveness of Geo-CoT and temporal transition context. A cascaded routing pipeline directs routine cases to lightweight models while reserving complex cases for RoadState-VLM. Since its deployment in March 2025, RoadState has supported lane-level map updating across more than 360 cities and reduced overall production costs by 30%. Together with human-in-the-loop auditing, the system maintains robust performance under stress-test conditions. These results demonstrate how domain-prior-enhanced reasoning, structured state interfaces, selective routing, and human quality control can bridge the gap between VLM-based road-scene understanding and large-scale lane-level map updating.

</details>


### [106] [Unified Framework with Convex-Concave Decomposition and Sparse Topological Learning for Skeleton-Based Clinical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10758)
*Quang-Anh N. D.,Duc Pham Minh,Minh-Anh Nguyen,Hien Quang Kha,Manh Hung Ha,Minhhuy Le,Oscal Tzyh-Chiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CC2-STG多流模型，通过凸凹距离函数分解、稀疏正则化近端优化与自适应拓扑，实现基于骨骼的病理动作康复评估，在KiMoRe和UIPRMD上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨骼的康复评估存在三方面局限：固定拓扑无法建模代偿性动作模式；对所有关节统一处理，忽视临床重要的偏差；缺乏几何层面的理解。

Method: CC2-STG为多流框架，融合几何分解、稀疏特征学习与自适应拓扑：将关节关系分解为互补的凸-凹距离函数，以刻画局部与全局运动特征；采用带近端优化的稀疏正则化筛选具有临床意义的几何特征；并通过多尺度拓扑分析捕捉病理动作中的非解剖性关节依赖。

Result: 在KiMoRe与UIPRMD数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性，在临床病理动作评估任务上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 几何分解、稀疏特征选择与自适应拓扑的结合可有效克服固定拓扑与均匀关节处理的局限，提升病理动作康复评估的准确性与临床可解释性。

Abstract: Skeleton-based rehabilitation assessment faces fundamental limitations of fixed topologies which cannot model compensatory patterns, uniform joint treatments overlook clinically important deviations, and lack of geometric understand- ing. We propose Concave-Convex Decomposition Sparse Topological Graph Convolutional Network (CC2-STG), a multi-stream model that combines geo- metric decomposition, sparse feature learning, and adaptive topology. Our framework decomposes joint relationships into complementary convex-concave distance functions to model both local and global movement characteristics, with sparse regularization with proximal optimization selecting clinically relevant geo- metric features, while multi-scale topological analysis captures non-anatomical joint dependencies in pathological movements. Experiments on the KiMoRe and UIPRMD datasets validate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving state-of-the-art performance on clinical pathological movement assessment.

</details>


### [107] [BAPLe++: Cross-Model Transferability and Prompt-Level Defense of Backdoors in Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10764)
*Ufaq Khan,Umair Nawaz,Satyajit Tourani,Awais Rauf,Fahad Shamshad,Asif Hanif,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Muhammad Haris Khan,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BAPLe++，首次系统研究提示学习后门在医疗基础模型间的跨模型可迁移性，并提出免重训练主干的对策DePLe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Med-FM通过提示学习适配临床任务，已有工作表明联合优化提示与不可感知噪声触发器可植入后门；由于提示紧凑易共享，需回答两个问题：被植入后门的提示在另一Med-FM上是否仍有效，以及能否在不重训练主干的情况下修复。

Method: BAPLe++包含两部分：(1) 跨模型迁移性研究，提出CAD（干净精度差）与BAD（后门精度差）两个指标量化迁移；(2) DePLe防御，将受感染提示作为固定锚点，在少量干净数据上学习新提示，既保留干净任务性能，又偏离被污染的嵌入区域。

Result: 在四个Med-FM与六个胸部X光和病理数据集上，后门可跨模型迁移，但呈非对称性且依赖数据集；与四种免训练输入变换相比，DePLe更稳定地降低后门精度，同时保持甚至在若干设定下提升干净精度。

Conclusion: 提示学习后门具有跨Med-FM的迁移风险，提示共享构成实际威胁；基于提示重学习的DePLe可在不重训练主干的条件下有效缓解该风险，是较为一致的防御方案。

Abstract: Medical foundation models (Med-FMs) are increasingly adapted to clinical tasks through prompt learning, which tunes a small set of prompt tokens while the backbone remains frozen. Recent work has shown that this efficiency introduces a vulnerability: jointly optimizing learnable prompts and an imperceptible noise trigger during few-shot adaptation implants a backdoor that leaves clean performance intact but steers triggered inputs to an attacker-chosen class. Since prompts are compact and readily shared, this raises two questions: whether a backdoored prompt remains effective on a different Med-FM, and whether it can be repaired without retraining the backbone. We address both in BAPLe++. First, we present the first study of cross-model transferability of prompt-based backdoors, quantified by two proposed metrics, Clean Accuracy Difference (CAD) and Backdoor Accuracy Difference (BAD). Second, we propose DePLe (Defense via Prompt Learning), which uses the infected prompt as a fixed anchor and learns a new prompt on a small clean set that preserves the clean task while diverging from the compromised embedding region. Experiments on four Med-FMs and six chest X-ray and histopathology datasets show that backdoors transfer across models, but asymmetrically and in a dataset-dependent manner. Compared with four training-free input transformations, DePLe reduces backdoor accuracy more consistently while preserving, and in several settings improving, clean accuracy. Our code can be accessed on https://github.com/Ufaqkhan/Baple-Plus-Plus

</details>


### [108] [How reliable is an embedding of the seabed? Repeat-look measurement of frozen encoders on synthetic aperture sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10770)
*Harold Horsley,Oscar Pizarro,Hayat Rajani,Morten Omholt Alver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将海底合成孔径声呐(SAS)图像的表示评估视为"测量可靠性"问题，揭示标准单视指标稳定的嵌入在重复观测下可靠性很差，并主张将重复观测可靠性纳入海底表示评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督预训练编码器在海底声呐图像上采用"每目标一嵌入"的评估协议，但实际中同一海底目标patch会在重叠航次的另一视角被重复成像，每次嵌入只是一次测量。标准协议无法说明这些测量的一致性，也无法区分嵌入散布来自patch本身还是采集几何。

Method: 使用SAS测量数据，评估冻结的自然图像编码器(DINOv3 ViT-S/16)嵌入的重复观测可靠性；采用距离基组内相关系数(dbICC)、最近邻与聚类的跨视一致性、有效秩、聚类与珊瑚线性探针等标准单视指标；用泛化理论的方差成分分解量化视角采集几何的贡献；并对声呐预训练编码器(BenthicDINO)与经典SAS纹理描述子做对照。

Result: 标准单视指标(有效秩、聚类、珊瑚线性探针)对视角均稳定，探针AUC达0.93；但同一嵌入的重复观测可靠性差，dbICC仅0.20，最近邻与聚类跨视几乎不一致。探针学到的是一个稳定方向，其ICC为0.60，是全嵌入的3.0倍。方差成分显示细胞单元内散布中不到十分之一归于视角采集几何，其余为单元特异且需几何匹配的重复航次才能分离。BenthicDINO与经典SAS纹理描述子表现相同模式。

Conclusion: 在该数据集与所测表示上，依赖patch身份的应用(如跨航次比较、检索)失败，而栖息地类别层面应用(如珊瑚分类)不受影响。由于标准指标未能检测此问题，作者主张任何海底表示评估都应附带重复观测可靠性指标。

Abstract: Pretrained encoders from self-supervised representation learning are increasingly applied to sonar imagery of the seafloor, where they are evaluated with protocols that take one embedding per target. A target patch of seabed, by contrast, is re-imaged from an alternate look on overlapping survey passes, so each embedding is one measurement of that target. Standard protocols cannot say how consistent those measurements are, or how much of their spread belongs to the patch and how much to the acquisition geometry. We treat this as a measurement-reliability problem. Using a synthetic-aperture sonar (SAS) survey, we measure the repeat-look reliability of embeddings from a frozen natural-image encoder (DINOv3 ViT-S/16). Standard single-look metrics, effective rank, clustering and a linear probe for coral, are all stable to the look, and the probe performs well (AUC 0.93). Yet the repeat-look reliability of the same embeddings is poor, with a distance-based intraclass correlation (dbICC) of 0.20 and nearest neighbours and clusters that barely agree across looks. This contradiction resolves as we find the probe learns a stable direction, whose ICC of 0.60 is 3.0 times that of the full embedding. Variance components, calculated from generalizability theory, attribute under a tenth of the within-cell spread to look acquisition geometry. The rest is cell-specific and cannot be separated without repeat passes at matched geometry. A sonar-pretrained encoder (BenthicDINO) and a classical SAS texture descriptor show the same pattern. For this dataset and the tested representations, uses that depend on patch identity fail, such as cross-pass comparison and retrieval, while uses at the level of habitat type (e.g. coral classification) do not. Because the standard metrics did not detect this, we argue that repeat-look reliability should accompany any evaluation of representations of the seafloor.

</details>


### [109] [WorldFoundry: Towards Unified Infrastructure for World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10771)
*Juanxi Tian,Jiadong Hou,Baiang Li,Xin Lin,Zhengbo Jiao,Weijia Zhang,Yeying Jin,Truong Nguyen,Haitian Zheng,Zhifei Zhang,Jianfei Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Felix Heide,Wei Xiong,Enze Xie,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式世界模型的形式化定义与智能导向评测框架，并发布开源基础设施 WorldFoundry，统一视频、3D/4D、具身动作与交互式世界系统的推理与评测，实现可复现、可溯源的世界建模能力比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型使"合成观测"与"模拟环境"界限模糊，但视觉逼真度不等于成功世界建模：输出可能缺乏持久状态、物理动力学或对干预的因果一致响应。领域缺少统一概念定义与可复现、可溯源的评测基础设施，难以在异构系统间比较持久、可控的动力学建模能力。

Method: 将生成式世界模型定义为预测性动力系统：维护以历史为条件的持久环境表征，响应动作/干预更新该表征，并生成反映观测与反事实干预结果的时间一致轨迹。据此构建涵盖感知落地、持久状态建模、可控变换、动力学感知预测与闭环交互的智能导向框架；实现 WorldFoundry 开源基础设施，统一模型与基准发现、运行时与资产准备、执行编排、产物管理与交互可视化，并以模态专用适配器保留原生输入、执行语义、输出表示与评测协议；通过显式清单、持久产物契约与证据感知评分卡，将执行就绪度与评测主张分离，使主张可溯源至支撑产物。

Result: 目录收录 270 个模型条目、74 个基准与 62 个注册指标条目，就绪度与执行证据分别记录；支持跨异构系统对持久、可控动力学的可复现比较。

Conclusion: WorldFoundry 推动该领域从感知合成走向可度量的"世界智能"，为生成式世界模型的推理与评测提供可复现、可审计的基础设施。

Abstract: Visual generative models increasingly blur the line between synthesizing observations and simulating environments. Yet visual plausibility alone does not constitute successful world modeling: compelling outputs may lack persistent state, physical dynamics, or causally consistent responses to interactions. We define a generative world model as a predictive dynamical system that maintains a persistent, history-conditioned representation of an environment, updates that representation in response to actions or interventions, and generates temporally coherent trajectories that reflect outcomes under observed and counterfactual interventions. From this definition, we derive an intelligence-oriented framework spanning perceptual grounding, persistent state modeling, controllable transformation, dynamics-aware prediction, and closed-loop interaction. We present WorldFoundry, open-source infrastructure for reproducible inference and evaluation across video, 3D and 4D, embodied action, and interactive world systems. WorldFoundry unifies model and benchmark discovery, runtime and asset preparation, execution orchestration, artifact management, and interactive visualization, while modality-specific adapters preserve native inputs, execution semantics, output representations, and evaluation protocols. Explicit manifests, durable artifact contracts, and evidence-aware scorecards separate execution readiness from evaluative claims, making each claim traceable to its supporting artifacts. The catalog contains 270 model entries, 74 benchmarks, and 62 registered metric entries, with readiness and execution evidence recorded separately. WorldFoundry enables reproducible comparison of persistent, controllable dynamics across heterogeneous systems, advancing the field from perceptual synthesis toward measurable world intelligence.

</details>


### [110] [Same Measurements, Different Velocity Risk: Complementary Rolling-Shutter Readouts under Clock Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10774)
*Lunshuo Tian,Dongheng Liu,Mingwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在相同测量预算下，曝光时序不确定的卷帘相机装配中，重复读出与互补读出的速度估计最坏情况风险存在本质差异：前者至少为σρ_eff量级，后者极小极大风险为σ²量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机装配的测量预算相同，但曝光时序不确定时速度估计速率不同；作者希望建立这一区别，并在场景点、辅助位姿与时钟均未知的卷帘重建设定下给出严格的理论刻画。

Method: 构建两个标定移动锚点、未知场景点和独立未知辅助位姿与时钟的卷帘重建模型；用有限物理补偿路径导出下界，用非线性速度逆映射证明互补读出的稳定性；分析使两类设计均正则的安装构型并量化度量不确定性带来的风险下限。

Result: 在分离横向装配附近的固定局部物理类上，重复读出的最坏情况平方速度风险至少为σρ_eff阶，互补读出为σ²阶极小极大风险（ρ_eff>0固定，σ→0）；并给出全参数域刻画（独立6ms时钟）、配对噪声与深度偏移干预下的域内重建测试，以及光栅图像形成与标定敏感性研究。

Conclusion: 曝光时序不确定性下，读出设计（重复 vs 互补）决定了速度估计的统计速率；互补读出可达参数速率，重复读出受固定时钟位移导致的σρ_eff风险地板限制。

Abstract: Camera rigs with identical measurement budgets can have different velocity estimation rates when exposure timing is uncertain. We establish this distinction for rolling-shutter reconstruction with two calibrated moving anchors, unknown scene points, and independently unknown auxiliary poses and clocks. On the same fixed local physical class near a separated lateral rig, repeated readouts have worst-case squared velocity risk at least of order $σρ_{\mathrm{eff}}$, whereas complementary readouts have minimax risk of order $σ^2$, for a fixed admissible clock displacement $ρ_{\mathrm{eff}}>0$ as the noise standard deviation $σ$ tends to zero. A finite physical compensation path gives the lower bound; a nonlinear velocity inverse proves complementary stability. All motion, structure, pose, and clock nuisances remain unknown. We identify mounting configurations in which both designs become regular and quantify the risk floor induced by metric uncertainty. We quantify full-parameter domains with independent 6-ms clocks and test in-domain reconstruction under paired noise and a depth-offset intervention. Raster studies examine image formation and calibration sensitivity.

</details>


### [111] [STAR-VF: Spatio-Temporal Aware Representation Condensation for Data-Efficient Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10811)
*Yuchen Xian,Yunqiu Xu,Guosheng Lin,Yang He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAR-VF，首个面向视频融合的数据集蒸馏框架，将训练集压缩为可学习的时空表征，仅用约1/16时序训练数据即可达到接近全量训练的融合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频融合需同时学习跨模态互补性与时序依赖，通常依赖大规模高质量视频数据集，存储与反复优化的开销高昂，因此亟需数据高效的学习范式；作者从数据集蒸馏视角重新审视视频融合问题。

Method: 将原始训练集蒸馏为紧凑的可学习时空表征，解码为合成视频片段，并用冻结代理模型的特征级监督进行优化（无需GT融合视频）；融合模型采用无光流的帧间时序建模与查询驱动的帧内聚合，在表征空间实现直接时序交互与自适应信息选择。

Result: 在红外-可见光、多曝光、多聚焦与医学视频融合四类基准上，仅用约1/16的原始时序训练集即达到与全量训练方法相当的融合质量；消融验证了学习式蒸馏与无光流时序建模的贡献，并证明蒸馏监督在不同代理编码器与下游融合模型间的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据集蒸馏可有效缓解视频融合的数据依赖，时空表征压缩与表征空间时序建模相结合，为实现数据高效的视频融合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video fusion aims to integrate complementary cues from heterogeneous sources, producing visually informative videos with coherent spatio-temporal structures. Jointly learning cross-modal complementarity and temporal dependencies typically relies on large-scale, high-quality video datasets, whose storage and repeated use during optimization incur substantial costs, making data-efficient learning highly desirable. To address this challenge, we rethink video fusion from the perspective of dataset condensation and propose STAR-VF, the first framework that condenses spatio-temporal representations for data-efficient video fusion. STAR-VF condenses the original training set into a compact set of learnable spatio-temporal representations, which are decoded into synthetic video clips and optimized using feature-level supervision provided by a frozen proxy model. The resulting condensed data preserves fusion-relevant spatial and temporal cues without requiring ground-truth fused videos as supervision. To adapt fusion learning to condensed representations, we equip the final fusion model with flow-free inter-frame temporal modeling and query-driven intra-frame aggregation, enabling direct temporal interaction and adaptive selection of fusion-relevant information in representation space. Extensive experiments on infrared-visible, multi-exposure, multi-focus, and medical video fusion benchmarks demonstrate that STAR-VF, trained with only approximately 1/16 of the original temporal training set, achieves fusion quality comparable to methods trained on the full data. Further analyses validate the contributions of learned condensation and flow-free temporal modeling, and demonstrate the robustness of the condensed supervision across different proxy encoders and downstream fusion models. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/STAR-VF-Project-Page/.

</details>


### [112] [AmodalBox: Automated 3D Bounding Box Annotation under Partial Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10821)
*In-Jae Lee,Namtae Kim,Robert Exley,Kwonyoung Ryu,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自动标注框架，利用图像生成的3D资产作为非模态几何先验，解决部分观测目标的3D框标注问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏驾驶场景中LiDAR点云仅覆盖目标表面小部分，现有开放词汇3D框自动标注方法因线索不足导致定位错误或漏标。

Method: 以图像生成的3D资产作为非模态几何先验；通过输入图像与资产渲染视图建立对应关系，将规范非模态几何与部分观测的世界坐标LiDAR点云稳健对齐；每个实例仅生成单个资产，动态实例在单帧估计一次度量尺度并跨帧传播，静态实例聚合多帧观测以提升时间一致性并降低计算开销。

Result: 基于该标注训练3D检测器，在nuScenes上较强基线提升14.1 mAP；在Waymo Open Dataset上车辆类IoU 0.5下提升12.45 AP。

Conclusion: 图像生成资产提供的非模态几何先验可有效补偿LiDAR部分观测，实现高质量开放词汇3D框自动标注，显著提升下游检测性能。

Abstract: Recent progress in vision foundation models (VFMs) has made automated open-vocabulary 3D bounding box annotation increasingly feasible by jointly leveraging LiDAR point clouds and images. However, partial observations remain a major challenge in sparse driving scenes, where visible LiDAR points often cover only a small fraction of the object's surface and provide insufficient cues for estimating its full spatial extent. As a result, existing methods often produce mislocalized boxes or leave objects unlabeled. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{\Ours{}}, an automated annotation framework that leverages image-generated 3D assets as amodal geometric priors for partially observed objects. By establishing correspondences between input images and rendered views of 3D assets, \Ours{} robustly aligns canonical amodal geometry with partially observed world-space LiDAR point clouds. To improve temporal consistency and reduce computational cost, \Ours{} generates a single asset per instance, estimates its metric scale once at a single frame and propagates it across frames for dynamic instances, and aggregates multi-frame observations for static instances. 3D object detectors trained on annotations from \Ours{} improve performance over strong baselines by 14.1 mAP on the nuScenes dataset and by 12.45 AP at IoU 0.5 for the vehicle class on the Waymo Open Dataset (WOD). Our project page is available at: \href{https://oliver0922.github.io/AmodalBox/}{https://oliver0922.github.io/AmodalBox/}

</details>


### [113] [Fine-resolution land-use segmentation and synthetic image-label augmentation in the Kathmandu Valley](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10847)
*Pranil Parajuli,Sugam Dahal,Kiran Chandra Dahal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了加德满都谷地408幅RGB场景、六类地物的地块级土地利用语料库，揭示随机划分导致严重空间泄漏，并提出改进SegFormer以提升稀有细结构类别分割性能，同时警示合成数据的盲目使用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方快速城市化城市普遍缺乏地块级土地利用数据，而现有分割架构依赖多光谱影像和规划化布局，难以适配仅有RGB、光谱相似度高、类别极不平衡（近100:1）、河流等关键结构仅2–3像素宽的场景。

Method: 发布覆盖202 km²、408幅精细RGB场景的六类手工标注语料库；对比五种架构，并采用严格序列块划分替代随机划分以消除空间泄漏；在SegFormer上加入半分辨率残差细节路径、边界可靠性重加权、拓扑感知损失与存在性均衡采样器（仅增1.9%参数）；并用扩散模型生成配对数据做增强实验，同时在LoveDA外部基准测试。

Result: 随机划分掩盖了大量空间泄漏，严格序列块划分使性能下降最多0.095 mIoU，该惩罚超过架构间差距；改进模型在所有测试折上提升mIoU，增益集中于稀有细结构类别；但两模型在LoveDA上表现相同；扩散生成数据在等算力下反而降低所有架构精度，稀有类别受损最重。

Conclusion: 严格空间划分是评估城市土地利用分割的必要前提；轻量结构改进可有效提升稀有细结构类别；合成数据须经严格评估与筛选后方可用于训练，跨域泛化仍受限。

Abstract: Parcel-scale land-use data is missing for most rapidly urbanizing Global South cities, as current segmentation architectures rely on multispectral imagery and planned layouts. We introduce a hand-annotated six-class land-use corpus covering 202 km$^2$ of Kathmandu Valley across 408 fine-resolution RGB scenes. The dataset is uniquely challenging: RGB spectral similarity is high, class imbalance approaches 100:1, and key structural features like rivers measure just two to three pixels wide at a standard hierarchical decoder's prediction stride. Evaluating five architectures reveals that standard random data splitting conceals massive spatial leakage. Switching to a strict sequence-block split drops performance by up to 0.095 mIoU. This penalty exceeds the performance gap between the architectures themselves. We modified the SegFormer baseline by integrating a half-resolution residual detail path, boundary-reliability reweighting, a topology-aware loss, and a presence-balanced sampler. Adding only 1.9% more parameters, the enhanced architecture improves mean IoU across every test fold, with gains concentrated in rare, thin-structure classes. However, both models achieve identical results on the external LoveDA benchmark. Finally, augmenting with diffusion-generated pairs at matched compute degraded accuracy across all architectures, especially harming rare classes it aimed to improve. This demonstrates that blindly incorporating synthetic data can severely damage performance, highlighting a critical need to rigorously evaluate and filter generated samples before training.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Bit Matching: Orthogonal Watermarks for Collusion-Resistant Image Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10858)
*Zhakshylyk Nurlanov,Tobias Weißberg,Florian Bernard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VectorMark图像指纹水印方案：将用户密钥映射为近正交高维单位向量，用水印方向余弦相似度识别合谋者，并基于球面几何给出Beta零分布的逐密钥虚警率控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均合谋（averaging collusion）是图像指纹溯源的核心威胁：多接收者平均各自的带标记副本可压制个体水印；离散比特串水印尤易受攻击，因为平均会使比特证据趋于模糊判决。

Method: 将用户密钥映射为近正交高维单位向量；K个向量密钥理想平均后，每个合谋者的余弦信号保持约1/√K量级，无关密钥集中于零附近；利用随机方向的球面几何得到余弦相似度的移位Beta零分布，在无水印图像上校准后实现解析式逐密钥虚警率控制；采用神经水印编码器与中心化提取器，结合JND掩蔽及覆盖光度、几何与合谋鲁棒性的渐进式课程训练。

Result: K个向量密钥平均后每名合谋者保留约1/√K的余弦信号，非涉事密钥余弦集中于零附近；理论零分布为移位Beta分布，支持逐密钥解析FPR控制；在MS-COCO、OpenImages与AI生成图像上，VectorMark在光度与几何失真下保持稳健溯源，在评测方法中取得最佳严格全合谋者检测性能，并保持高视觉质量。

Conclusion: 以高维近正交向量替代离散比特串，可将平均合谋从"模糊判决"问题转化为方向（余弦）检测问题，并借助球面几何获得可解析的虚警率控制；配合神经编解码器与课程训练，可在多种失真下实现高视觉质量的多合谋者可靠归因。

Abstract: Image fingerprinting assigns each distributed copy a user-specific watermark for source tracing. Averaging collusion is a central threat: several recipients can average their differently marked copies to suppress each individual mark. Discrete bit-string watermarks are especially vulnerable because averaging drives bit evidence toward ambiguous decisions. We introduce \textbf{VectorMark}, which maps user keys to near-orthogonal high-dimensional unit vectors and identifies colluders by cosine similarity to the extracted watermark direction. Under ideal averaging of $K$ vector keys, the normalized average preserves a cosine signal of order $1/\sqrt{K}$ for each colluder, while unrelated keys remain concentrated near zero. The spherical geometry of random directions gives a shifted-Beta null distribution for cosine similarities, enabling analytic per-key false-positive-rate control after validation on unwatermarked images. VectorMark uses a neural watermark encoder and centered extractor trained with JND masking and a progressive curriculum for photometric, geometric, and collusion robustness. Experiments on MS-COCO, OpenImages, and AI-generated images show that VectorMark maintains robust watermark attribution under photometric and geometric distortions, and achieves the best strict all-colluder detection among evaluated methods, while preserving high visual quality. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/nurlanov-zh/VectorMark

</details>


### [115] [Interactive Person Retrieval with On-the-Fly Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10869)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉反馈交互式行人检索新范式VFI-PR，构建VFI-PEDES数据集与评测协议，并设计VFI-ReID框架，将检索候选产生的视觉反馈转化为后续提问与重排序的证据，持续提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本行人检索假设目击者一次性给出完整描述；交互式行人检索（Inter-PR）通过问答逐步细化，但提问者需查看候选、回答者仅能访问目击侧记忆，无法报告候选相关的视觉差异。现有方法多学习离线提问顺序，候选视觉反馈这一闭环尚未被充分利用。

Method: 提出VFI-PR交互范式：检索到的候选产生视觉反馈，作为后续提问与重排序的证据。构建VFI-PEDES数据集及评测协议，并提出VFI-ReID框架，含动态累积证据检索器（DAR）与检索感知的提问优化，使检索与问题生成随证据状态演化而自适应。

Result: 在VFI-PR各交互阶段，VFI-ReID均稳定提升检索效果，优于现有Inter-PR基线，验证视觉反馈对交互式行人检索的有效性。

Conclusion: 将候选视觉反馈纳入交互闭环可提供互补证据，形成可训练的检索—提问—重排序协同机制，为交互式行人检索提供新范式、数据与基线框架。

Abstract: Traditional text-based person retrieval (TPR) assumes that a witness provides a complete description at once, whereas interactive person retrieval (Inter-PR) refines a partial description through question-answer exchanges. In this setting, the questioner inspects retrieved candidates to decide what evidence to request next, while the answerer sees only the witness-side memory and cannot report candidate-specific visual differences. Existing methods mainly learn offline question orderings, leaving this visual-feedback loop underexplored. To address this limitation, we introduce visual-feedback interactive person retrieval (VFI-PR), a new interactive retrieval paradigm in which retrieved candidates produce visual feedback that becomes evidence for subsequent questioning and re-ranking. To support training and evaluation in this setting, we construct VFI-PEDES, a dataset and evaluation protocol for visual-feedback interaction, and propose VFI-ReID, a framework that learns to use visual feedback during interactive person retrieval. Through the Dynamic Accumulated-evidence Retriever (DAR) and retrieval-aware question optimization, VFI-ReID adapts retrieval and question generation to evolving evidence states, turning visual feedback into complementary evidence for subsequent retrieval. Experiments show that VFI-ReID consistently improves retrieval across VFI-PR interaction stages, demonstrating the utility of visual feedback for interactive person retrieval and outperforming existing Inter-PR baselines.

</details>


### [116] [REDiff-AD: No Training and No Target Masks for Industrial Anomaly Image Generation via Diversity-Driven In-Context MMDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10875)
*Chih-Yu Tsai,Chih-Heng Chang,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REDiff-AD 是一种免训练、免目标掩码的工业异常生成框架，将 Rectified Flow MMDiT 引入异常生成，借助上下文学习把单张参考异常的外观迁移到正常产品图像上，并复用生成过程中的内部注意力实现像素级伪掩码定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业缺陷真实数据稀缺且多样，现有异常生成方法通常需针对产品微调、需目标掩码标注或流程昂贵，难以兼顾真实性与多样性，也难以支撑下游模型训练与大规模数据增广。

Method: 提出 REDiff-AD：以正常图像加一张带掩码的参考异常为输入，用上下文学习完成缺陷外观迁移；引入 Rectified Flow MMDiT 实现少步高效采样；复用生成过程中的内部注意力信号进行异常定位，并结合边缘与颜色线索细化为像素级伪掩码；另设两个免训练轻量模块——自适应参考条件增强缺陷保真度，多样性引导在不额外训练下生成多样缺陷模式。

Result: 实验表明 REDiff-AD 能较好平衡异常生成的真实性与多样性，在下游异常分类、检测与定位任务上取得有竞争力的性能，可支撑大规模异常数据增广与像素级数据构建。

Conclusion: REDiff-AD 是一种简单、高效、有效的异常生成与像素级数据构建方案，无需模型训练与目标掩码，具备良好的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection is challenged by the scarcity and diversity of real defects, leaving insufficient anomalous data to effectively train downstream models. Existing anomaly generation methods often require product-specific fine-tuning, target mask annotation, or costly procedures to achieve realistic and diverse defects. We propose REDiff-AD, a framework that requires neither model training nor target masks and introduces Rectified Flow MMDiT into industrial anomaly generation for efficient sampling with fewer generation steps. Given a normal product image and a single masked reference anomaly, REDiff-AD employs in-context learning to transfer the defect appearance from the reference into the normal image. Internal attention signals generated during this process are further reused to localize the synthesized anomaly, while edge and color cues refine the localization into pixel-level pseudo-masks. Two lightweight modules further improve generation quality: adaptive reference conditioning enhances defect fidelity, while diversity guidance promotes varied defect patterns without additional training. Experiments demonstrate that REDiff-AD can well address the tradeoff between realism and diversity for anomaly pattern generation and achieve competitive downstream anomaly classification, detection, and localization performance. These results show that REDiff-AD is a simple, efficient, and effective solution for large-scale anomaly data augmentation and pixel-level data construction. Github code: https://github.com/efdefe4-droid/REDiff-AD

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Real or Fake: A Dual-Channel Authenticity and Reasoning Protocol for Photographic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10908)
*Xiaoxiao Li,Ruinan Jin,Lili Meng,Miaosen Wang,Athula Balachandran,Pei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出双通道真实性协议2CAP，将GenAI图像操纵分解为相机通道（传感器指纹）与语义通道（场景一致性），统一实现四类真实性分类与带定位的证据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有取证验证把任务简化为"真/假"二分类，混淆了全合成、篡改与AI修饰三类图像，而三者后果差异巨大；因此需要按操纵机理细粒度刻画其可区分痕迹。

Method: 提出两通道分解：相机通道的传感器指纹（被神经处理破坏）与语义通道的场景级一致性（被内容编辑破坏），每类操纵具有独特双通道签名。据此构建2CAP：对比学习训练的相机编码器 + 冻结语义编码器共享骨干；(i) 通过带可学习可靠性权重的交叉注意力融合做四类真实性分类；(ii) 以真实图像为参考，两编码器输出逐通道对齐分数与patch级显著性图，经Observe-Generate-Refine循环作为特权上下文输入冻结VLM，无需取证微调即生成带定位的解释。

Result: 在多源基准上取得最佳分类性能，尤其在现有方法失效的AI修饰检测上表现突出，同时提升了解释质量。

Conclusion: 以通道分解替代二分类范式，可在同一共享骨干上同时支撑细粒度分类与可解释取证证据，并借助冻结VLM免微调获得定位级解释。

Abstract: Forensic verification has converged on a binary "real vs. fake" label that confates fully synthetic, tampered, and AI-retouched images despite their very different consequences. We argue that GenAI manipulations decompose along two complementary channels: a camera channel of sensor fingerprints (destroyed by neural processing), and a semantic channel of scene-level coherence (disrupted by content edits). Each manipulation type leaves a distinctive two-channel signature. We instantiate this taxonomy in 2CAP (2-Channel Authenticity Protocol), pairing a contrastively-trained camera encoder with a frozen semantic encoder to serve two applications on a shared backbone: (i) four-class authenticity classification via cross-attention fusion with learnable reliability weights; and (ii) evidence generation, where an authentic image serves as a reference for a query and the two encoders produce per-channel alignment scores together with a patch-level saliency map. These signals are supplied as privileged context to a frozen Vision-Language Model (VLM) through an Observe-Generate-Refine loop, yielding explanations with localization without forensic fine-tuning. On a multi-source benchmark, 2CAP attains the best classification performance, including strong retouching detection where existing methods fail, and improves explanation quality.

</details>


### [118] [Remembering to Reason: Structured Memory for Scene and Workflow Understanding in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10917)
*Zhiran Zhang,Hu Wang,Yichen Wu,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemSurg：以结构化记忆图存储手术视频历史观测并按时间与实体链接，再经任务自适应检索生成证据支撑提示，提升场景与流程推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术智能辅助依赖手术视频理解，但相似手术视野可能对应不同动作或流程阶段，需回溯先前观测；而逐帧提示（frame-centric prompting）无法提供该历史信息。

Method: 构建结构化记忆图，存储带时间戳的实体类别、位置、运动线索与交互摘要；用时间顺序链接保留近期上下文，用实体关联链接跨过程连接重复观测；任务自适应检索提供证据支撑提示，支持冻结推理与记忆条件监督微调。

Result: 机器人辅助前列腺切除术及两个互补手术基准上，较SurgRAW+GPT-4o提升10.43个百分点；匹配Qwen3-VL-8B微调下记忆条件带来6.52点增益；推理时插件为EndoChat提升7.04点。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆可作为手术视频理解中可复用的流程证据来源；代码将公开。

Abstract: Surgical video understanding is an important foundation for intelligent assistance in robot-assisted surgery. Similar surgical views can reflect different actions or procedural stages. Interpreting these views requires access to earlier observations, yet frame-centric prompting leaves this history unavailable. We introduce MemSurg, a framework that remembers surgical observations before reasoning over them. Its structured memory graph stores timestamped entity categories, locations, motion cues, and interaction summaries. Chronological links preserve recent context, while entity-associated links connect recurring observations across the procedure. Task-adaptive retrieval then supplies evidence-grounded prompts for scene and workflow reasoning, supporting both frozen inference and memory-conditioned supervised fine-tuning. Evaluation on robot-assisted prostatectomy and two complementary surgical benchmarks shows a 10.43-percentage-point gain over SurgRAW with GPT-4o. Memory conditioning adds 6.52 points under matched Qwen3-VL-8B fine-tuning, and an inference-time plug-in adds 7.04 points to EndoChat. These results support structured memory as a reusable source of procedural evidence for surgical video understanding. Our code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [119] [Semantic Synthesis over Brute-Force Search: LLM-Guided Transformation Design for Efficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10940)
*Usha Shrestha,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究用代码大模型合成可执行的图像变换程序以替代传统数据增强搜索，在统一训练协议下比较9种策略，LLM生成的变换程序在CIFAR-10/100和SVHN上全面领先，且候选评估成本大幅降低；同时揭示了一轮次低预算评估与20轮次结果相关性极不稳定（0.756至近0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据变换管线决定了视觉模型的训练分布，但其设计本身计算代价高昂；现有增强策略受限于预定义增强空间，且依赖大量候选评估与低预算代理评分，因此需要探究LLM能否在预定义空间之外高效地合成变换程序。

Method: 构建统一的训练协议，对比9种策略：标准增强策略、暴力搜索、两个LLM的直接代码生成、以及在按精度排序的变换对上微调的LLM进行性能引导生成；对最佳候选重训练20个epoch，并分析1-epoch选择分数与20-epoch结果的相关性，同时设置阳性对照检验极差限制的影响。

Result: LLM生成变换程序在所有数据集上领先：CIFAR-10 0.8877、CIFAR-100 0.5825、SVHN 0.9588，优于6000候选的暴力搜索。CIFAR-10与SVHN最佳变换来自使用270个候选的微调生成器，CIFAR-100最佳仅来自使用10个候选的现成生成器；相比暴力搜索，生成阶段候选评估分别减少22倍与600倍。1-epoch与20-epoch结果相关性在0.756至近零之间波动，随数据集与架构而异，阳性对照表明极差限制不足以解释低相关。

Conclusion: 基于代码LLM的语义合成是搜索式变换发现的高效替代方案；同时提醒在优化数据变换时必须验证低预算评估的保真度，因为缩减预算的代理评分并不总可靠。

Abstract: Data transformation pipelines shape the training distribution in computer vision, but designing them is a computationally expensive problem. We investigate whether large language models can enable efficient discovery by synthesizing executable transformation programs outside predefined augmentation spaces. We compare nine strategies under a unified training protocol, including standard augmentation policies, brute-force search, direct code generation with two LLMs, and performance-guided generation using LLMs fine-tuned on accuracy-ordered transformation pairs. When the best candidates are retrained for 20 epochs, LLM-generated transformation programs lead on every dataset, reaching 0.8877 accuracy on CIFAR-10, 0.5825 on CIFAR-100, and 0.9588 on SVHN, surpassing brute-force search over 6,000 candidates. The best transform on CIFAR-10 and SVHN comes from a fine-tuned generator using 270 candidates, while the best on CIFAR-100 comes from an off-the-shelf generator using only 10. This needs 22x fewer candidate evaluations in the generation step and 600x fewer for off-the-shelf generations, relative to brute-force search. Beyond transformation discovery, we analyze the reliability of the reduced-budget evaluation commonly used for augmentation search. Correlating one-epoch selection scores with 20-epoch outcomes reveals strong, weak, and absent predictive relationships depending on the dataset and architecture, with correlations ranging from 0.756 to near zero. A positive control indicates that range restriction alone is unlikely to explain the low correlations. Our results indicate that semantic synthesis with code LLMs provides an efficient alternative to search-based transformation discovery and highlight the importance of validating evaluation fidelity when optimizing data transformations.

</details>


### [120] [Anatomy-Activated Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10960)
*Szymon Płotka,Gizem Mert,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Anna Marcinkiewicz,Wiktoria Romańczyk,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Łukasz Struski,Ewa Szczurek,Jacek Tabor,Arkadiusz Sitek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anatomy-Activated Mixture-of-Experts (A²MoE)，通过直方图路由与器官级查询投影，将解剖先验注入医学视觉-语言预训练的计算路径，在3D多任务上取得SOTA并具备跨模态泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉-语言预训练架构本质上是"解剖无关"的：对所有体素块采用统一、稠密的参数化，而临床诊断依赖器官特异性上下文；已有MoE路由器仅基于孤立局部特征进行门控，无法显式获取扫描或裁剪区域的器官组成信息。

Method: A²MoE包含两个组件：(1) Histogram Representation Router (HRR)，以全局视觉上下文和解剖组成直方图预测为条件进行专家选择，实现解剖相关的专家加权；(2) Organ-Query Multi-Scale Projector (OQMSP)，利用可学习查询提取器官级嵌入以进行细粒度对齐，推理阶段无需人工标注。采用patch级而非token级路由，大幅降低路由复杂度，并在构造上满足负载均衡。

Result: 在超过60,000例多期CT扫描上预训练，在分割、分类、检测基准上达到SOTA；解剖学基础先验支持对MRI与超声的稳健泛化。

Conclusion: 将解剖学归纳偏置嵌入模型计算路径，可构建可扩展的医学表征学习基础框架，并具备跨模态迁移能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Pre-training (VLP) leverages the semantic richness of radiology reports to learn 3D representations, yet current architectures remain fundamentally anatomy-agnostic. While clinical diagnostics rely on organ-specific context, standard encoders apply a uniform, dense parameterization to all volumetric patches, and existing Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers gate on isolated local features without explicit access to the organ composition of the scan or crop. We propose Anatomy-Activated Mixture-of-Experts (A$^{2}$MoE), a framework that incorporates anatomical inductive biases within the model's computational path. A$^{2}$MoE introduces two key components: (1) a Histogram Representation Router (HRR) that conditions expert selection on global visual context and a predicted anatomical composition histogram, encouraging anatomy-dependent expert weighting; and (2) an Organ-Query Multi-Scale Projector (OQMSP) that utilizes learnable queries to extract organ-level embeddings for fine-grained alignment without requiring manual annotations at inference. By routing at the patch level rather than the token level, A$^{2}$MoE reduces routing complexity by orders of magnitude while satisfying load balancing by construction. Pre-trained on over 60,000 multi-phase CT scans, A$^{2}$MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance across segmentation, classification, and detection benchmarks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our anatomically grounded inductive biases support robust generalization to MRI and ultrasound, establishing a scalable foundation for medical representation learning. The code is available at https://github.com/gmum/A2MoE.

</details>


### [121] [Sensitivity-Gated Accumulator Caching for On-Device Learning of Low-Bit Hand Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10991)
*Soroor Ghandali,Kanika Mamgain,Manjot Kaur,Akanksha Shukla,Shashira Guntuka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出灵敏度门控累加器缓存（SGAC），仅为少量动态权重组保留残差状态，用8位累加器的组相联缓存累积亚步更新并对被逐出行做随机舍入回写，在383k参数手部关键点网络上实现每权重8.1比特的片上自适应训练，并在多场景部署指标上领先九种低比特方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型手部跟踪网络以4/8位编码部署后，仍需在设备上用接近模型自身的内存预算持续适配用户相机。量化感知训练需保留32位主副本及优化器状态（约96比特/权重），开销过高；直接用随机舍入训练编码虽无需副本，却在每步注入舍入噪声，而消除该噪声的方法需为每个权重保存残差缓冲区。

Method: SGAC采用组相联缓存，仅缓存少量且动态变化的权重组的8位累加器残差状态。按Fisher加权的平方编码步长挑选灵敏度最大的组，缓存行累积这些组的亚步更新并凑成整步写入编码；逐出行以随机舍入刷新，未获缓存行的组在被接纳前不更新。理论层面证明回写无偏，并在局部二次模型下证明：对亚步更新做舍入的损失代价与该更新自身收益同阶，故仅在期望梯度相对曲率较大时舍入才划算。

Result: 在383k参数手部关键点网络上，SGAC以每权重8.1比特状态完成训练。在两个模拟相机上，以设备适配后运行的4/8位网络评分，其在九种部署低比特网络中平均准确率最高；在十二种ImageNet-C损坏上，其腐蚀与干净准确率平均值为七种部署方法中最高，优于PEQA式、ECO式与直接低比特基线（置信区间不含零），并以56%的状态量达到逐权重8位累加器的水平。

Conclusion: SGAC通过灵敏度门控的稀疏残差缓存，将低比特网络的片上持续自适应训练状态开销压至约8比特/权重，理论上保证回写无偏且舍入代价可控，实验上在准确率与内存效率上均优于现有部署方案。

Abstract: Small hand-tracking networks are deployed as 4- and 8-bit codes, and they should keep adapting to a user's camera after deployment, on the device and within roughly the memory of the model itself. Quantization-aware training cannot do this cheaply, because it keeps a 32-bit master copy of every weight together with its optimizer state, about 96 bits per weight. Training the codes directly with stochastic rounding needs no copy but injects rounding noise at every step, and the methods that remove this noise keep a residual buffer for every weight. We introduce sensitivity-gated accumulator caching (SGAC), which keeps residual state for only a small and changing set of weight groups. A set-associative cache of 8-bit accumulators collects the sub-step updates of the groups with the largest Fisher-weighted squared code step and writes them into the codes as whole steps; an evicted line is flushed by stochastic rounding, and a group without a line does not move until it is admitted. We prove that the write-back is unbiased and show, under a local quadratic model, that rounding a sub-step update costs loss of the same order as the update's own gain, so that rounding pays only where the expected gradient is large relative to the curvature. On a 383k-parameter hand-keypoint network, SGAC trains with 8.1 bits of state per weight. Scored on the 4/8-bit network that the device runs after adaptation, it has the highest mean accuracy of nine deployed low-bit networks on two simulated cameras. On twelve ImageNet-C corruptions it has the highest average of corrupted and clean accuracy of seven deployed methods, ahead of the PEQA-style, ECO-style and direct low-bit baselines with confidence intervals that exclude zero, and it matches an 8-bit accumulator kept for every weight with 56% of that state.

</details>


### [122] [Efficient 3D plant segmentation through multiscale graph learning and optimized geometric processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11007)
*Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约7.8万参数的多尺度边缘图注意力模型，用于植株点云分割，并系统评估精度与几何预处理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑计算平台上的植株器官分割既需要准确预测，也需要可负担的几何预处理；现有方法在精度与部署成本之间难以兼顾。

Method: 构建结合局部形状描述子、两个邻域尺度与残差茎叶校正的多尺度边缘图注意力模型；在本地玉米数据集上以7个外层分组、3个随机种子进行四变体嵌套消融；并对全点云PCA、采样与近邻查询进行不改动权重和描述子的工程优化。

Result: 完整模型平均IoU最高，为86.36%；不确定性分析显示边界误差对各组件贡献有约束。PlantNet与TomatoWUR上1,180组配对桌面实验保持logits与预测完全一致；多尺度流水线时延分别由330.33降至96.82 ms、由1,054.52降至463.10 ms。PointNeXt是测试中最强的外部精度参考，且经采样优化后整体流水线仍更快。

Conclusion: 结果支持低几何开销的紧凑图学习方法；树莓派5实测仅证明推理可行性，尚不涉及能效或自主飞行就绪性。

Abstract: Plant organ segmentation on compact computing platforms requires accurate predictions and affordable geometric preparation. We develop and evaluate a multiscale edge based graph attention model that combines local shape descriptors, two neighborhood scales and a residual stem and leaf correction in approximately 78 thousand parameters. The engineering application is plant point cloud segmentation for precision agriculture, assessed through grouped accuracy experiments and physical Raspberry Pi 5 inference. Broad comparisons with hierarchical, graph and transformer baselines establish PointNeXt as the strongest external accuracy reference among the tested local recipes. A nested four variant ablation on a local maize collection separates geometric information from the combined multiscale configuration across seven outer groups and three seeds. The complete model achieves the highest mean intersection over union, 86.36%, although uncertainty and boundary errors qualify the contribution of each component. We then optimize full cloud principal component analysis, sampling and neighbor queries without changing learned weights or descriptor definitions. All 1,180 paired desktop comparisons on PlantNet and TomatoWUR preserve exact logits and predictions. Multiscale pipeline latency falls from 330.33 to 96.82 milliseconds on PlantNet and from 1,054.52 to 463.10 milliseconds on TomatoWUR. PointNeXt also benefits from optimized sampling and remains faster over the complete pipeline. These results support compact graph learning with reduced geometric overhead, while physical board measurements establish inference feasibility rather than energy efficiency or readiness for autonomous flight.

</details>


### [123] [Virtual Task Prompting for Multi-Task Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11041)
*Zedong Wang,Yucheng Wang,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟任务提示（VTP），将多任务提示建模为从共享虚拟状态中检索任务自适应组合，通过逐层可学习路由矩阵与图像token交互，实现与现成视觉基础模型无缝兼容的多任务场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务场景理解模型依赖改造视觉编码器或复杂解码器进行跨任务交互，限制了灵活性与对现成视觉基础模型（VFM）的兼容性。

Method: VTP在主干网络旁维护一组扩展的虚拟任务，并在每一层使用可学习路由矩阵：以任务特定的组合为输入读取共享虚拟状态，与图像token交互后将结果写回，形成编码器之外的显式任务交互通路；虚拟状态与路由过程端到端联合优化，主干保持"干净"不变。

Result: 在Pascal-Context与NYUD-v2上取得新的最优性能；在包含13个异构任务的Taskonomy压力测试中展现出极端设置下的鲁棒性；消融与分析表明性能增益源于自适应路由而非容量增加。

Conclusion: 多任务提示可被重构为对共享虚拟状态的自适应检索与组合，该范式在不修改主干的前提下即插即用地适配预训练VFM，并优于依赖编码器改造的方案。

Abstract: Multi-task scene understanding models typically rely on modified vision encoders or complex decoders for cross-task interaction, which limits their flexibility and compatibility with off-the-shelf vision foundation models (VFMs). We introduce Virtual Task Prompting (VTP), which frames multi-task prompting as retrieving task-adaptive compositions from a shared virtual state, where both the state and routing process are optimized in an end-to-end manner. Concretely, VTP maintains an expanded set of virtual tasks alongside the backbone. At each layer, learnable routing matrices read task-specific compositions as input to interact with image tokens and write the processed results back, forming explicit pathways that capture the intricacies among tasks outside the encoder. Since VTP keeps the backbone clean, it integrates seamlessly with pretrained VFMs, advancing the state of the art on Pascal-Context and NYUD-v2. Stress test on Taskonomy with 13 heterogeneous tasks further shows VTP's robustness in extreme settings. Ablations and analysis indicate that VTP's gains stem from adaptive routing rather than increased capacity. The project page is available at https://jacky1128.github.io/VTP/.

</details>


### [124] [Differentiable Virtual-Slot Partitioning for Adaptive Tokenization in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11058)
*Gaoxiang He,Bin Zhu,Bo Xie,Xiaotong Yu,Yi Chen,Hongfei Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微分虚拟槽分区（DVSP），将四细token与一粗token设为连续插值两端，在训练中不删除任何token即可端到端学习两级四叉树自适应分词，COCO检测保留97.9% Box AP并去除64.2%视觉token、端到端加速1.61×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应分词方法存在两类缺陷：固定或冻结的评分规则无法针对任务损失训练；用直通估计器训练的离散掩码梯度噪声大，而全可微替代方案又偏离预训练的正则网格结构。作者因此从正则网格视角重新审视自适应分词，探索能否在不删除任何token的前提下端到端学习两级四叉树。

Method: 发现一个粗token与其四个细子token可构成单一连续插值的两个端点，据此提出Differentiable Virtual-Slot Partitioning（DVSP）：在每个2×2区域保留四个“虚拟槽”，用单一权重α对其token特征与注意力质量进行混合，当α∈{0,1}时精确复现离散网格，从而在训练中保持token数不变并获得可微梯度。

Result: 在EVA-02-B骨干的COCO检测上，DVSP去除64.2%视觉token仍保留97.9%稠密Box AP，端到端加速1.61×；在相同预算下优于基于熵的APT、DART及均匀低分辨率稠密适配。增益依赖守恒的注意力质量，去除该机制损失1.59 Box AP。在冻结Segmenter上提升Cityscapes mIoU，在冻结DeiT骨干上于相同每图token数下超过熵选择。

Conclusion: 正则网格上的两级四叉树可在不删除token的前提下端到端可微学习；虚拟槽连续插值形式兼顾了离散网格的精确性与梯度质量，注意力质量守恒是性能关键，方法在检测与分割、多骨干设定下均具泛化性。

Abstract: Adaptive tokenization aims to reduce the compute spent on uninformative image regions while preserving accuracy. Existing work relies on (i) fixed or frozen scoring rules, which cannot be trained toward the task loss, or (ii) discrete masks trained with straight-through estimators, which yield noisy gradients, while fully differentiable alternatives instead leave the pre-trained regular grid. We revisit adaptive tokenization from a regular-grid perspective and ask: can a two-scale quadtree be learned end-to-end without deleting a single token during training? We show that one coarse token and its four fine children can be made the two endpoints of a single continuous interpolation. We introduce Differentiable Virtual-Slot Partitioning (\dvsp{}), which keeps four \emph{virtual slots} per $2{\times}2$ region and blends their token features and attention mass with a single weight $α$, exactly reproducing the discrete grid at $α\in \{0,1\}$. On COCO detection with EVA-02-B, \dvsp{} retains $97.9\%$ of dense Box AP while removing $64.2\%$ of visual tokens, giving a $1.61\times$ end-to-end speedup, and it outperforms entropy-based APT, DART, and uniform lower-resolution dense adaptation at matched budgets; these gains depend on the conserved attention mass, whose removal costs $1.59$ Box AP. On a frozen Segmenter it also improves Cityscapes mIoU, and on frozen DeiT backbones it exceeds entropy selection at identical per-image token counts.

</details>


### [125] [What Does Multiple Choice Actually Measure? Disentangling Video Understanding from Option Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11062)
*Elliott Piercy,Justin Chan,Sepand Dyanatkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有VideoQA多选评测中大量得分来自选项与语言先验等评测语境而非真实视觉感知，并提出Option-Delayed Replay (ODR)机制与可复现评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VideoQA基准依赖多选选项引导、语言先验和脆弱评分流程，难以隔离模型真实的视频感知与任务求解能力；需要拆解这些混杂因素，衡量分数中有多少来自评测语境而非忠实感知。

Method: 在六个不同时长的VideoQA基准上，移除视觉输入仍测试准确率；分析shortcutting与option-prior依赖，并用忠实性语言刻画option-exploitation与answer-conditioned推理轨迹；提出ODR机制，在开放式轨迹的受控阶段披露选项，从而由忠实的无选项推理获得客观多选分数；将诊断集合与ODR形式化为可复现评测协议，并发布人工重标注的LVBench（修正20.5%的键）以消除标注噪声。

Result: 在六个VideoQA基准上，无任何视觉输入时准确率显著高于随机；分数由语言先验、选项出现时机以及输出与标签转成正确性的方式共同决定；采用ODR与忠实推理的agentic方法可达到与利用选项相当的性能。

Conclusion: VideoQA分数并非纯粹反映感知能力，必须解耦评测语境贡献；ODR与忠实推理提供客观多选评测途径，配套可复现协议与去噪的LVBench标注可提升评测有效性。

Abstract: Video understanding evaluations are intended to measure a model's proficiency in video perception and task solving. However, current VideoQA benchmarks struggle to isolate these capabilities due to their reliance on multiple-choice (MC) answer guidance, language priors, and fragile scoring pipelines. Disentangling these confounding factors reveals how much of a model's score is supplied by the evaluation context rather than earned through faithful perception. Across six VideoQA benchmarks of varying length, we find that accuracy is significantly above chance without any visual input. This is caused by shortcutting and option-prior reliance. We express these effects in the language of faithfulness: option-exploitation and answer-conditioned reasoning traces are unfaithful even when they are correct. Together, our results show that a VideoQA score is jointly determined by language priors, option timing, and how outputs and labels are converted into correctness. We produce a novel Option-Delayed Replay (ODR) mechanism that discloses options at controlled stages of an open-ended (OE) trajectory, to obtain objective MC scores from faithful option-free inference. Our work demonstrates that agentic methods with ODR and faithful inference can match option-exploiting performance. Finally, we formalize our diagnostic collection and ODR as a reproducible evaluation protocol, and we release a human-relabelled edition of LVBench (correcting $20.5\%$ of keys) to remove annotation noise as a confounding variable.

</details>


### [126] [NanoNet: Ultra-Lightweight Network for MRI Brain Tumor Classification toward Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11071)
*Tran Nguyen-Le,Huu-Thuan Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出超轻量CNN NanoNet用于MRI脑肿瘤分类：仅5个倒残差块、8132参数，在BRISC 2025上达92.80%准确率/92.97%宏F1，并验证剪枝、蒸馏与INT8量化压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动MRI脑肿瘤分类有助可及性医学影像分析，但常规深度模型常超出资源受限边缘AI平台的预算，需极轻量方案。

Method: 受MobileNetV2启发，大幅削减网络深度、通道容量、扩展比与最终特征维度，构建仅5个倒残差块、8132参数的NanoNet；并进一步考察依赖感知结构化剪枝、知识蒸馏与INT8量化感知训练（QAT）。

Result: BRISC 2025上92.80%准确率、92.97%宏F1，参数8132、10.498 MFLOPs；三个压缩种子下比70%剪枝的MobileNetV2平均准确率高5.20个百分点，参数少60.2%、计算少9.8%；INT8 NanoNet在三个QAT种子下89.70%准确率、90.22%宏F1；早期TFLite测量基线存储27.7 KB。

Conclusion: 超轻量架构可在极小参数预算下保持较高分类精度，且优于剪枝后的MobileNetV2，为未来边缘AI部署提供可行基线。

Abstract: Automated brain tumor classification from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) can support accessible medical image analysis, but conventional deep learning models often exceed the resource budgets of highly constrained edge-AI platforms. We present NanoNet, an ultra-lightweight convolutional neural network designed for resourceconstrained MRI classification and motivated by future edge-AI applications. Inspired by MobileNetV2, NanoNet substantially reduces network depth, channel capacity, expansion ratios, and final feature dimensionality, resulting in only five inverted residual blocks and 8,132 parameters. We further investigate dependency-aware structured pruning, knowledge distillation, and INT8 quantization-aware training for additional compression. Experiments on the BRISC 2025 benchmark show that NanoNet achieves 92.80% accuracy and 92.97% macro F1 with 8,132 parameters and 10.498 MFLOPs. Across three compression seeds, it exceeds the mean accuracy of 70%-pruned MobileNetV2 by 5.20 percentage points while using 60.2% fewer parameters and 9.8% less computation. Converted INT8 NanoNet achieves 89.70% accuracy and 90.22% macro F1 across three QAT seeds. Earlier TensorFlow Lite measurements indicate 27.7 KB of baseline model storage, motivating future edge-AI deployment.

</details>


### [127] [ADC-RANet: Improving Resolution Adaptive Neural Networks with Attention, Distillation and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11076)
*Jeferson Severino de Araujo,Lilian Hollard,Eric Granger,Marco Pedersoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADC-RANet：在RANet基础上融合自底向上通道注意力、自顶向下级联知识蒸馏与MixUp过自信校准，在CIFAR-100与ImageNet上于各计算预算下稳定提升性能并保持效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态神经网络可为每个样本自适应分配计算量，在保持性能的同时降低计算成本；RANet通过按样本难度渐进分配不同分辨率的计算资源，在图像分类中表现突出，但仍存在性能提升空间（特征利用不足、子网络训练不充分、置信度校准偏差等）。

Method: 提出ADC-RANet，由三个紧密耦合的组件构成：（1）自底向上通道注意力（bottom-up channel attention），增强特征表达；（2）自顶向下级联知识蒸馏（top-down cascaded knowledge distillation），以深层/高分辨率出口指导浅层/低分辨率子网络；（3）基于MixUp的过自信校正（overconfidence calibration）。三者协同设计，统一协调RANet的早退与主路径。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet上实验，在所有计算预算（不同分辨率出口）下均取得一致性性能增益，同时保持RANet原有计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过注意力、级联蒸馏与置信度校准的协同组合，可有效释放动态分辨率自适应网络的潜力，在无额外推理开销的情况下实现更优的精度—效率权衡，且方法具有通用性。

Abstract: Dynamic neural networks are a class of adaptive models in which the computation assigned to each input sample is determined dynamically, potentially reducing computational cost while maintaining similar performance. For image classification, the Resolution Adaptive Network (RANet) is a strong approach that progressively allocates computational resources across input scales based on sample difficulty. In this work, we introduce RANet with Attention, Distillation and Calibration (ADC-RANet), an enhanced version of RANet that improves performance across all computational budgets. Our approach combines three tightly connected components: bottom-up channel attention, top-down cascaded knowledge distillation, and overconfidence correction using MixUp, in a synergistic design that coordinates both pathways. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet datasets show consistent gains while preserving the efficiency of RANet. Our code is available: https://github.com/jefersonseverino/ADC-RANet

</details>


### [128] [SoftPAT3D: Physics-Augmented Text-to-3D Scene Generation with Deformables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11087)
*Lei Shu,Alberta Longhini,Leonidas Guibas,Gordon Wetzstein,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftPAT3D 提出一个面向文本到三维场景生成的物理增强框架，可将布料、毛绒玩具、绳索等可变形物体生成为物理有效、可直接仿真的刚柔混合场景，并在全部65个提示下实现零非期望穿模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理增强式文本到3D流程仅支持刚体，无法处理可变形物体；而文本到3D生成的可变形网格几何不合法（可能自交），缺少静止形状、材料模型与可容许初始状态，即缺少可变形仿真所需的全部输入。

Method: 从流程已有信息中构造仿真输入：静止形状取自物体的制造方式而非物理不一致的生成形变配置；材料库为每个可变形物体赋初始弹性参数并支持优化。重建被遮挡的支撑物并转为水密碰撞网格，将物体置于提示所指定支撑部件上方可优化的释放位置，在统一接触模型下驱动整场景至静态平衡，并用几何检查与视觉语言模型验证完成度。失败时先用放置网格搜索修正，再用同一接触能量可微仿真的伴随法实现网格搜索难以达到的目标、多体联合放置以及材料参数恢复与优化。

Result: 多体场景可在单次仿真中达到平衡，且流程可仅由文本提示驱动；在全部65个提示上，SoftPAT3D 不产生任何形式的非期望穿插，而其他所有系统均会出现；生成场景可直接支持场景编辑与机器人操作。

Conclusion: 通过在生成流程内部补齐可变形仿真所需的静止形状、材料模型与初始状态，并统一接触求解与可微优化，SoftPAT3D 将文本到3D从单纯几何生成推进到物理有效、仿真就绪且可交互的场景生成。

Abstract: We present SoftPAT3D, a physics-augmented framework for text-to-3D scene generation in which deformable objects, such as cloth, stuffed toys and ropes, are generated as physically valid, simulation-ready bodies and placed in intersection-free contact with the furniture that supports them, with one another and with themselves. Existing physics-augmented pipelines simulate rigid bodies only and cannot accommodate a generated deformable object, which text-to-3D generation returns as a geometrically invalid mesh, sometimes self-intersecting, that has no rest shape, no material model and no admissible initial state. These are the inputs a deformable simulation requires, and we construct each from information already in the pipeline. The rest shape comes from how the object is manufactured rather than from the generated physically inconsistent deformed configuration, and a material library assigns each deformable object its initial elastic parameters, which can then be optimized. The occluded support is regenerated and converted into a watertight collision mesh, each object is released from an optimizable placement above the supporting part that the prompt specifies, and the complete scene is driven to static equilibrium under one unified contact model, with completion verified by geometric checks and a vision-language model. When a scene fails the prompt, a grid search over placements corrects it, and the adjoint of a differentiable simulation of the same contact energy reaches the targets the grid does not, places several bodies jointly and recovers and optimizes material parameters. Multi-body scenes equilibrate in a single simulation, and the pipeline also runs from the text prompt alone. On all sixty-five prompts, SoftPAT3D produces no undesired interpenetration of any kind, whereas every other system does, and its scenes directly support scene editing and robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [129] [Does Pose Encoding Matter? Analyzing Geometric Representations for Discrete Motion Vocabulary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11093)
*Amir Kimiyaie,Nahal Mafi,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较四种骨骼运动编码在VQ-VAE离散化下的效果，发现输入几何空间决定有效词表规模，重建损失无法揭示这一差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散运动token已成为运动理解与生成流水线的首个环节，但潜在空间的几何由其输入姿态编码继承，哪种编码更利于高效矢量量化尚不清楚。

Method: 研究四种编码：像素格点上的高斯关节热图（被证明为关节点的、以二次结构函数为核的形态学膨胀）、欧氏空间中的根相对关节位置、经6D连续嵌入的SO(3)局部关节旋转、以及冗余的完整运动学编码。为每种编码训练码本位于单位超球面的VQ-VAE分词器，变化码本大小、时间步长与码维度；除重建外，测量码本利用率与"有效词表规模"（即与观测token分布熵相同的等概率球面Voronoi单元数）。

Result: 旋转与位置编码能持续将新增码字转化为有效词表，完整运动学编码在数百个有效单元处饱和，热图则少于60个；四种编码中三种在最小码维度下利用率最高；时间步长的影响依编码而异。

Conclusion: 同一姿态族的不同编码可产生相差一个数量级的有效词表，而仅看重建损失无法察觉，说明输入几何是运动token化的一阶设计选择。

Abstract: Compressing continuous human motion into a finite vocabulary of discrete tokens is now the first stage of many motion understanding and generation pipelines. Vector quantization partitions a latent space into Voronoi cells around a finite codebook, but the geometry of that latent space is inherited from the input pose encoding, and it is unclear which encoding admits an efficient discretization. We study four encodings that place skeletal motion in different geometric spaces: Gaussian joint heatmaps on the pixel lattice, which we show to be a morphological dilation of the joint point set by a quadratic structuring function; root-relative joint positions in Euclidean space; local joint rotations on $\SO$ through a continuous 6D embedding; and a redundant full kinematic encoding. For each, we train VQ-VAE tokenizers whose codebooks lie on the unit hypersphere, varying codebook size, temporal stride, and code dimension. Beyond reconstruction, we measure codebook utilization and the effective vocabulary size, i.e., the number of equiprobable spherical Voronoi cells with the same entropy as the observed token distribution. Rotation and position encodings keep converting additional codewords into effective vocabulary, whereas the full kinematic encoding saturates at a few hundred effective cells and heatmaps at fewer than sixty. The smallest code dimension gives the highest utilization for three of the four encodings, and the effect of temporal stride depends on the encoding. Encodings of the same pose family can thus yield effective vocabularies differing by an order of magnitude, a difference that reconstruction loss alone does not reveal, which points to input geometry as a first-order design choice in motion tokenization.

</details>


### [130] [MySign: A High-Fidelity Motion-Capture Dataset for 3D Sign Generation in Bahasa Isyarat Malaysia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11115)
*Jiayu Shen,Kalin Stefanov,Lay-Ki Soon,Vee Yee Chong,Alpha Agape Gopalai,KokSheik Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MySign：首个马来手语（BIM）孤立词的动捕数据集，含5,000样本/1,000词条，由5名聋人母语者采集，提供真值SMPL-X序列，并作为gloss条件3D手语生成的挑战性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D手语生成受限于高保真3D手语数据稀缺，低资源语言尤为严重；现有依赖视频回归SMPL-X参数的数据集只能提供伪标签，存在深度歧义、手指姿态不准与时间抖动问题。

Method: 构建专为手语定制的动捕采集流程，采集5名聋人母语者表演的1,000个词条共5,000个孤立词样本；将动捕记录重定向为真值SMPL-X序列；在同一词汇与签名者条件下，与伪标签训练进行受控对比；并建立gloss条件3D手语生成与词级识别两组基线评测。

Result: 相比视频伪标签，使用真值序列训练可提升生成质量；生成基线虽输出视觉上合理，但在细粒度手部/手指articulation上明显落后于真值；识别基线则持续取得高词级准确率，表明数据可可靠支撑监督学习。

Conclusion: MySign填补了BIM手语动捕数据空白，验证了真值动捕序列相对伪标签的优势，并揭示articulated sign motion建模的持续差距；数据集已公开，可作为低资源手语3D生成的标准基准。

Abstract: Progress in 3D sign generation is constrained by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D sign language datasets, a limitation that is particularly severe for under-resourced languages. We introduce MySign, the first motion-capture dataset for isolated signs in Bahasa Isyarat Malaysia (BIM). Its 5,000 samples cover 1,000 lexical items (glosses) and were produced by five Deaf native signers. Prior datasets that rely on SMPL-X parameters regressed from video provide pseudo-labels, which are often affected by depth ambiguity, inaccurate finger poses, and temporal jitter. MySign instead provides ground-truth SMPL-X sequences retargeted from recordings made with a motion-capture setup tailored for sign language; these sequences offer higher-fidelity motion, particularly in fine-grained hand and finger articulation. A controlled comparison on the same glosses and signers indicates that training on these sequences improves generation over training on such pseudo-labels. We establish MySign as a challenging benchmark for gloss-conditioned 3D sign generation: state-of-the-art models produce visually plausible outputs but fall short of the ground truth, most notably in fine-grained articulation, revealing persistent gaps in modeling articulated sign motion. In contrast, recognition baselines consistently achieve high lexical accuracy, indicating that the dataset reliably supports supervised learning. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/mysigner/MySign

</details>


### [131] [Query-Only Prompt-Agnostic Attacks on Visual In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11116)
*Xiao Hu,Muhammad Ibrahim,Hao Wen,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianliang Wu,Antonio Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究仅查询、提示无关的视觉上下文学习（VICL）定向攻击：将提示条件化的RGB渲染视为攻击面，提出颜色劫持（CH）与任务劫持（TH），并用自提示代理（SPS）实现跨未见提示迁移，显著破坏分割输出与下游自动标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VICL通过视觉示例条件下的RGB预测实现可提示分割，适合自动标注，但其在"仅可访问查询、部署提示未知"场景下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究，存在现实威胁缺口。

Method: 设定查询-only、提示无关的定向攻击威胁模型（仅观测返回的RGB输出，无模型内部与部署提示）；将提示条件化RGB渲染作为独立攻击面，设计外观空间攻击：Color Hijack（CH）将前景颜色推向背景以抑制可见性，Task Hijack（TH）将实例区分性颜色向共享颜色分配坍缩；为跨未见提示迁移，构建由干净查询及其输出组成的Self-Prompt Surrogate（SPS）。在SegGPT上与等预算Random-Prompt Surrogate（RPS）配对比较。

Result: SegGPT配对等预算对比中，SPS优于RPS：CH实例前景保留率由0.918降至0.155，TH调色板感知容差距离由0.345降至0.225；在COCO与HierText上的四个VICL模型中，CH抑制语义/实例/文本风格输出的前景，TH在留出部署提示下使实例颜色坍缩；经调色板解码后TH降低解码实例数与掩码保留率并增加实例合并；30%污染率下，基于公开VICL自动标注流水线生成的CH污染标注使下游YOLO-seg mAP50由0.761降至0.281。

Conclusion: 仅查询、提示无关的外观空间攻击可有效迁移至未见部署提示并跨模型泛化，说明VICL自动标注存在显著安全风险，需在设计部署中加以防护。

Abstract: Visual In-Context Learning (VICL) enables promptable segmentation by conditioning RGB predictions on visual demonstrations, making it attractive for auto-labeling, but its robustness under query-only access and unknown deployment prompts remains underexplored. We study query-only, prompt-agnostic targeted attacks where the attacker observes only returned RGB outputs, without access to model internals or deployment prompts. Treating prompt-conditioned RGB rendering as a distinct attack surface, we introduce two appearance-space attacks: Color Hijack (CH) drives foreground colors toward the background to suppress visibility, while Task Hijack (TH) collapses instance-distinguishing colors toward a shared color assignment. To transfer across unseen prompts, we propose a Self-Prompt Surrogate (SPS) constructed from the clean query and its output. In a paired, matched-budget SegGPT comparison, SPS outperforms the Random-Prompt Surrogate (RPS), reducing CH instance foreground retention from 0.918 to 0.155 and TH palette-aware tolerance distance from 0.345 to 0.225. Across four VICL models on COCO and HierText, CH suppresses foregrounds across semantic-, instance-, and text-style outputs, while TH collapses instance colors under held-out deployment prompts. After palette-based decoding, TH also reduces the decoded instance count and mask retention while increasing instance merges. Finally, at 30% corruption, CH-corrupted annotations from a publicly accessible VICL-based auto-labeling pipeline reduce downstream YOLO-seg mAP50 from 0.761 to 0.281.

</details>


### [132] [What You Show Is Not What You Get: Rethinking Demonstration Following in Visual In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11122)
*Xiao Hu,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文揭示视觉上下文分割中SegGPT的两类偏差——背景颜色映射偏差与部分到父对象扩张，并据此提出语义对比提示选择(SCPS)方法，通过语义对比编码与质量控制选择演示，提升目标IoU并减少泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SegGPT等视觉上下文分割模型依赖演示引导预测，但存在演示跟随失败的偏差：非目标区域获得不同输出颜色（背景颜色映射偏差），预测扩张到父对象（部分到父对象扩张）。作者希望在内部特征仍可区分的情况下解释这些偏差来源并加以改善。

Method: 先通过受控改变演示颜色与请求目标进行诊断实验，提出"演示在熟悉语义区域上引导角色与颜色分配、受学习到的语义与颜色偏好影响"的解释；再提出SCPS：语义对比编码为请求部件与其所属对象其余部分分配不同颜色、其余场景置黑；并结合现有检索分数与来自带标注开发图像的质量估计来选择演示及其二值或语义对比编码。

Result: 在三个基准、七种检索方法上平均，SCPS相比原始二值选择器将目标IoU提升0.054，并将向对象其余部分的泄漏降低22.7%。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解与改进视觉上下文分割中的演示跟随提供了新视角，说明编码方式与演示选择对角色/颜色分配至关重要。

Abstract: Visual in-context segmentation uses visual demonstrations to guide predictions for a new image. In SegGPT, we identify background color mapping deviation, where non-target regions sharing a background label receive different output colors, and part-to-parent expansion, where predictions extend beyond the requested part into its parent object. These deviations can occur even when the relevant regions remain distinguishable in internal features. Controlled changes to demonstration colors and requested targets support an account in which demonstrations guide role and color assignments over familiar semantic regions, influenced by learned semantic and color preferences. Building on these findings, we introduce Semantic Contrast Prompt Selection (SCPS). Its semantic contrast encoding assigns separate colors to the requested part and the rest of its object while keeping the remaining scene black. SCPS combines existing retrieval scores with quality estimates from labeled development images to select demonstrations and their binary or semantic contrast encodings. Averaged across seven retrieval methods on three benchmarks, SCPS improves target IoU by 0.054 and reduces leakage into the rest of the object by 22.7% relative to the original binary selectors. Our findings offer a new perspective on understanding and improving demonstration following in visual in-context segmentation.

</details>


### [133] [CrossLiDAR: Controllable Multi-Sensor LiDAR Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11131)
*Khanh Nguyen,Thomas Walker,Hichem Telli,Carlos de la Torre-Ortiz,Stefano Fiorini,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Wassim Swaileh,Tinghuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossLiDAR：基于MM-DiT与rectified flow、在多传感器range-image潜空间中生成可控且时空一致的多LiDAR序列的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真LiDAR仿真对自动驾驶规模化开发与验证至关重要，但现有生成器局限于单一传感器配置、对结构与几何的控制有限，且在长时程4D生成中出现漂移。

Method: 在紧凑的range image潜空间上，采用rectified flow目标配合MM-DiT主干；引入Inter-sensor Module实现跨传感器交互以支持多传感器；采用ControlNet风格分支提供结构条件（如BEV先验或3D包围框）；通过生成短N帧序列并自回归扩展以增强长时程时序一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes与Waymo上显著优于SOTA基线，JSD平均提升29.56%，MMD提升15.36%。

Conclusion: CrossLiDAR实现了标定一致的多传感器LiDAR长时程rollout，支持可控的基于场景的测试，弥合了与现代自动驾驶仿真之间的差距。

Abstract: High-fidelity LiDAR simulation remains a bottleneck for scalable development and validation of autonomous systems. Existing generators can synthesize realistic scans, but they are typically constrained to a single sensor configuration, offer limited control over structure and geometry, and drift in long-horizon 4D generation. We present CrossLiDAR, a unified transformer-based framework capable of generating controllable and spatio-temporally consistent multi-sensor LiDAR sequences. Our approach leverages a rectified flow objective with a Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer (MM-DiT) backbone, within a compact latent space of range images. To support multiple LiDAR sensors, an Inter-sensor Module enables interaction across sensors, while a ControlNet-style branch provides structural conditioning (e.g., BEV priors or 3D bounding boxes). For modeling the temporal consistency, CrossLiDAR generates short $N$-frame sequences and extends them autoregressively to improve long-horizon coherence. Our model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average improvement of 29.56\% in JSD and 15.36\% in MMD evaluated on nuScenes and the Waymo Dataset. CrossLiDAR bridges the gap to modern autonomous-driving simulation by enabling calibration-consistent multi-sensor LiDAR rollouts over long horizons for controllable, scenario-based testing.

</details>


### [134] [From Game Generation to Game Playing: Fully Automated Ability Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11136)
*Changran Liu,Qi Wu,Wenchao Ma,Sharon X. Huang,Haomiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Script2Play 框架：用 LLM 智能体自动构建游戏环境并生成专家演示，以合成监督训练 4B VLA 游戏策略，无需人类演示或强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在多款游戏中遵循玩法指令，受限于收集人类演示的高昂成本。

Method: 由 LLM 驱动的智能体调用游戏构建工具，自动生成可执行环境与针对特定技能的任务；程序化专家利用仿真器的特权状态生成对齐的指令、渲染观测及键鼠动作；再用行为克隆对 4B 视觉-语言模型做后训练，得到游戏策略 Script2Play，训练数据为 150.3 小时的合成 Minecraft 与 Roblox 玩法。

Result: 在多个公开游戏基准上的评测显示，Script2Play 能有效遵循玩法指令，并具备跨游戏引擎的泛化能力，验证了定向合成监督的有效性。

Conclusion: 自动化生成环境与专家演示可替代昂贵的人类演示，为 VLA 游戏策略提供可扩展、可迁移的合成监督路径。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) models to follow gameplay instructions across diverse games is limited by the cost of collecting demonstrations. We introduce a framework that automatically generates game environments and expert demonstrations. An LLM-powered agent uses game-building tools to construct executable environments and tasks targeting specified skills. Programmatic experts then use privileged simulator state to generate aligned instructions, rendered observations, and keyboard and mouse actions. We train our game policy, Script2Play, by post-training a 4B vision-language model with behavior cloning on 150.3 hours of synthetic Minecraft and Roblox gameplay, without human gameplay demonstrations or reinforcement learning. Evaluation on multiple public game benchmarks demonstrates that Script2Play follows gameplay instructions effectively and generalizes across game engines, highlighting the effectiveness of targeted synthetic supervision.

</details>


### [135] [Graphene: A Lightweight Transformer with Ising Mean-Field Refinement for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11139)
*Seungki Hong,Taekwang Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级图像超分架构 Graphene，融合 Ising 模型启发的局部特征精炼（IRB）与基于阶段历史的特征精炼（HistAttn），在 Urban100×4 上以更少参数、FLOPs、延迟和显存取得更优 PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量超分网络在局部特征建模与跨阶段特征复用上存在冗余计算，需在保持性能的同时显著降低参数、算力、延迟与内存开销。

Method: IRB 复用注意力输出与 pre-softmax logits 构造窗口内有符号成对 token 耦合，执行展开的平均场更新并以门控残差校正精炼特征；HistAttn 在同一 token 位置上对当前阶段输出与早期表示做历史加权聚合，自适应融合且不引入空间成对注意力。

Result: Urban100×4 上 Graphene/Graphene-light 较 IET/IET-light 分别提升 0.24 dB 与 0.38 dB，参数量减少 34.3%/13.7%，FLOPs 减少 44.8%/44.4%，延迟降低 78.4%/71.5%，峰值内存降低 76.0%/85.5%；真实场景 SR 中细节恢复与纹理保持良好。

Conclusion: 两种互补精炼机制可有效提升轻量超分的精度—效率权衡，具备实际部署与真实退化场景的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes Graphene, a lightweight image super-resolution architecture with two complementary feature refinement mechanisms: Ising-model-inspired local feature refinement using an Ising Refinement Block (IRB) and stage-history-based feature refinement using a History Attention (HistAttn) module. The IRB reuses attention output features and pre-softmax attention logits to construct signed pairwise token couplings within each local window, applies unrolled mean-field updates, and refines features through a gated residual correction. These signed couplings allow alignment and opposition between latent spins to modulate local feature refinement. HistAttn attends over the current stage output and earlier representations at the same token position and adaptively blends the current stage output with a history-weighted feature aggregate without adding spatial pairwise attention. On Urban100 at $\times4$, Graphene and Graphene-light improve over IET and IET-light by 0.24 dB and 0.38 dB, respectively, with 34.3%/13.7% fewer parameters, 44.8%/44.4% fewer FLOPs, 78.4%/71.5% lower latency, and 76.0%/85.5% lower peak memory. Real-world SR comparisons further show that Graphene recovers fine details and preserves textures across diverse degradations. Code and reference checkpoints are available at https://github.com/skethz/Graphene

</details>
