</details>


### [205] [Weight-Space Programming for Medical Imaging: Do We Still Need Training Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12066)
*Maximilian Lindholz,Richard Ruppel,Annette Milnik,Sebastian Halskov,Semil Eminovic,Charlie A. Hamm,Robin Schmidt,Anika Knupfer,Jana Hutter,Johanna P. Müller,Carolina Dominguez Aleixo,Jawed Nawabi,Jens Vogel-Claussen,Tobias Penzkofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 大语言模型（LLM）能否仅凭医学知识、无需任何训练数据就写出医学图像分类器的参数？作者通过"权重空间编程"（WSP）进行验证：让 Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6 Astra 在单次回复中直接生成小型 LeNet 风格 CNN 的全部权重与偏置，并在胸部X光、乳腺超声、腹部CT（MedMNIST）与盆腔MRI（SynthUterus）共7个任务上，与零样本 MedSigLIP 及用 Adam 训练的同架构网络对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器通常依赖昂贵的标注数据，许多场景下标注数据难以获得。作者希望检验 LLM 是否能把从医学文献/知识中习得的先验直接"写入"网络权重，从而绕开数据与优化过程，探索一种无需训练数据的分类器构建途径。

Method: 采用权重空间编程（WSP）：LLM 在无工具调用、无任何图像输入的情况下，一次性输出小型 LeNet 风格 CNN 的权重与偏置，不做任何基于数据的优化。基线包括：(1) 零样本 MedSigLIP；(2) 同一架构用 Adam 在仅 256 张标注图像上训练。评测覆盖 4 种模态、7 个任务：MedMNIST 的胸部X光、乳腺超声、腹部CT（各含2个任务），以及 SynthUterus 盆腔MRI。指标为测试集 AUC。

Result: LLM 写出的网络在全部6个 MedMNIST 任务上均优于随机，平均测试 AUC：Opus 0.719、Astra 0.711；在6个任务中的5个上超过 MedSigLIP，且参数量少逾10,000倍。但同一架构仅用256张标注图像经 Adam 训练，在每个 MedMNIST 任务上均更优（平均 AUC 0.828）。在 SynthUterus 上，Opus 达 AUC 0.764，Astra 因系统性错误低于随机（AUC 0.136）。

Conclusion: LLM 能够直接写出具有实际判别能力的医学图像分类器，说明医学知识可部分转化为权重空间中的有效先验；但其性能尚不及少量标注数据下的常规训练，因此目前无法替代训练数据。作者开源了协议、日志与全部生成权重。

Abstract: Can a large language model (LLM) write the parameters of a medical image classifier from medical knowledge alone, without any training data? Such classifiers normally require labelled images, which are costly and often unavailable. We investigate this question through weight-space programming (WSP), in which an LLM writes or adapts the weights and biases of a neural network directly, without optimisation on training data. Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra each generate the parameters of a small LeNet-style CNN in a single reply, without tool use and without access to any image. We compare these networks against zero-shot MedSigLIP and against the same architecture trained on labels using the Adam optimiser, across 7 classification tasks in four modalities: chest X-ray, breast ultrasound and abdominal CT (all from MedMNIST), and pelvic MRI (SynthUterus). The LLM-written networks perform above chance on all six MedMNIST tasks, with a mean test AUC of 0.719 for Opus and 0.711 for Astra. Both models outperform MedSigLIP on 5 of 6 tasks while using more than 10,000 times fewer parameters. However, the same network trained with Adam on only 256 labelled images achieves a higher AUC on every MedMNIST task (mean AUC 0.828). On SynthUterus, Opus attains an AUC of 0.764, whereas Astra falls below chance (AUC 0.136) owing to a systematic error. These results show that LLMs can write functional medical classifiers, but that they do not yet replace training data. We release the protocol, logs and all generated weights at https://github.com/MaximilianLindholz/weight-space-programming-medical.

</details>


### [206] [MPEG-Diffusion: Mixed-Resolution Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12069)
*Dennis Menn,Tianpei Gu,Guande He,Aryan V S,Yanke Song,Nan Liu,Desai Xie,Xun Huang,Chenfeng Xu,Diana Marculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MPEG-Diffusion，通过双分辨率注意力、混合分辨率解码器与自选高分辨率区域三项设计，修复现有混合分辨率视频生成的质量缺陷，在仅用30%全分辨率token下实现4.8倍去噪加速且质量接近全分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer（DiT）计算开销巨大；混合分辨率生成（重要区域高分辨率、其余低分辨率）可降本，但现有方法在视频上质量严重下降且需预先给定高分辨率区域。作者论证这些失败并非混合分辨率的固有缺陷。

Method: 识别三大根因并提出对应设计：(1) 双分辨率注意力，每个query仅读取自身分辨率的K/V，缓解"混合分辨率诅咒"；(2) 混合分辨率解码器，在解码前融合HR与LR隐变量以消除畸变；(3) 利用DiT自身知识自选高分辨率区域，无需预先指定。

Result: 仅处理30%全分辨率token，在Wan2.1 14B上去噪加速4.8倍（1.3B上3.8倍），几乎所有指标优于先前方法；用户研究中超过90%的成对比较被偏好，评分接近全分辨率。

Conclusion: 混合分辨率的视频生成质量问题可通过针对性架构与解码设计解决；MPEG-Diffusion在保持接近全分辨率视觉质量的同时显著降低计算成本，提升实际可用性。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) generate realistic videos, but their heavy computational cost limits broader real-world applications. Mixed-resolution generation reduces this cost by generating the visually important region at high resolution (HR) and the rest at low resolution (LR), yet existing methods suffer a severe quality drop on videos and require knowing the HR region in advance. We show that these failures are not inherent to mixed resolution, and propose $\textbf{MPEG-Diffusion}$ to address their three root causes: (1) dual-resolution attention to mitigate the curse of mixed resolution, where each query reads the K/V of its own resolution, (2) a mixed-resolution decoder to blend the HR and LR latents before decoding and resolve distortion, and (3) a self-selected HR region using the DiT's knowledge to find the visually important region without requiring it in advance. Processing only $30\%$ of full-resolution tokens, MPEG-Diffusion speeds up denoising by $4.8\times$ on Wan2.1 14B ($3.8\times$ on 1.3B), outperforming prior methods on nearly every metric. In the user study, we demonstrate the high quality of MPEG-Diffusion, with raters preferring it over prior mixed-resolution methods in over $90\%$ of pairs and rating it close to full resolution.

</details>


### [207] [Color wave: A systematic evaluation of active screen illumination for injection attack detection in remote face authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12070)
*Daniyar Kurmankhojayev,Elmurat Nigmatov,Dmitrii Gordin,Andrei Shadrikov,Mikhail Shkorin,Danijar Gabdullin,Vyacheslav Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将注入攻击按静态图像、预录视频、实时深度伪造分类，把主动屏幕照明方法实现为 color wave 并建立注入专用评测协议，系统评估其检测有效性、工况泛化性、信号退化鲁棒性与逃逸脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程人脸认证在比对前需同时防御呈现攻击与生物特征数据注入攻击；基于主动屏幕照明的方法对二者均有潜力，但现有研究多集中于呈现攻击检测（PAD），针对注入攻击检测（IAD）的系统性评估仍很有限。

Method: 依据注入内容对挑战信号的预期响应，将其分为静态图像、预录视频和实时深度伪造三类；将主动屏幕照明方案适配为 "color wave" 实现，并设计针对注入攻击的系统评测协议，考察检测有效性、跨操作条件泛化、对信号退化的鲁棒性以及对特定逃逸策略的易感性。

Result: 在受控条件下呈现强判别信号；但在过曝、低屏幕亮度和被试运动条件下性能下降，并对所选逃逸策略存在脆弱性。

Conclusion: 通过将检测结果与攻击类型和操作条件关联，该研究给出了主动屏幕照明作为IAD机制何时成功、为何失败的可解释性评估。

Abstract: Remote face authentication requires protection against both presentation attacks and biometric data injection attacks prior to face matching. Methods based on active screen illumination appear promising for both by introducing randomized visual stimuli through the user's device screen and evaluating the corresponding facial responses. Although such methods have been more studied for presentation attack detection (PAD), their systematic evaluation for injection attack detection (IAD) remains limited. To address this gap, we first classify injected content into static images, prerecorded videos, and real-time deepfakes based on their expected responses to the challenge. We then adapt an active screen-illumination approach into an implementation we term color wave and develop a systematic, injection-specific evaluation protocol. The protocol examines detection effectiveness, generalization across operating conditions, robustness to signal degradation, and susceptibility to selected evasion strategies. The findings demonstrate a strong discriminative signal under controlled conditions but also reveal performance limitations under overexposure, low screen brightness, and subject motion, as well as vulnerabilities to selected evasion strategies. By relating detection outcomes to attack types and operating conditions, this study provides an interpretable assessment of when and why active screen illumination succeeds or fails as an IAD mechanism.

</details>


### [208] [How a Model's Architecture and Performance Influences Explainability: A Study of Grad-CAM and CRAFT in CNN-Based Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12075)
*Kilian Göller,Haadia Amjad,Steffen Seitz,Carsten Knoll,Ronald Tetzlaff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察模型性能（训练进程）与架构复杂度对XAI解释质量的影响：在10个ImageNet-1K子集上训练VGG16、ResNet50、ConvNeXt-Tiny，各取10个检查点，用Grad-CAM与CRAFT在Faithfulness、Correctness、Sensitivity、Simplicity四个维度评估。结果显示复杂架构随训练推进faithfulness提升、sensitivity降低，而VGG16解释更简单但faithfulness与correctness较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN决策不透明，在高风险领域（医疗、自主系统）需要可靠的XAI方法；而解释质量可能受模型性能与架构复杂度影响，这一影响此前缺乏系统研究，因此需厘清二者对主流XAI评估指标的作用规律。

Method: 构建统一流水线：取ImageNet-1K的10个子集（各含80个唯一类别），每个子集训练三种结构复杂度递增的CNN——VGG16、ResNet50、ConvNeXt-Tiny；对每个模型取10个准确率递增的训练检查点；采用两类XAI方法——基于特征归因的Grad-CAM与基于概念的CRAFT；在Faithfulness、Correctness、Sensitivity、Simplicity四类指标上评估。

Result: 随训练推进（准确率提升），复杂架构（ResNet50、ConvNeXt-Tiny）的Faithfulness上升、Sensitivity下降，解释更稳健；VGG16结构较简单，其解释更简洁（Simplicity高），但Faithfulness与Correctness偏低。

Conclusion: 模型性能与架构复杂度显著影响XAI解释质量，且不同架构呈现不同权衡（简洁性 vs. 忠实性/正确性）；评估XAI方法时需控制模型性能与架构因素，为后续制定更严谨的XAI评测基准提供了初步依据。

Abstract: Deep learning models, particularly convolutional neural networks (CNNs), are widely adopted in computer vision, yet their opaque decision-making raises concerns in high-risk applications like healthcare and autonomous systems, where trust and accountability are critical. Therefore, robust explainable AI (XAI) methods are desirable to provide transparent insights into model decisions. To ensure these methods are reliable and effective across diverse scenarios, it is important to understand how model performance and architectural complexity influence explanation quality. Our study takes an initial step toward systematically investigating the impact of these two factors on key XAI evaluation metrics. We constructed a pipeline using 10 subsets of ImageNet-1K, each containing 80 unique classes, on each of which we trained three CNN architectures of increasing structural complexity: VGG16, ResNet50, and ConvNeXt-Tiny. For each individual model, we analyzed 10 training checkpoints representing increasing levels of model accuracy using two XAI methods: Grad-CAM (feature-attribution-based) and CRAFT (concept-based). We assessed these methods across four evaluation categories: Faithfulness, Correctness, Sensitivity, and Simplicity. Our findings reveal that as training progresses, the complex architectures (ResNet50 and ConvNeXt-Tiny) exhibit improved Faithfulness and lower Sensitivity, indicating more robust explanations. In contrast, VGG16's simpler architecture yields simpler but less faithful and correct explanations.

</details>


### [209] [Compass 1: Towards Open-World and Persistent Embodied Navigation from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12087)
*Hai Zhang,Siqi Liang,Yanjun Guo,Shoufeng Zhang,Yunhao Wang,Hangyu Du,Haoguang Mai,Hongchen Li,Jiazhi Yang,Jialong Zeng,Yu Hu,Chen Chen,Gangcheng Jiang,Zishun Zhuo,Lirui Zhao,Zhigang Li,Yu Qiao,Zhenjie Yang,Fu Zhang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Compass 1 是首个统一语言理解与视频生成的具身导航基础模型，借助 History Representation（稀疏关键帧+历史表示图）与 History Utilization（结构化VQA监督）实现近2分钟视觉历史回溯，开放世界成功率79.4%（最强基线17.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航架构难以同时维持持久空间感知与长时程执行稳定性，仅能在最近少数帧上有效；作者希望突破"短记忆窗口"瓶颈，实现长时程空间记忆与回溯能力。

Method: 不改变底层架构，引入两部分设计：(1) History Representation——将稀疏视觉关键帧与显式历史表示图结合，保存长期时空上下文；(2) History Utilization——结构化VQA监督协议，含任务阶段定位、探索一致性、时空目标检索三类任务，实现地图 grounded 推理。此外构建单目RGB视频标注流水线，开源500+小时真实交互数据与2000+小时标注网络视频数据。

Result: 在开放世界评测中总体成功率79.4%，最强基线仅17.8%；基础任务零样本执行超90%，探索能力提升250%，困难的历史回溯任务从0%提升至58%。模型主要基于1m高度相机分布训练，可跨轮式机器人(0.35m)、四足机器人(0.5m)、人形(0.85m)、无人机(1.5m)等具身形态及室内至行人密集街道场景泛化。

Conclusion: 统一语言理解与视频生成、并显式建模/利用长时视觉历史的导航基础模型，可显著提升长时程稳定性、探索效率与历史回溯能力，并具备跨具身、跨场景的零样本泛化性。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to achieve persistent spatial awareness and long-horizon execution stability in open-world environments. However, existing navigation architectures struggle to sustain both capabilities, remaining effective only over a handful of recent frames. We present Compass 1, the first embodied navigation foundation model to unify language understanding and video generation while enabling memory backtracking through nearly 2mins of visual history. Without altering the underlying architecture, we introduce History Representation, which integrates sparse visual keyframes with an explicit History Representation Map to preserve long-term spatiotemporal context. To effectively leverage this history, we further introduce History Utilization, a structured VQA supervision protocol comprising three categories: task-stage grounding, exploration consistency, and spatiotemporal target retrieval, enabling map-grounded reasoning and stable persistent long-horizon navigation. To fuel this system, we establish a complete annotation pipeline for monocular RGB videos and open-source a massive dataset featuring over 500 hours of high-quality real-world interactions and over 2,000 hours of annotated web videos. Trained mainly on the distribution of 1m height camera, Compass 1 generalizes seamlessly across diverse embodiments and scenarios, ranging from indoor environments to pedestrian-dense streets, spanning wheeled robot at 0.35m, quadruped dog at 0.5m, humanoid at 0.85m, and aerial drone at 1.5m. Compass 1achieves a dominant overall success rate of 79.4% across open-world evaluations, compared to 17.8% for the strongest baseline. It demonstrates exceptional zero-shot execution exceeding 90% on foundational tasks, a 250% boost in exploration, and an unparalleled leap from 0% to 58% in challenging persistent history backtracking.

</details>


### [210] [RAPA: Ray-Aware Physics Augmentation for Robust Camera-LiDAR Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12129)
*Kyung-Rin Oh,Ji-Hoon Kim,TaeJoon Ha,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出光线感知物理增强(RAPA)，仅需单次棋盘格观测即可生成多样合成样本，将LiDAR侧棋盘格定位转化为柱面反射率图像上的2D检测任务，从而自动化相机-LiDAR标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于靶标的相机-LiDAR标定依赖棋盘格观测，但LiDAR点云稀疏导致棋盘格特征提取困难；现有方法需人工标注3D角点或依赖几何特征提取，在观测不足时不可靠；同时带标注训练数据稀缺。

Method: 将棋盘格定位建模为柱面LiDAR反射率图像上的2D检测问题；提出光线感知物理增强(RAPA)，从单次棋盘格观测生成多样化合成样本以提升检测器的鲁棒性与泛化性。

Result: 在4个独立采集的室内外场景中，mAP@0.5较最强图像空间增强基线提升16.1–43.1个百分点；在242次观测上与免训练靶标方法对比，返回有效靶标位姿的定位数量最多，有效率达96.4%，并可支撑全部4个场景的下游标定。

Conclusion: RAPA通过仅需单个标注靶标观测实现自动化LiDAR侧靶标定位，解决了基于靶标标定的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR calibration is essential for reliable perception in robotic and autonomous systems. Most target-based calibration methods rely on checkerboard observations, but accurate checkerboard feature extraction from LiDAR point clouds remains challenging due to measurement sparsity. Existing approaches either require manual annotation of 3D corners or depend on geometric feature extraction methods, both of which can become unreliable when checkerboard features are insufficiently observed. In this paper, we formulate checkerboard localization as a 2D detection problem using cylindrical LiDAR reflectivity images. To overcome the limited availability of labeled training data, we propose ray-aware physics augmentation (RAPA), which generates diverse synthetic samples from a single checkerboard observation. The generated data are used to improve detector robustness and generalization under varying sensing conditions. Across four independently captured indoor and outdoor locations, the proposed synthetic augmentation exceeds the strongest evaluated image-space augmentation baseline by 16.1-43.1 percentage points in mAP@0.5. Compared with training-free target-based methods on 242 observations, RAPA yields the largest number of valid target localizations, with 96.4% of its returned detections yielding valid target poses. These reliable localizations support downstream camera-LiDAR calibration across all four evaluated locations. Overall, RAPA addresses a key bottleneck in target-based calibration by enabling automated LiDAR-side target localization with only a single annotated target observation.

</details>


### [211] [DisasterVL: Evidence-Based Visual Reasoning Dataset and Model for Disaster Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12144)
*Ali Diba,Aisha Al-Mohannadi,Yin Yang,Muhammad Imran,Ferda Ofli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DisasterVL——约120万图像、2500万结构化标注的灾害影像证据grounded推理数据集与基准，9B DisasterVLM在多项任务上超越通用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害影像理解需要基于可见证据的推理，而现有资源缺乏将答案与视觉证据、区域与文本短语显式关联的大规模结构化标注，故需构建专用数据集与基准。

Method: 数据覆盖142国约120万图像，标注包含场景描述、事件与位置评估、空间关系及VQA；VQA标注将答案链接到可见证据，涵盖线索存在/缺失、多答案格式与难度标签；区域级标注连接 caption 短语与边界框及空间关系。测试集38,254图像、75国、28类灾害，经Gemini 3.1 Pro审核并人工分层校验；训练9B DisasterVLM并对比通用基线。

Result: DisasterVLM在captioning、开放式VQA、事件推理与区域级grounding上优于通用基线；文本重叠指标提升幅度大于证据评分提升。

Conclusion: DisasterVL为证据grounded的灾害影像推理提供了大规模基准；文本指标增益大于证据指标提示模型在视觉证据对齐上仍存差距，数据集、模型与代码将公开。

Abstract: We introduce DisasterVL, a dataset and benchmark for evidence-grounded reasoning about disaster imagery. It contains approximately 1.2 million images from 142 countries, paired with 25 million structured annotations covering scene captions, incident and location assessments, spatial relationships, and visual question answering (VQA). The VQA annotations link answers to visible evidence and include questions about both present and absent cues, multiple answer formats, and difficulty labels. Region-level annotations link caption phrases to bounding boxes and spatial relationships to the regions they connect. The test set contains 38,254 images spanning 75 countries and 28 hazard types. Gemini 3.1 Pro audited the test annotations, and human reviewers validated a stratified subset. On this benchmark, the 9B DisasterVLM model outperforms the evaluated general-purpose baselines on captioning, open-ended VQA, incident reasoning, and region-level grounding. Gains in text overlap are larger than gains in evidence scores. We plan to publicly release the dataset, trained models, and code.

</details>


### [212] [EgoLabel: Zero-Shot Egocentric Auto-Labeling with Cross-Modal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12149)
*Xiaozhi Zhang,Changjia Xu,Yaoyuan Yan,Haoxiang Jie,Bingjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本第一视角自动标注流程EgoLabel，整合现成模型并提供跨模态配准与一致性诊断，手部深度锚定使逐序列中位残差降低23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立开发的深度、相机位姿、物体与人体姿态模型在单位、坐标约定和尺度上不一致，导致各自看似合理的预测在几何上互相矛盾，需要显式的跨模态配准和一致性诊断。

Method: EgoLabel零样本流水线：MonST3R负责几何，LocateAnything结合GMC-BoT-SORT与SAM 2负责物体，V-HPOT负责手部，FisheyeViT作全身兼容性演示；主手部评测使用H2O传感器深度，采用逐手深度锚定并输出残差诊断。

Result: 20个序列上，逐手深度锚定相对单一全局尺度将逐序列中位手-深度残差的均值降低23%，合并中位数为84.6 mm（真值参考20.5 mm）；手部PA-MPJPE为14.53±1.29 mm，而绝对MPJPE为119.1±31.3 mm；三个纯RGB序列Sim(3)对齐后相机ATE为1.40±0.29 cm。

Conclusion: 对齐精度与度量精度存在显著差距；手部锚定与残差共用同一深度图，故残差反映内部一致性而非独立精度；另发现滑动窗口实现问题会使传感器初始化深度在优化中漂移，整体刻画了配准流水线的效用与当前局限。

Abstract: Egocentric auto-labeling requires depth, camera pose, object, and human-pose estimates to agree across independently developed models. Differences in units, coordinate conventions, and scale can make individually plausible predictions geometrically inconsistent. We present \textbf{EgoLabel}, a zero-shot pipeline that combines off-the-shelf models without target-domain labeled training and provides explicit cross-modal registration and consistency diagnostics. The pipeline integrates MonST3R for geometry, LocateAnything with GMC-BoT-SORT and SAM~2 for objects, and V-HPOT for hands; FisheyeViT provides a separate whole-body compatibility demonstration. Our main hand evaluation uses H2O sensor depth. Across 20 sequences, per-hand depth anchoring reduces the mean of per-sequence median hand--depth residuals by $23\%$ relative to a single global scale, yielding a pooled median of $84.6\,\mathrm{mm}$ against a $20.5\,\mathrm{mm}$ ground-truth reference. Hand PA-MPJPE is $14.53\pm1.29\,\mathrm{mm}$, whereas absolute MPJPE is $119.1\pm31.3\,\mathrm{mm}$, revealing a substantial gap between aligned and metric accuracy. On three RGB-only sequences, camera ATE is $1.40\pm0.29\,\mathrm{cm}$ after $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ alignment. We also identify a sliding-window implementation issue that allows sensor-initialized depth to change during optimization. Because hand anchoring and its residual share the same depth map, the residual measures internal consistency rather than independent accuracy. These results characterize both the utility and the current limits of the registration pipeline.

</details>


### [213] [CADMA: Capacity-Aware Coverage Decomposition for Generative Model Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12243)
*Debin Meng,Zheng Gao,Ziquan Liu,Yanran Li,Ioannis Patras,Georgios Tzimiropoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CADMA，一种容量感知的召回型评估框架，通过真实与生成样本间的局部容量匹配，将几何覆盖与有效覆盖分离，实现可区分模式丢失与模式崩塌的多维覆盖诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型评估不仅需衡量样本保真度，还需衡量生成集对真实分布的覆盖程度。现有召回类指标常将分布覆盖简化为几何覆盖，仅给出单一聚合分数，无法区分模式丢失与模式崩塌等关键多样性失效，且可能对覆盖很差的生成分布给出过高评分，削弱了诊断价值与可解释性。

Method: CADMA 通过真实样本与生成样本之间的局部容量匹配来评估分布覆盖，将几何覆盖（geometry-based coverage）与有效覆盖（effective coverage）解耦，从而输出多维度的覆盖诊断量，用于量化模式丢失与模式崩塌。

Result: 在玩具数据、真实数据集及多种生成模型上的大量实验表明，相比现有指标，CADMA 给出更可靠的覆盖估计，并对多样性失效提供更细粒度、更可解释的诊断信号，同时缓解几何型指标的过度乐观偏差。

Conclusion: CADMA 为召回导向的生成模型评估提供了容量感知的统一框架，兼顾可靠性估计与细粒度诊断，代码已开源。

Abstract: Evaluating generative models requires measuring not only sample fidelity but also how well a generated set covers the real data distribution. Recent work has therefore introduced recall-oriented metrics for distribution-level evaluation. However, existing recall metrics often reduce distributional coverage to geometry-based coverage. This simplifying assumption weakens their diagnostic value and interpretability in two ways: these metrics provide only a single aggregate score that cannot distinguish essential diversity failures such as mode dropping and mode collapse, and geometry-based coverage can provide unreliable estimates by assigning high scores to poorly covered generative distributions. We therefore introduce CADMA, a capacity-aware framework for recall-oriented evaluation and diagnosis. CADMA evaluates distributional coverage through local capacity matching between real and generated samples, separating geometry-based coverage from effective coverage. This yields multi-dimensional coverage diagnostics that quantify mode dropping and mode collapse while reducing the overly optimistic estimates produced by geometry-based metrics. Through extensive experiments on toy data, real-world datasets, and generative models, we show that CADMA provides more reliable coverage estimates and more fine-grained, interpretable diagnostic signals of diversity failures than existing metrics. Our code is available at https://github.com/Open-Debin/CADMA_NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [214] [A forensic evaluation framework for egocentric foundation models: an EFM3D case study for crime scene reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12259)
*Davidson Chisom Onwubiko,Shahana Bano,Carlos Francisco Moreno-García*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出面向第一人称视角基础模型的结构化法医评估框架，并以EFM3D为例，在四个模拟室内犯罪现场上进行房间级重建、语义理解、计算性能与法医适用性评测，结论是EFM3D宜作情境记录与现场复核的辅助工具，而非高保真法医采集的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型日益融合几何、语义与空间上下文用于三维场景理解，但其在法医犯罪现场重建中的适用性尚不明确，缺乏可复现的评估方法。

Method: 构建包含四维度的结构化法医评估框架（房间尺度重建、语义场景理解、计算性能、法医适用性）；在Robert Gordon大学East Lodge House法医培训设施内，用Meta Project Aria Gen 1录制四个模拟室内犯罪现场，对EFM3D进行评测；因缺少参考网格与专家标注的三维真值，输出仅作描述性统计；并采用七项准则的能力画像。

Result: 四个场景平均生成12,668±2,430个顶点、24,838±4,903个面片的网格；共39,529条语义预测，覆盖21–24类，平均置信度0.40；最终推理每场景处理1,388±203个片段，速度0.69±0.01 samples/s，单次录制耗时50.77±6.12分钟。七项能力画像显示，房间几何、家具定位、场景上下文与空间关系的支持较强，而对细尺度法医物证的支持较弱。

Conclusion: EFM3D定位为情境记录与现场复核的补充工具，不能替代高保真法医采集；该框架为在专业法医场景中评估未来第一人称基础模型提供了可复现基础。

Abstract: Foundation models increasingly combine geometry, semantics, and spatial context for three-dimensional scene understanding, but their suitability for forensic crime scene reconstruction remains unclear. This study presents a structured forensic evaluation framework for egocentric foundation models and demonstrates it using the Egocentric Foundation Model for 3D (EFM3D). Four simulated indoor crime scenes were recorded with Meta Project Aria Gen~1 at Robert Gordon University's East Lodge House forensic training facility. The framework evaluates room-scale reconstruction, semantic scene understanding, computational performance, and forensic suitability. Across the four scenes, EFM3D generated meshes averaging $12{,}668\pm2{,}430$ vertices and $24{,}838\pm4{,}903$ faces, with 39,529 recorded semantic predictions spanning 21--24 categories at a mean confidence of 0.40. Because reference meshes and expert-labelled 3D ground truth were unavailable, these statistics are treated as descriptive outputs rather than measures of geometric or detection accuracy. Final inference runs processed $1{,}388\pm203$ snippets per scene at $0.69\pm0.01$ samples/s and required $50.77\pm6.12$ minutes per recording. A seven-criterion capability profile indicated stronger support for room geometry, furniture localisation, scene context, and spatial relationships than for fine-scale forensic evidence. The results position EFM3D as a complementary tool for contextual documentation and scene review rather than a replacement for high-fidelity forensic capture. The framework provides a reproducible basis for evaluating future egocentric foundation models in specialised forensic settings.

</details>


### [215] [HumanSightBench: A Benchmark for Human Visual Perception in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12305)
*Zixin Su,Yangguang Lin,Zegang Peng,Yuntong Sun,Xuanyi Wang,Jiahao Wang,Qiankai Xu,Bing Yuan,Jinze Bai,Youbin Wu,Jingjia Huang,Ruyang Liu,Yanxu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanSightBench，一个包含 2,876 道短答案视觉问答、覆盖 7 个大类与 21 个子类的基准。其设计原则是"构造上人类容易、评估上模型困难"，通过五道纳入门槛（问题清晰、答案唯一、视觉可定位、人类可解、纯文本捷径过滤）控制歧义与外部知识混淆，以揭示 MLLM 与人类视觉感知之间的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在知识密集与推理型视觉语言基准上表现强劲，却仍难以回答人类轻松解决的视觉问题。现有端到端评测混合多种能力，且难以确认单条题目是否有效，导致观测到的错误可能源于视觉感知、语言先验、背景知识、问题歧义或答案欠明确等多种混杂因素，使 MLLM 与人类感知水平的差异难以刻画。

Method: 构建 HumanSightBench：2,876 道短答案视觉问题，覆盖 7 个顶层感知类别、21 个子类。采"人类易构造、模型难评估"的设计准则，设置五道纳入门槛筛选题目：问题清晰性、答案唯一性、视觉 grounding、人类可解性、纯文本捷径过滤。依据协议，错误定义为在经人类验证的视觉任务上、使用被评测接口时给出错误答案；门槛旨在减少歧义与外部知识带来的混杂。对八个 MLLM 进行评测。

Result: 八个被评测 MLLM 中最佳 pass@3 仅为 65.6%；在 Counting 类别上没有任何模型超过 27.5%。

Conclusion: 总体分数掩盖了细粒度能力缺口，该基准能够显式暴露这些差距，可用于后续训练实验与留出评估。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on knowledge-intensive and reasoning-based vision-language benchmark datasets, but still struggle to solve the visual questions that people can solve easily. Evaluating the differences between MLLMs and human-level visual perception is challenging due to end-to-end evaluation which combines various skills, or because we are uncertain if individual items are valid. Specifically, observed errors may reflect visual perception, language priors, background knowledge, question ambiguity, and underspecified answers. Our guideline for HumanSightBench is that we construct items that are human-easy by construction and model-hard by evaluation. HumanSightBench contains 2,876 visual questions with short answers across 7 top-level perceptual categories and 21 subcategories. We only include items that pass five inclusion gates: question clarity, answer uniqueness, visual grounding, human solvability, and text-only shortcut filtering. According to the protocol, errors are defined as incorrect answers on human-validated visual tasks with the evaluated interface. The inclusion gates are intended to reduce confounds from ambiguity and external knowledge. The best pass@3 score is 65.6% across eight evaluated MLLMs. No evaluated model exceeds 27.5% on Counting. These results identify capability gaps that are hidden by overall scores and can help future training experiments and held-out evaluation.

</details>


### [216] [TMT: Adapting CLIP to Infrared Imagery via Text-Derived Modality Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12345)
*Ruofan Wang,Puzuo Wang,Xin Chen,Zibo Yu,Jungang Yang,Yi Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TMT方法，仅用成对红外-可见图像将CLIP适配到红外域，借助文本定义的红外→可见方向迁移模态知识，并以生成-感知协同监督增强语义与细节表示，在零样本IR分类、检索与开放词汇分割上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP的图文对齐在红外图像上不可靠，因为大规模预训练以可见光谱数据为主；而红外-文本标注成本高昂，因此希望仅利用成对红外-可见图像完成适配，同时兼顾模态特定知识、场景内容保持与CLIP原有图文对齐能力。

Method: TMT（Text-Derived Modality Knowledge Transfer）：以文本嵌入差异所定义的红外→可见方向引导可见与红外模态间的知识迁移，从而不抹除红外特有信息；并引入生成-感知协同监督，结合CLIP可见图像编码器的语义对齐与扩散模型噪声预测反馈，增强红外图像的语义与细节表示；适配对象为CLIP图像编码器。

Result: 在四个源域不相交的红外分类基准上取得最高平均零样本精度48.92%；在适配阶段无红外-文本监督条件下取得对比方法中最优的红外-文本检索结果；在CART与SemanticRT上取得CLIP类方法中最高的开放词汇分割mIoU。

Conclusion: 文本嵌入差异可有效刻画模态差异并指导跨模态知识迁移，配合生成-感知协同监督，可在低成本（仅成对图像）监督下将CLIP可靠适配到红外域，并泛化至分类、检索与分割多种下游任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP achieve strong performance across computer vision tasks, yet their image-text alignment is less reliable for infrared (IR) imagery because large-scale pre-training is dominated by visible-spectrum data. Due to the high cost of the infrared-text labeling, we investigate adapting CLIP to IR through only paired infrared-visible images, which requires considering modality-specific knowledge while preserving scene content and CLIP's image-text alignment. To achieve this, we propose TMT (Text-Derived Modality Knowledge Transfer), which adapts CLIP's image encoder through a text-defined infrared-to-visible direction and supervision from paired IR-visible images. First, we found that modality discrepancy can be described by corresponding text embeddings, thus using the direction of text embedding differences to guide the knowledge transfer between visible and infrared modalities without erasing infrared-specific information. To enhance the representation of semantics and details of infrared images, we introduce Generative-Perceptual Co-Supervision that combines semantic alignment from CLIP visible image encoder with diffusion noise-prediction feedbacks. On four source-disjoint IR classification benchmarks, TMT achieves the highest mean zero-shot accuracy among the compared methods (48.92%). It also achieves the best IR-text retrieval results among the compared methods without infrared-text supervision during adaptation and the highest open-vocabulary segmentation mIoU on both CART and SemanticRT among the CLIP-like methods.

</details>


### [217] [ReVMap: Vectorized Global Mapping via Connectivity-Aware Local Map Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12346)
*Hongjae Shin,Jungho Kim,Donghyuk Kwak,Myeongjun Kim,Seunghoon Yu,Jiyong Oh,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVMap是一个可靠的局部到全局矢量HD地图自动标注框架，通过不确定性引导的先验利用与连通性引导的全局地图修正，迭代耦合局部预测与全局构建，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2上取得局部预测与全局构建的双重SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模矢量高清地图标注成本高昂，现有局部到全局自动标注方法忽略预测不确定性、依赖启发式地图融合，容易传播误差并造成拓扑不一致。

Method: 1) 利用逐点定位不确定性，将全局地图先验注入局部预测，提升对不准确先验的鲁棒性；2) 预测局部到全局连通性，基于局部预测可靠性进行不确定性感知的融合，选择性更新全局地图；3) 迭代耦合局部预测与全局修正，逐步提升局部精度与全局一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes与Argoverse 2基准上，局部地图预测和全局地图构建均达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 以不确定性建模和连通性引导为核心的迭代式局部—全局框架能有效抑制误差传播、保证拓扑一致性，为可扩展的HD地图自动标注提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Large-scale annotation of vectorized high-definition (HD) maps is labor-intensive and costly, motivating local-to-global auto-labeling approaches that incrementally construct global maps from local vectorized predictions. However, existing approaches often overlook prediction uncertainty and rely on heuristic map integration, potentially propagating errors and introducing topological inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose ReVMap, a reliable local-to-global framework that iteratively refines local map predictions and global map construction through uncertainty-guided prior utilization and connectivity-guided global map revision. Specifically, ReVMap leverages point-wise localization uncertainty to incorporate global map priors into local map prediction, improving robustness against inaccurate prior information. It then predicts local-to-global connectivity and selectively updates the global map through uncertainty-aware fusion based on the reliability of local predictions. By iteratively coupling local prediction and global revision, ReVMap progressively improves both local map accuracy and global map consistency. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 benchmarks demonstrate that ReVMap achieves state-of-the-art performance in both local map prediction and global map construction.

</details>


### [218] [QCC-WM: Linking Macro-State Dynamics and Visual Generation via Quotient Causal Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12353)
*Zhizhong Fu,Wei Zhou,Zhaoyang Jiang,Yudong Lin,Songlun Li,Zhi Li,Feifan Chen,Gaoming Li,Xiaorong Ding,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出QCC-WM，一种基于商式因果构造（Quotient-based Causal Construction）的视觉世界模型，用动作响应引导宏观变量构造并进行递归动作条件预测，通过共享参考商测度约束预测状态与观测变化的一致性，在US-VLA数据集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界建模的关键挑战在于：需从高维观测中构造支持动作下递归预测的宏观状态，同时保证其预测演化与生成观测中的视觉变化保持一致。

Method: 采用平移与旋转动作及其预测响应，通过因果抽象将局部视觉特征聚合为具有空间对应关系的宏观节点；在共享参考测度下递归预测节点内容与运动；宏观级预测引导未来观测生成，预测运动将历史外观信息进行传输；再将生成观测与宏观目标之间的节点级误差映射回对应空间区域进行校正。

Result: 在US-VLA数据集上，四步预测ROI-MSE为0.01861；相较各预测时域最佳基线，四步预测与八步、十六步递归外推的LPIPS分别降低48.59%、41.68%、30.23%。消融与机制分析表明，运动引导的参考传输主要提升像素重建，节点级误差反馈进一步改善感知质量及生成观测与宏观预测的一致性。

Conclusion: QCC-WM通过商式因果构造与共享参考测度，实现了宏观状态预测与视觉生成的紧密耦合，验证了运动引导参考传输与节点级误差反馈在重建质量与一致性上的互补作用。

Abstract: A key challenge in action-conditioned world modeling is to construct macro-states from high-dimensional observations that support recursive prediction under actions, while keeping their predicted evolution consistent with visual changes in generated observations. We propose QCC-WM, a visual world model based on Quotient-based Causal Construction. QCC-WM uses action responses to guide macro-variable construction for recursive action-conditioned prediction. A shared reference quotient measurement constrains the consistency between predicted states and observed changes, allowing macro-level predictions to provide structured constraints for future visual generation.
  Using translational and rotational actions and their predicted responses, the model aggregates local visual features into macro-nodes with spatial correspondence through causal abstraction, and recursively predicts node content and motion under a shared reference measurement. Macro-level predictions guide future observation generation, while predicted motion transports historical appearance information. Node-level errors between generated observations and macro-level targets are then mapped back to the corresponding spatial regions for correction, improving consistency between visual generation and macro-level predictions.
  On the US-VLA dataset, QCC-WM achieves an ROI-MSE of 0.01861 for four-step prediction. Compared with the best baseline at each prediction horizon, it reduces LPIPS by 48.59%, 41.68%, and 30.23% for four-step prediction and eight- and sixteen-step recursive extrapolation, respectively. Ablation studies and mechanism analyses show that motion-guided reference transport mainly improves pixel reconstruction, while node-level error feedback further improves perceptual quality and consistency between generated observations and macro-level predictions.

</details>


### [219] [TRIAD: Multimodal Retrieval across Abstraction, Constraints, and State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12364)
*Xiangyang Wu,Chen Ju,Xu Chen,Likang Peng,Jinsong Lan,Haihong Tang,Yang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态检索基准 TRIAD，用于评测检索系统能否满足跨层级、多约束、跨对话轮次演化的复杂请求，包含六个子集；评测 17 个系统的 20 种配置后发现没有任何系统在六个子集上全面领先，多轮与纯负向约束场景性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向智能体系统的多模态检索请求常涉及跨层级关系、多重约束与对话轮次演化；此时"高度相似"的结果仍可能违反用户要求，且检索内容可能未经人工筛选直接驱动下游动作，因此需要评测系统是否真正"满足完整请求"而非仅"检索相似内容"。

Method: 构建 TRIAD 基准，含三条评测轨：abstraction（视觉实例与不同层级概念间的双向检索）、constraints（正负条件联合满足）、state（跨对话轮次整合需求并在用户修改时更新早期条件）。基准共六个子集，每子集 1,000 条查询、每查询固定 1,000 个候选池，统一采用多正例 mAP 协议；辅以更简单的参照任务和按违反需求类型分组的难负例来刻画具体检索难点。

Result: 评测 17 个系统的 20 种配置：无系统在全部六个子集领先；性能随层级距离增大而下降；相同约束数下纯负向查询普遍劣于纯正向查询；在表达同一最终意图的成对任务中，40 个"配置-领域"比较里有 39 个多轮检索 mAP 更低；显式推理的收益因模型与任务而异。

Conclusion: 现有检索系统在"检索相似内容"与"满足完整请求"之间仍存在明显差距，尤其在层级抽象、负向约束与多轮状态更新场景下表现不足，亟需针对请求满足度而非相似度的建模与评测。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval increasingly supports agentic systems, where user requests can involve cross-level relations, combine multiple constraints, and evolve across dialogue turns. In these settings, a highly similar result may still violate the user's requirements, while retrieved content may directly inform downstream actions without human screening. We introduce TRIAD, a multimodal retrieval benchmark for evaluating how well retrieval systems satisfy these complex, evolving requests. TRIAD comprises three tracks: abstraction, testing bidirectional retrieval between visual instances and concepts at different hierarchical levels; constraints, testing the joint satisfaction of positive and negative conditions; and state, testing the integration of requirements across dialogue turns and the updating of earlier conditions when users revise their requests. The benchmark contains six subsets, each with 1,000 queries and a fixed pool of 1,000 candidates per query, evaluated using a common multi-positive mean average precision (mAP) protocol. Simpler reference tasks and hard negatives grouped by the requirements they violate help characterize specific retrieval difficulties. We evaluate 20 configurations of 17 systems and find that no system leads across all six subsets. Performance declines with hierarchical distance, and negative-only queries generally underperform positive-only queries with the same number of constraints. Across paired tasks expressing the same final intent, multi-turn retrieval yields lower mAP in 39 of 40 configuration-domain comparisons. The benefits of explicit reasoning also vary across models and tasks. These findings highlight the gap between retrieving similar content and satisfying a complete request.

</details>


### [220] [PTC-IoU: CPU-Friendly Detection Quality Estimation for Edge Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12405)
*Wei Geng,Hyerin Kim,Chang Lu,Zhenghao Lu,Joerg Ott*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PTC-IoU（Predictive Temporal Consistency IoU），一种将检测质量估计工作负载从 GPU 转移到 CPU 的确定性估计器，通过压缩检测框历史实现结果打分，在 Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano 上显著降低时延、提升吞吐并大幅降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视频分析在运行时无真值标签，需估计本地检测质量以决定哪些帧需上报云端强模型。检测器给出的置信度分数无法揭示漏检目标和错位边界框，因此需要额外质量估计器；而基于神经网络（NN）的估计器会加重已在运行检测的 GPU 负担，同时 CPU 算力可能闲置。

Method: 提出 Predictive Temporal Consistency IoU（PTC-IoU）：将质量估计负载迁移至 CPU，通过压缩检测框历史（compacting detection box histories）并利用时序一致性进行确定性结果评分，替代神经网络估计器。

Result: 在 Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano 上，相比神经网络估计器：单帧估计时延由 10.3 ms 降至 2.2 ms（4.7 倍加速），流水线吞吐提升 13.6%，估计器能耗最多降低 95%；同时在多个数据集上验证了具有竞争力的最终检测质量。

Conclusion: PTC-IoU 表明，将质量估计从 GPU 卸载到 CPU 的确定性时序一致性方法，可在保持检测质量的同时显著降低时延与能耗并提升吞吐，是一种适用于边缘视频分析的高效质量估计方案。

Abstract: An edge video analytics system must estimate the quality of local detections since there are no ground-truth labels available during runtime. Detectors usually provide confidence scores for their results, but these scores can not reveal every missed object or misplaced bounding box. So a quality estimator is necessary to provide a decision signal indicating which frame has potential detection failures that need to be escalated to a more powerful model in the cloud. Neural network based estimators add workload to the GPU already running detection, while CPU capacity may remain available. We present Predictive Temporal Consistency IoU (PTC-IoU), which moves this workload to CPU by compacting detection boxes histories to support deterministic result scoring. Comparing to neural counterparts, experiments on representative edge hardware, Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano, show that our method reduced per-frame estimator latency from 10.3 ms for neural counterparts to 2.2 ms (4.7x faster), increased pipelined throughput by 13.6%, and cuts estimator energy consumption by up to 95%, while maintaining competitive final detection quality verified on multiple datasets.

</details>


### [221] [PhysOPSD: Code as Privilege for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12411)
*Harold Haodong Chen,Rui Peng,Lingteng Qiu,Kejie Qiu,Chao Xu,Zilong Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysOPSD 提出以可执行代码作为视频生成中的特权物理上下文，配合 PhysOPSD-Engine 与双重物理特权、共享噪声下的受控物理变化，在仅 3K 样本下显著提升物理合理性并保持通用生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频生成模型视觉保真度高，但生成物理合理动态与复杂交互时脆弱。针对性后训练必要，但 SFT 存在离线状态不匹配，RL 受稀疏且定义不良的物理奖励限制。OPSD 虽能结合 on-policy rollout 与稠密纠正反馈，但核心难点转为设计有效特权上下文：文本提示只是声明式，参考视频则把物理规律与外观纠缠且缺乏参数化控制。

Method: 以可执行代码作为特权物理上下文。由 PhysOPSD-Engine 将真实物理场景转译为可验证的仿真程序；采用 (i) 双层级物理特权，通过中间瓶颈桥接信息鸿沟；(ii) 在共享 rollout 噪声下施加受控物理变化，以暴露因果动态差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，PhysOPSD 在多种骨干网络上显著提升物理合理性，仅用 3K 样本即优于 SFT 与 RL 基线，同时保持通用视频生成质量。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种可编辑、具内在抽象性与可控性的特权物理上下文，能有效支撑 on-policy 自蒸馏后训练，是提升视频生成物理一致性的高效路径。

Abstract: While modern video generative models achieve remarkable visual fidelity, they remain noticeably brittle when generating physically plausible dynamics and complex interactions. Targeted post-training is crucial, yet supervised fine-tuning suffers from offline state mismatch, and reinforcement learning is hindered by sparse, ill-defined physical reward functions. On-Policy (Self-)Distillation (OPSD) reconciles on-policy rollouts with dense corrective feedback, shifting the core challenge to designing effective privileged context. However, conventional modalities fall short: text prompts are inherently declarative, whereas real reference videos heavily entangle physical laws with visual appearance without parametric control. To this end, we propose PhysOPSD, introducing executable code as privileged physical context for video generation. Unlike static visual observations, executable code instantiates an editable physical world with intrinsic abstraction and controllability. Powered by PhysOPSD-Engine, we translate real-world physical scenarios into verifiable simulation programs. PhysOPSD then incorporates (i) dual-level physical privilege to bridge the information gap via an intermediate bottleneck, alongside (ii) controlled physical variations under shared rollout noise to expose causal dynamic shifts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PhysOPSD substantially improves physical plausibility across diverse backbones, outperforming SFT and RL baselines with only $3$K samples, while preserving general video generation quality.

</details>


### [222] [dNaViT: Discrete Codes are General-Purpose Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12426)
*Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Jialv Zou,Minhao Jing,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Hai Huang,Bo Zhao,Yan Feng,Peng Pei,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 dNaViT，一个原生分辨率的离散视觉 Transformer，用有限离散词表将视觉世界表示为视觉 token，在 12 个理解基准、4 个生成基准与 1 个编辑基准上匹配或超越任务专用基线，证明紧凑离散词表可作为通用视觉“货币”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散自回归建模（next-token prediction）是当代语言模型的基石，将其扩展到视觉可使视觉与语言模型收敛到统一框架，共享方法论与基础设施。核心问题：有限的离散词表能在多大程度上表征视觉世界的丰富性。

Method: 基于冻结的预训练编码器构建原生分辨率离散 ViT；采用轻量残差调制与双分支目标，将重建相关线索注入语义特征；利用分组乘积量化（grouped product quantization）在不增加空间序列长度的前提下扩展离散容量；配合平衡训练策略同时保留语义与像素级信息，从而同时充当通用离散视觉 tokenizer 与高保真图像解码器。

Result: 在 12 个视觉理解基准、4 个生成基准以及 1 个图像编辑基准上，dNaViT 与任务专用基线持平或更优；单一离散 tokenizer 可统一支撑下游编码与高保真解码。

Conclusion: 紧凑的离散词表足以作为通用视觉表征单元，支持完全统一的原生多模态方法，为视觉-语言统一建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Discrete autoregressive modeling, notably through next-token prediction, has become a cornerstone of modern language models. Extending this paradigm to vision allows visual and language models to converge on a unified framework, thereby fostering shared methodology and infrastructure. This work investigates a central question: how far can a finite discrete vocabulary go in representing the richness of the visual world? We present dNaViT, a Discrete Native-resolution Vision Transformer that produces discrete visual primitives analogous to language tokens. Built upon a frozen pretrained encoder, dNaViT applies lightweight residual modulation with a dual-branch objective to inject reconstruction-aware cues into semantic features. By leveraging grouped product quantization, it scales discrete capacity without inflating spatial sequence lengths, while a balanced training recipe maintains both semantic and pixel-level information. Consequently, dNaViT functions as a versatile discrete visual tokenizer, enabling unified encoding across downstream tasks alongside high-fidelity image decoding. Extensive evaluations show that dNaViT matches or exceeds task-specific baselines across 12 visual understanding benchmarks, 4 generation benchmarks, and an image editing benchmark. These findings demonstrate that a compact discrete vocabulary can serve as a universal visual currency and unlock the full potential of fully unified, native multimodal methodology.

</details>


### [223] [Occupancy Modelling of Biased Landslide Inventories: When Does Separating Occurrence from Recording Improve on Neutralisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12437)
*Abdirashid Omar,Abdikadir Ugaas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将生态学占域-探测模型引入滑坡易发性建模，把滑坡"发生"与"记录"分离，并与传统偏差变量中性化方法比较；模型在纠正道路偏差和恢复真实滑坡率上优势明显，但对最佳中性化模型的优势不稳定，可由一个低交互间隙指标预判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡易发性模型训练所用的滑坡编录只记录"被观测到"的滑坡，而非真实发生的滑坡，存在报告偏差。既有补救方法是拟合偏差描述变量并在制图时中性化，其适用条件尚不清楚。作者希望检验能否借助多份不完美编录、显式分离"发生"与"记录"来改进纠偏。

Method: 迁移生态学的占域-探测模型（occupancy-detection），将多个滑坡编录视为同一事件的多个不完美观测者，分离发生概率与记录概率。使用2018北海道胆振东部、2015尼泊尔Gorkha、2010海地三次地震的近完备编录并注入模拟报告偏差，另用三份真实Gorkha编录；与标准做法（偏差变量拟合并中性化）比较，并做移植实验与"交互间隙"指标检验。

Result: 相对标准做法，占域模型在25组配对抽样中的24组降低道路偏差；AUC在Gorkha提升0.08–0.14、海地0.03、胆振东部仅0.01–0.02。预测/真实滑坡率中位数0.99（0.44–1.89），基线为0.24–0.30；用同一锚定面积重标定基线可修正中位数，但误差范围扩大到0.20–4.34。单元级概率仍偏极端（校准斜率0.59）。与最佳中性化模型相比，AUC在胆振东部高0.03–0.07，在Gorkha与海地低0.01–0.05。移植实验表明胆振东部的优势源于其真实滑坡空间格局结构，而非地形或道路网络。在全部8个低交互间隙设定（可由有偏记录计算）中，中性化至少同样准确，包括海地的预设检验；高间隙并不保证占域模型占优。真实Gorkha编录呈坡度偏差而非道路偏差，模型无实质增益。

Conclusion: 占域-探测建模能有效去除道路类报告偏差并更准确地还原真实滑坡率，但对已优化中性化模型的增益不确定，取决于真实滑坡格局结构和偏差-易发性交互强度；可用低交互间隙指标事先判断是否值得采用。基于报告的编录仍是最主要的未检验场景。

Abstract: Landslide susceptibility models are trained on inventories that record where landslides were seen, not where they occurred. The established remedy fits bias-describing variables and neutralises them when mapping. We adapt occupancy-detection modelling from ecology, treating several inventories as imperfect observers of one event and separating occurrence from recording, and ask when this improves on neutralisation. We use near-complete inventories of the 2018 Hokkaido Eastern Iburi, 2015 Gorkha and 2010 Haiti earthquakes with simulated reporting bias, and three real Gorkha inventories. Compared with standard practice, the occupancy model reduces road bias in 24 of 25 paired draws and raises AUC by 0.08-0.14 on Gorkha and 0.03 on Haiti, but only 0.01-0.02 on Iburi. Its median predicted-to-true landslide rate is 0.99 (range 0.44-1.89), against 0.24-0.30 for the baselines. Rescaling the baselines with the same anchor area corrects their median but leaves larger errors (range 0.20-4.34). Cell-level probabilities remain too extreme (calibration slope 0.59). Compared with the best neutralised model, AUC is higher on Iburi (by 0.03-0.07) and lower on Gorkha and Haiti (by 0.01-0.05). Transplant experiments tie the Iburi advantage to the structure of its true landslide pattern, not to its terrain or road network. In all eight settings with a low interaction gap, computable from biased records, neutralisation was at least as accurate, including a prespecified test on Haiti; a high gap did not guarantee an advantage. The real Gorkha inventories are slope-biased, not road-biased, and there the model brings no meaningful gain. Report-based catalogues remain the main untested case.

</details>


### [224] [MoSTE: Mixture of Spatio-Temporal Experts for Deepfake Detection in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12443)
*Charbel Yahchouchi,Nabyl Quignon,Noemi Roggero,Laurent Saroul,Antitza Dantcheva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合时空专家框架MoSTE，通过无监督token级路由将适配分解为空间与时间并行专家池，实现深度伪造视频检测的条件化表征专化，在跨数据集与跨操纵泛化上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造视频的伪造痕迹随操纵类型差异巨大，且常混合多种空间与时间不一致性。现有参数高效适配方法依赖单一共享表征，易产生操纵类型偏置，难以泛化到未见伪造类型。

Method: 将适配分解为并行的空间专家池与时间专家池，采用无监督的token级路由机制动态将token分配给最相关专家，无需操纵标签或多阶段训练；同时将CLIP表征有效迁移至视频域。

Result: 在跨数据集与跨操纵基准上，平均泛化性能刷新SOTA，同时在各类操纵家族上保持强劲性能；专家路由与注意力分析显示出现专家多样性，表明学到互补的时空取证特征。

Conclusion: 条件化、多样化的专家专化是提升深度伪造视频泛化检测的有效途径，MoSTE以参数高效方式实现互补时空取证表征的自发涌现。

Abstract: Generalizable deepfake video detection remains challenging because forgery artifacts vary substantially across manipulation types and often combine diverse spatial and temporal inconsistencies. Existing parameter-efficient adaptation methods typically rely on a single shared representation, rendering them prone to manipulation-specific bias and limiting transfer to unseen forgeries. To address this challenge, we propose Mixture Of Spatio-Temporal Experts (MoSTE), an adaptation framework that enables conditional specialization of forensic representations. Instead of forcing one adapter to model all forgery patterns, MoSTE decomposes adaptation into parallel spatial and temporal expert pools and dynamically routes tokens to the most relevant experts through an unsupervised token-level routing mechanism. This allows experts to naturally specialize in complementary forgery cues without requiring explicit manipulation labels or multi-stage training, while effectively transferring representations from CLIP to the video domain. Extensive experiments across cross-dataset and cross-manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MoSTE sets a new state of the art in average generalization while preserving strong performance across diverse manipulation families. Additional analysis of expert routing and attention behavior reveals the emergence of expert diversity, suggesting that MoSTE learns complementary spatio-temporal forensic features. Code is available at https://github.com/charbelyah/MoSTE

</details>


### [225] [LongSCOP: Semantically Consistent Long Video Outpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12445)
*Yu Tang,Zhiyuan You,Zhixiao Wang,Fanghua Yu,Qingyu Zhang,Xiang Yin,Chao Dong,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongSCOP：面向长视频外扩的分块迭代生成框架，通过稳定化训练配方与VLM智能体参考帧选择，解决块间不一致、误差累积与语义漂移，并构建62K数据与LVO-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频外扩（video out-painting）研究集中于短视频片段，长视频外扩鲜有探索。直接采用分块迭代外扩会带来两个问题：(1) 块间不一致与误差累积导致生成不稳定甚至失败；(2) 长时序范围的语义漂移。

Method: 提出LongSCOP（Semantically Consistent long Video Out-Painting）。训练侧：由self-forcing训练、latent calibration、future references三部分组成的训练配方，保证长时程生成平滑稳定。生成侧：利用长视频丰富时序上下文，设计基于VLM的智能体，从整段视频中自动挑选信息量最大的参考帧，以维持主体、场景与背景的一致性。数据与评测：构建62K高质量视频片段训练集，并提出由人工专家策展的LVO-Bench基准，评估长时程稳定性与语义一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在生成稳定性与语义连贯性两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 长视频外扩是一个被忽视的重要问题；通过稳定化的迭代生成训练策略结合VLM驱动的全局参考帧选择，可有效缓解块间不一致与长时语义漂移，配套的数据集与基准为该方向后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Video out-painting is an important problem, yet existing research focuses primarily on short clips, leaving long video out-painting largely underexplored. Extending video out-painting to long videos poses substantial challenges. A natural solution is iterative out-painting, i.e., generating videos chunk by chunk, but this introduces two major issues: generation instability or even failure caused by inter-chunk inconsistencies and error accumulation, and semantic drift over long temporal horizons. In this paper, we propose LongSCOP, short for Semantically Consistent long Video Out-Painting. First, we introduce a training recipe with three components for smooth and stable long-horizon generation: self-forcing training, latent calibration, and future references. Beyond generation stability, we recognize that the rich temporal context of long videos provides valuable references for maintaining semantic consistency. We therefore develop a VLM-based agent that automatically selects the most informative reference frames from the entire video to maintain consistent subjects, scenes, and backgrounds throughout long-horizon generation. We also construct a high-quality training dataset of 62K video clips. To facilitate research on this underexplored problem, we introduce LVO-Bench, a benchmark curated by human experts to assess long-horizon stability and semantic consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both generation stability and semantic coherence.

</details>


### [226] [Perturb, Aggregate then Reconstruct: Consensus Aggregation and Fourier-Attended Multi-Decoders for Reconstruction-based Multi-View Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12477)
*Yongho Son,Junyeong Yun,Je Hyeong Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于重建的多视角异常检测框架PAR，通过视图一致伪异常生成、视图专用多解码器与傅里叶注意力提升多视角异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角重建类方法虽表现优异，但多视角场景下扩展困难，核心障碍在于难以生成视图一致的伪异常，导致伪异常跨视角不对齐、重建不精确。

Method: 1) 特征扰动策略结合共识聚合模块，跨视角整合扰动特征，抑制视图不一致异常并构造高度对齐的伪异常表示；2) 视图专用多解码器架构，每视角独立解码器提升表征复杂度，生成更可行、局部化的异常图而非视图无关的粗糙重建；3) 解码器中引入傅里叶注意力，强调全局频域结构以抑制局部token证据，防止恒等映射。

Result: 在Real-IAD数据集上，PAR在样本级、图像级与像素级异常检测指标上均持续优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 验证了重建范式在多视角异常检测中的有效性，视图一致伪异常生成与视图专用解码设计是关键增益来源；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-view anomaly detection is crucial for industrial inspection to identify defects hidden from single viewpoints. Yet, despite their strong success in single-view applications, reconstruction-based methods remain largely underexplored in multi-view settings. A key challenge in extending these methods lies in generating view-consistent pseudo-anomalies. To address this, we propose PAR, a reconstruction-based framework for enhanced multi-view anomaly detection. First, we introduce a feature perturbation strategy coupled with a consensus aggregation module that integrates these perturbed features across viewpoints to mitigate view-inconsistent anomalies and construct highly aligned pseudo-anomaly representations. Second, we employ a view-specialized multi-decoder architecture; by assigning a dedicated decoder to each view, we increase the model's representational complexity, enabling the generation of highly feasible, localized anomaly maps rather than less precise, view-agnostic reconstructions. Last, we incorporate Fourier attention into the decoders to emphasize global frequency-domain structures over local token evidence, effectively preventing identity mapping. Extensive experiments on the Real-IAD dataset demonstrate that PAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in sample-level, image-level, and pixel-level anomaly detection. Our code is available at https://github.com/SpatialAILab/PAR_mvad.

</details>


### [227] [MARS: Multiscale Appearance-Residual Streams for Video Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12508)
*Ahmed Y. Radwan,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS：双路径视频深度伪造检测器，将固定的多尺度时空高斯差分残差流与适配归一化参数的外观语义流结合，并由片段相关门控融合，统一应对人脸篡改与全生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造痕迹随操纵方法与场景内容变化，跨人脸篡改与全生成视频的泛化困难；显式时空差异虽能捕捉变化，却也对自然纹理与运动响应，故需与外观特征联合解释。

Method: 两路径视频检测器：残差路径对冻结的中间特征施加固定的空间/时间高斯差分算子，并跨帧与外观上下文联合建模；语义路径适配编码器归一化参数做帧级分类；由clip相关的门控融合两路预测；并提供带符号的patch与帧贡献解释。

Result: 七数据集共享协议下平均AUROC达91.43%，优于七个对比检测器；仅用人脸篡改数据训练时，GenVideo达93.99%、DF26达75.50%，实现零生成视频数据的迁移；去除实验验证了解释的忠实性。

Conclusion: 多尺度特征差异与适配外观表示的联合建模可同时提升两类伪造的检测与跨域迁移能力，并具备可验证的可解释性。

Abstract: Generalizing deepfake detection across facial manipulation and fully generated video remains challenging due to forgery traces varying with the manipulation method and scene content. Explicit spatial and temporal feature differences offer a way to capture this variation, but also respond to natural texture and motion, motivating their joint interpretation with appearance features. We investigate whether combining multiscale feature differences with adapted appearance representations improves detection across both forgery categories. We introduce Multiscale Appearance-Residual Streams (MARS), a two-path video detector. The residual path applies fixed spatial and temporal difference-of-Gaussians operators to frozen intermediate features and models the resulting differences alongside appearance context across frames. The semantic path adapts the encoder's normalization parameters for frame classification, and a clip-dependent gate combines their predictions. Under a shared protocol across seven datasets, MARS achieves the highest average AUROC of 91.43\%, outperforming seven compared detectors. When trained on facial manipulations alone, MARS achieves 93.99\% AUROC on GenVideo and 75.50\% on DF26, demonstrating transfer to generated videos without generated-video training data. MARS also provides signed patch and frame contributions, whose faithfulness we evaluate through controlled spatial and temporal removal against random controls. MARS is publicly available for research: Project page: https://vectorinstitute.github.io/mars/ Github: https://github.com/VectorInstitute/mars

</details>


### [228] [EditJudge-Bench: Auditing VLM Image-Edit Judges with Synthetic Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12524)
*Jens Parslov,Marco Schouten,Dim P. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EditJudge-Bench，一个基于 Blender 渲染的 1,382 对合成前后图像、覆盖九类编辑指令且具备精确 3D 场景真值的诊断基准，用于将 VLM 评审者作为"指令是否被正确执行"的二元验证器进行受控审计。测评 25 种评审配置发现显著可靠性缺口，且基准排名与两个真实图像人类偏好基准高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 被广泛用作图像编辑基准的自动评审者，但其可靠性缺乏系统理解。审计困难源于：真实编辑图像缺乏受控真值，且将所请求的编辑与模型伪影、非预期副作用、成功程度模糊性纠缠在一起，难以将评审者的感知能力与编辑器质量分离。

Method: 构建合成诊断基准：用 Blender 渲染 1,382 对前后图像，编辑参数化覆盖由真实用户请求归纳的九类编辑，因编辑由受控场景的已知干预生成，故具备来自已知 3D 场景参数的精确真值。将评审任务定义为二元验证（指令是否被正确执行），从而隔离评审感知与编辑器质量。共评测 25 种评审配置，并分析提示格式（如图文顺序）的影响，最后与真实图像人类偏好基准做排名相关性检验。

Result: 发现显著可靠性缺口：(a) 广泛使用的 VIEScore 模板配合 GPT-4o 仅达 69.0% AUROC；(b) 微调评审者仅 51.7–66.3%；旋转、形状等空间编辑仍具挑战性；(c) 图文顺序等提示格式选择可使性能波动高达 14.1 个百分点。尽管仅使用合成数据，基准上的评审者排名与两个真实图像人类偏好基准强相关（ρ=0.95 与 ρ=0.71）。

Conclusion: 受控合成审计能有效揭示对真实评测同样成立的失效模式，说明合成诊断基准可作为真实图像评审可靠性的低成本代理；现有 VLM 评审者在图像编辑判定上可靠性不足，且对提示格式敏感，需改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for image-editing benchmarks, yet their reliability is poorly understood. Auditing these judges is difficult because real edited images lack controlled ground truth and entangle the requested changes with model artefacts, unintended side effects, and ambiguous degrees of success. In this paper, we introduce EditJudge-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,382 synthetic before/after image pairs rendered in Blender, with edits parameterised across nine categories derived from real user requests and exact ground truth from known 3D scene parameters. Because every edit is generated by a known intervention on a controlled scene, EditJudge-Bench evaluates VLM judges as binary verifiers of whether an instruction was correctly executed, isolating judge perception from editor quality. Across 25 judge configurations, we find substantial reliability gaps: (a) the widely adopted VIEScore template reaches only 69.0% AUROC with GPT-4o; (b) fine-tuned judges score 51.7-66.3%; spatial edits such as rotation and shape remain challenging; (c) prompt-formatting choices like image-text ordering shift performance by up to 14.1 points. Despite using only synthetic data, judge rankings on EditJudge-Bench correlate strongly with two real-image human-preference benchmarks ($ρ$=0.95 and $ρ$=0.71), indicating that controlled audits surface failure modes relevant to real evaluation.

</details>


### [229] [WanderBench: Can World Models Explore Dynamic Worlds from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12557)
*Yuhang Chen,Jiheng Li,Jiale Wang,Zai Wang,Guangyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WanderBench 视频条件世界模型基准与免训练框架 WanderLand。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频条件世界模型用于重拍、自由视点回放与真实到仿真等场景，要求生成 rollout 忠实于被记录世界；但现有基准仅从单张图像或文本提示出发，视频条件生成主要评估感知质量与相机跟随，无法直接衡量探索中主体身份、事件进程与重访位置场景一致性是否保持。

Method: 构建 WanderBench：745 段真实与 AI 生成的单镜头源视频，分三档难度（3–25 s），配以键盘动作形式的相机路径，含 6 个维度、24 个人类验证子指标；并提出 WanderLand，一种免训练框架，将冻结模型的每一帧条件化于同一时刻的源视频，并在重访时条件化于其自身此前的静态内容。

Result: 评测八个模型发现：片段变长时模型仍保持静态场景与视觉质量，但丧失相机控制与源动态；且无一模型能保持重访地点一致。WanderLand 在全部维度上提升五个模型，源动态保真度最多提升 8.3 分。

Conclusion: 视频条件动态世界探索需专门基准评估身份、事件与重访一致性；WanderLand 以测试时条件化方式有效改善多模型表现。

Abstract: Video world models conditioned on an input video make it possible to explore real, recorded dynamic scenes, enabling re-shooting of footage, free-viewpoint replay, and real-to-sim environments for embodied agents. Such uses require the generated rollout to stay faithful to the recorded world, yet existing benchmarks start from a single image or a text prompt, and video-conditioned generation is evaluated mainly on perceptual quality and camera following. These evaluations do not directly assess whether exploration preserves subject identity, event progression, and scene consistency upon revisiting a location. We introduce WanderBench, a benchmark for exploring dynamic worlds from video. It comprises 745 single-shot source videos of real and AI-generated content in three difficulty tiers (3-25 s), paired with camera routes given as keyboard actions, and evaluates six dimensions with 24 human-validated sub-metrics. Benchmarking eight models, we find that as clips grow longer, models keep the static scene and visual quality but lose camera control and the source dynamics, and that none keeps revisited places consistent. We further propose WanderLand, a training-free framework that conditions each frame of a frozen model on the same-instant source and, on revisits, on its own earlier static content. WanderLand improves five models on every dimension, by up to 8.3 points in source-dynamics fidelity.

</details>


### [230] [GHiML: VLM-Guided Hierarchical Multi-Label Learning with Applications to Benthic Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12564)
*Isaac Xu,Devi Ayyagari,Martin Gillis,Benjamin Misiuk,Craig J. Brown,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHiML 从 VLM 中提取概念隐变量并生成样本相关描述，通过交叉注意力与逐节点门控路由器融合文本嵌入与图像嵌入；同时提出对稀疏深层层级更敏感的 inverted HML 评分，在三个数据集的层级多标签分类上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 权重中蕴含大量专家知识，但直接用于底栖影像等专门分类领域仍受限；而针对这些任务训练的视觉 Transformer 主要依赖颜色、纹理、形状等低层视觉潜变量，缺乏人类识别时所用的高层推理。作者希望验证并利用 VLM 中可被激活的领域知识来增强视觉学习不足领域的分类。

Method: 提出 Guided Hierarchical Multi-Label (GHiML) 流程：以训练数据为条件从 VLM 中提取概念隐变量，基于这些变量生成与样本相关的描述文本，再将文本嵌入与图像嵌入通过交叉注意力及逐节点门控路由器(per-node gated router)融合。同时引入 inverted HML 分数 d_inv，以图遍历距离与基数惩罚评估层级多标签预测。

Result: 在 BenthicNet、FathomNet 与 iNaturalist 上，路由器在域内底栖数据上相对纯视觉基线将 macro F1 最高提升 6.7 个百分点，d_inv 最高提升 21.1 个百分点，且在分布偏移下增益仍保持。消融显示：多标签生物层级上去除逐样本文本信号会降低 d_inv 但 macro F1 不变；单标签标注的 iNaturalist 则损失 18.7% 的 macro F1。作者据此指出文本主要通过预测基数而非节点判别起作用。

Conclusion: 结果支持"VLM 含有可被适当引导而利用的专门知识、可增强直接视觉学习不足领域的分类"这一假设；文本模态的增益机制随任务标注结构（多标签 vs 单标签）而不同。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) encode substantial expert knowledge within their weights, yet their direct application to specialised classification domains such as benthic imagery remains limited. Meanwhile, vision transformers trained on these tasks rely predominantly on low-level visual latents (colours, textures, and shapes) that lack the higher-level reasoning humans employ for identification. We propose Guided Hierarchical Multi-Label (GHiML) learning, a pipeline that extracts conceptual latent variables from a VLM conditioned on training data, generates sample-relevant captions grounded in those variables, and fuses the resulting textual embeddings with image embeddings via cross-attention and a per-node gated router. We also introduce the inverted HML score ($d_{\mathrm{inv}}$), a similarity score that evaluates hierarchical multi-label predictions through graph traversal distance and a cardinality penalty, providing an interpretable alternative to existing measures, which are insensitive to realistic degradations in sparse, deep hierarchies. Experiments on BenthicNet, FathomNet and iNaturalist show that the router improves macro $F_1$ over the vision-only baseline by up to 6.7 pp and $d_{\mathrm{inv}}$ by up to 21.1 pp on in-domain benthic data, with gains persisting under distribution shift. Ablations locate the contribution of the text itself: on the multi-label biotic hierarchies, removing the per-sample text signal degrades $d_{\mathrm{inv}}$ while leaving macro $F_1$ unchanged, consistent with text acting through prediction cardinality rather than node discrimination, whereas iNaturalist, whose annotations are single-label, loses 18.7% of macro $F_1$. Our results support the hypothesis that VLMs contain exploitable specialised knowledge that, when appropriately elicited, can augment classification in domains where direct visual learning is insufficient.

</details>


### [231] [Evaluating Cross-Signal Coherence in Mammography Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12574)
*Rebeca Nam,Chase M. Fensore,Christian A. Fauria-Robinson,Judy Gichoya,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出当前VLM评估仅看准确率不足，提出以“答案-置信度-解释”三者一致性为核心的评估框架，并在乳腺密度与BI-RADS分类任务上揭示模型的置信度不随图像改变而下降、选项顺序扰动会改变答案、存在推理与结论自相矛盾等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型直接解读医学影像，其输出已不限于标签，还包含自陈置信度与文本解释；然而学界仍主要用准确率单一指标评估VLM，无法反映置信度是否可靠、解释是否与结论一致，而这恰决定AI答案能否被信任。

Method: 在两个乳腺钼靶数据集上，针对乳腺密度与BI-RADS分类任务评估四个VLM；通过三类受控扰动——问题表述、多项选项顺序、图像本身——观察答案、置信度与解释三输出的变化，并检测模型自述推理与最终答案是否矛盾。

Result: 改变钼靶图像时模型置信度并未下降；打乱选项顺序会改变大多数VLM的答案；存在模型自述推理与最终答案相互矛盾的案例，且此类不一致无法被准确率指标捕捉。

Conclusion: 医学VLM评估应从单一准确率转向答案、置信度与解释的一致性检验；当前模型在置信度校准与推理-结论自洽性上存在明显缺陷，直接影响其临床可信度。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) move toward interpreting medical images directly, their outputs increasingly include not just a label but a stated confidence and a written justification. Yet the field still predominantly evaluates VLMs using just accuracy. We evaluate the coherence between answer, confidence, and explanation across four VLMs on breast density and BI-RADS categorization, using two mammography datasets. We perform controlled perturbations of the question, multiple choice option orders, and the images and assess changes in the three outputs. Our results suggest confidence does not decrease even as the mammography image is changed. Shuffling the options also changes most VLMs' answers. Notably, there are cases where a model's own stated reasoning contradicts its final answer, a distinction accuracy alone cannot capture but one that governs whether an AI-generated answer can be trusted.

</details>


### [232] [Towards Reconstructing and Recognizing Objects behind Fragmented Occlusion using Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12624)
*Roman Pflugfelder,Michael Lindenbaum,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单相机灰度图像序列的"去遮挡/重建"框架：通过联合配准并最大化平均图像（integrated image）对比度来估计物体帧间相对位置，从而在高密度碎片化遮挡（>90%）下重建物体，并可用于分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉能透过枝叶等碎片化遮挡识别物体，而计算机视觉仍具挑战；现有合成孔径（synthetic aperture）方法仅限静态物体且需复杂、精标定的多相机装置。

Method: 对单相机（静止或运动）拍摄的运动物体灰度图像序列，联合配准各帧并优化对齐参数，使帧平均图像（integrated image）的对比度最大化，以该平均图作为重建表示。

Result: 在合成与真实视频序列上验证，遮挡密度超过90%时仍可准确重建；性能与合成孔径技术相当；重建图作为现成分类器输入时，在严重遮挡下仍能实现可靠分类。

Conclusion: 对比度最大化的联合配准框架可在单相机、高密度碎片化遮挡条件下实现有效去遮挡重建与分类，且无需多相机精标定装置，较合成孔径方法更灵活。

Abstract: Perceiving objects through fragmented occlusions, such as foliage, is a natural capability of the human visual system but remains a fundamental challenge for computer vision. This work addresses the problem of object reconstruction, or de-occlusion, in the presence of high-density, fragmented occlusions. We propose a reconstruction framework that operates on a sequence of grayscale images of a moving object, acquired by a single camera, either stationary or in motion. The algorithm estimates the object's relative positions across frames by jointly registering the images and optimizing the alignment parameters to maximize the contrast of their average, referred to as the integrated image. This integrated image serves as the reconstructed representation of the object. Experimental evaluations on both synthetic and real video sequences demonstrate the feasibility of accurate reconstruction under occlusion densities exceeding 90%. The proposed approach is competitive with synthetic aperture techniques, which are limited to static objects and require complex camera setup with careful calibration. Furthermore, we show that the integrated image enables reliable object classification under severe occlusion when used as input to standard, off-the-shelf classifiers.

</details>


### [233] [When Does Sliced Inference Pay Off? Resolution, Tiling and Dense Fusion for Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12657)
*Stefan Becker,Jens Bayer,David Münch,Wolfgang Hübner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以“尺度”为显式变量重新审视切片推理，发现SAHI类方法的大部分收益其实是分辨率效应；并提出SCAHI，用等覆盖率加权累加与细粒度峰值网格替代框融合，在同等切片次数下稳定优于框融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像中的小目标常依赖SAHI等切片推理，但其增益通常是与低分辨率基线比较得到的，掩盖了“分辨率提升”与“切片融合”各自的贡献，导致对切片何时真正有效缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 在VisDrone与DOTA上，对4个家族共7个密集检测器，在预设假设、仅做校准式调参、留出测试集单次评估的协议下，对比不同分辨率与切片策略；并设计SCAHI：对所有推理通道的密集输出以“每个图像位置总覆盖相同”的权重累加，再用足够细的峰值网格读出目标，以区分拥挤邻域。

Result: VisDrone上，单一高分辨率推理在可训练该分辨率的每个检测器上都优于框融合切片，等算力下提高分辨率比增大模型更有效；同等切片次数下SCAHI在所有YOLO检测器、两个数据集上均优于框融合，FCOS上持平。DOTA上2048px的SCAHI切片对全部检测器优于最佳单次推理，mAP50提升4.3–5.6；仅将SAHI合并步骤换为SCAHI即增加2.3–4.0 mAP50。消融显示增益来自边界加权与峰值网格分辨率。

Conclusion: 切片推理的收益需与分辨率效应解耦评估；当图像分辨率远超单次可训练分辨率时（如DOTA），切片仍有实质价值，而关键在于融合方式——等覆盖率累加与高分辨峰值读出优于框融合。

Abstract: Sliced inference such as SAHI is the standard remedy for small objects in high-resolution imagery, but its gains are usually measured against low-resolution baselines. We revisit sliced inference with scale as the explicit variable and ask when tiling actually pays off. We compare seven dense detectors from four families on VisDrone and DOTA, under pre-specified hypotheses, calibration-only tuning and a single evaluation on held-out test data. Two findings emerge. First, much of the benefit attributed to slicing is a resolution effect: on VisDrone, a single pass trained at a higher resolution outperforms tiling with box fusion (merging decoded boxes) for every detector that could be trained at that resolution, and at equal compute a higher resolution helps more than a larger model. Second, tiles carry complementary information that box fusion wastes. We introduce SCAHI (Slicing with Coverage-Aware Hyper Inference), which accumulates the dense outputs of all passes with weights that give every image position the same total coverage, and reads objects off a peak grid fine enough to keep crowded neighbours apart. On identical passes, SCAHI outperforms box fusion for every YOLO detector on both datasets and matches it for FCOS. On DOTA, whose images far exceed the resolution a single pass can be trained at, tiles with SCAHI beat the best single pass for every detector, by 4.3 to 5.6 mAP50 at 2048 px, and replacing only SAHI's merge step by SCAHI adds 2.3 to 4.0 mAP50. An ablation attributes the gain to the border weighting and the resolution of the peak grid. Source code is available at https://github.com/beckerio/scahi.

</details>


### [234] [What Breaks Scribble-Supervised Segmentation? An Audit-Informed Benchmark for Scribble Annotation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12674)
*Minheng Wu,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Kailun Yang,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出六种受控扰动算子，构建可复现的涂鸦标注鲁棒性评测协议，发现语义类别污染对分割性能破坏最大，几何与支撑类扰动的影响依赖训练流程与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涂鸦监督分割仅在固定标注集上评测，对语义与几何缺陷的鲁棒性缺乏系统认识。基于对12,031条PASCAL VOC 2012人工涂鸦的审计及已有工作，作者希望建立超出干净集精度的鲁棒性诊断基准。

Method: 设计六个受控扰动算子：类别污染、空间偏移、方向扰动（直接对应审计信号）、笔画长度缩减（源自已有涂鸦研究）、随机丢弃与碎片化（诊断对照）；每个算子同时报告请求与实际达成的扰动量。评测覆盖人工与自动涂鸦、两个图像域、两种主干配置及多种训练流程，并加入MiT-B1 PFA锚点实验。

Result: 在五种子TEL-ResNet101 VOC实验中，mIoU从70.08分别降至：类别污染1.46、空间偏移35.57、方向扰动52.20、笔画长度缩减63.24（最强设置下）。语义锚点污染造成最大性能退化，几何与支撑类响应依流程与数据集而异。

Conclusion: 该基准为诊断标注鲁棒性提供了可复现协议，弥补了仅依赖干净集精度的评价局限，算子代码已开源。

Abstract: Scribble-supervised segmentation is typically evaluated with a fixed annotation set, leaving robustness to distinct semantic and geometric imperfections poorly understood. Motivated by an audit of 12,031 manual PASCAL Visual Object Classes (VOC) 2012 scribbles and prior work, we introduce six controlled perturbation operators. Class corruption, spatial offset, and directional perturbation directly operationalize audit signals. Stroke-length reduction is motivated by prior scribble studies, while random dropout and fragmentation serve as diagnostic controls. Each operator reports requested and achieved changes. We evaluate manual and automatic scribbles across two image domains, two principal backbone settings, and multiple training pipelines, with an additional MiT-B1 PFA anchor evaluation. In the five-seed Tree Energy Loss (TEL)-ResNet101 VOC study, mean intersection over union (mIoU) drops from 70.08 to 1.46 under class corruption, compared with 35.57 for spatial offset, 52.20 for directional perturbation, and 63.24 for stroke-length reduction at their strongest settings. At the evaluated endpoints, semantic-anchor corruption produces the largest degradation, while geometric and support responses depend on the pipeline and dataset. Our benchmark provides a reproducible protocol for diagnosing annotation robustness beyond clean-set accuracy. The operator code is available at https://github.com/Wmhiram99/scribble-operator-benchmark.

</details>


### [235] [National-Scale Crop Type Mapping in Morocco Using Google AlphaEarth Foundations Embeddings and the Fields of the World Field Boundary Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12681)
*Walid Elbarz,Mohamed Bourriz,Vincent Simonneaux,Hamd Ait Abdelali,François Bourzeix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用Google AlphaEarth Foundations预训练嵌入结合轻量级分类器实现摩洛哥全国尺度作物类型制图，无需处理原始影像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型提供了可直接用于下游任务的预训练嵌入表示，但预训练嵌入在全国尺度作物制图中的可行性、精度与计算效率尚缺乏实证评估。

Method: 直接以公开的64维AlphaEarth Foundations嵌入为输入，训练轻量级下游分类器，训练与推理均不使用原始卫星影像；目标类别为冬谷物、木本作物及其他作物；全国推理时借助Fields of the World全球田块边界图将预测约束于农业地块内，以实现高效大范围制图。

Result: 在独立测试集上进行10次重复运行，平均分类精度为87.17±0.42%；摩洛哥全国推理约耗时5小时，主要计算开销来自云端嵌入的检索而非模型执行。

Conclusion: 预训练嵌入表示可用于计算高效的国家尺度作物类型制图，为大规模地球观测应用提供了轻量化的新范式。

Abstract: Recent geospatial foundation models have introduced a new paradigm for large-scale Earth observation by providing pretrained embedding representations that can be directly exploited for downstream tasks. In this work, we investigate the use of Google AlphaEarth Foundations embeddings for national-scale crop type mapping in Morocco. The proposed workflow operates directly on publicly available 64-dimensional pretrained embeddings using lightweight downstream classifiers, eliminating the need to process raw satellite imagery during model training and inference. The approach targets three crop categories, namely winter cereals, tree crops, and other crops. For nationwide inference, the Fields of the World global field boundary map is used to constrain predictions to agricultural parcels, enabling efficient large-scale mapping. Experimental evaluation on an independent test set over ten repeated runs achieved an average classification accuracy of (87.17 +/- 0.42%). Nationwide inference over Morocco was completed in approximately five hours, with the dominant computational cost arising from cloud-based retrieval of the embeddings rather than model execution. These results demonstrate the potential of pretrained embedding representations for computationally efficient national-scale crop type mapping.

</details>


### [236] [Differentiable Inverse Rendering Recovers Intrinsic Properties from Intravascular Optical Coherence Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12686)
*Ajay Manicka,Karim Kadry,Sebastian Ruiz-Lopera,Maryam Alsharqi,Sanjana Arji,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Nestor Uribe-Patarroyo,Brett E. Bouma,Farhad Nezami,Elazer R. Edelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于可微物理前向模型的“可微逆渲染”框架，仅用单张 OCT 图像即通过梯度下降逐像素恢复光学衰减系数，无需训练数据与分割，并在临床数据上验证其可移植性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像中的内在组织特性（如 OCT 光学衰减系数，是病理与细胞组成的逐像素标志物）与系统相关的成像物理过程相互纠缠。传统恢复方法依赖限于简化光-组织模型的闭式反演，或绑定特定成像配置的学习方法，缺乏通用性与可移植性。

Method: 以 OCT 前向物理模型为基础构建可微前向模型，对单张 OCT 图像通过梯度下降反演逐像素衰减系数；不依赖训练数据与分割。方法先与解析解对照验证，再在 5 个体模（phantom）上验证，并原样应用于 128 名患者共 29,269 张临床血管内 OCT 图像。作者指出梯度信息可扩展至该高维问题，使逐像素恢复变得可行，而无梯度方法在此维度下不可行；替换前向模型或改为恢复其他光学参数时，优化流程保持不变。

Result: 方法在解析解上得到验证，在 5 个体模上通过验证，并在 128 名患者的 29,269 张临床血管内 OCT 图像上无需任何改动即成功应用；证明了梯度类优化在高维逐像素反演中的可扩展性，而梯度无关方法在此规模下不可行。

Conclusion: 逐像素光学参数恢复在可微物理前向模型 + 梯度下降框架下是可行的，且具有跨平台、跨回顾性数据集的可移植性，无需训练数据与分割。该工作支持基于 OCT 的定量组织表征，并为向其他成像模态与器官系统推广提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Medical images encode intrinsic tissue properties that indicate disease progression, yet these properties remain entangled with the system-dependent physics of image formation. Differentiable inverse rendering asks whether they are recoverable by inverting the imaging system's forward physics rather than through post-hoc correction. We address this in optical coherence tomography (OCT), focusing on the optical attenuation coefficient, a per-pixel marker of pathology and cellular composition. Conventional recovery relies on closed-form inversions limited to simplified light-tissue models, or learned methods coupled to specific imaging configurations. We instead recover per-pixel attenuation from a single OCT image by gradient descent through a differentiable physics-based forward model. We verified the method against analytical solutions, validated it on five in vitro phantoms, and applied it without modification to 29{,}269 clinical intravascular OCT images from 128 patients. The inversion needs no training data or segmentations and is portable across platforms and retrospective archives. Replacing the forward model or recovering another optical parameter leaves the optimization procedure unchanged. We demonstrate per-pixel recovery is tractable because gradient information scales to this high-dimensional problem, where gradient-free methods become intractable. Our work supports quantitative tissue characterization from OCT images and provides a framework for extension across imaging modalities and organ systems.

</details>


### [237] [GeoScope: Training-Free Program Execution beyond the Counting Horizon for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12699)
*Guohui Deng,Yuhang Jiang,Chengjun Wang,Zhongwen Hu,Zhaocong Wu,Linsheng Huang,Jinling Zhao,Chao Ruan,Miaozhong Xu,Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示VLM在超高分辨率遥感图像中存在“计数地平线”（多数模型约5个目标/视图），据此提出免训练方法GeoScope：文本LLM将问题解析为带类型空间程序以固定区域与分辨率，VLM仅在其能力范围内定位计数，方向由定位框显式计算，并按实测极限切分视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UHR遥感VQA方法主要决定VLM“看哪里”并信任其在所选视图中的答案，但计数目标往往仅占几像素。作者表明这一假设在计数上失效：视图内目标数超过一定阈值后，所有8个被测开源VLM均出现欠计数，且视图越大、目标越小，该界限越窄。

Method: 先以类似传感器标定的方式测量VLM的计数能力上限（幂律响应模型预测切分可恢复的欠计数与引入的虚假计数），据此确立“仅在超出地平线时切分”的规则。GeoScope中，纯文本LLM将问题解析为类型化空间程序，确定区域及其分辨率；VLM仅在其工作范围内做定位与计数；方向关系由定位框显式计算。

Result: 免训练条件下，在XLRS-Bench上使Qwen3-VL-8B区域计数提升16.3点、空间关系提升10.5点，并在区域计数及按问题统计的整榜上取得已报告最高精度（含训练方法）。在UHR-Micro上全局与区域计数分别提升16.8和36.2点（全球计数中15.6点来自切分）。在MME-RealWorld计数上提升11.4点，并指出该基准中一个几乎从不为正确答案却常被选中的选项会扭曲比较结果。

Conclusion: VLM的计数能力存在可测量的上限，按该极限进行视图切分并结合显式空间程序分解，可在无需训练的情况下显著提升UHR遥感计数与空间关系推理，并刷新多项基准记录。

Abstract: In ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing images, the objects that questions count in a region or relate by direction often span only a few pixels. Existing methods mainly decide where a vision-language model (VLM) should look and trust its answer in the chosen view. We show that this assumption fails for counting, and measure VLMs as one calibrates a sensor. All eight open VLMs tested fall increasingly short once a view holds more than a certain number of objects, about five for most on DOTA aerial images. This counting horizon narrows as views grow and objects appear smaller. An empirical power-law response model predicts the undercount a split recovers and the spurious counts it adds, and supports the rule of splitting only views beyond the horizon. In GeoScope, a text-only language model parses the question into a typed spatial program that fixes the region and its resolution. The VLM only grounds and counts within its operating range, and directions are computed explicitly from the grounded boxes. To our knowledge, it is the first UHR method to split views by a measured limit of the VLM. Without training, it improves Qwen3-VL-8B by 16.3 points on regional counting and 10.5 on spatial relations in XLRS-Bench, and has the highest reported accuracy, trained methods included, on regional counting and on the whole benchmark by question.
