On UHR-Micro it improves global and regional counting by 16.8 and 36.2 points, 15.6 of the former from splitting. On MME-RealWorld counting it gains 11.4 points, and we show that an option that is almost never correct yet often chosen distorts comparisons there. Project page: https://avalon-s.github.io/GeoScope/

</details>


### [238] [Spaghetti Splatting: Differentiating Curvilinear Dirac Masses via Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12716)
*George Shramko,Shrisudhan Govindarajan,Rudy Lam,Yizheng Xie,Daniel Rebain,David B. Lindell,Andrea Tagliasacchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Spaghetti Splatting，将 EWA/Poisson/3DGS 的"低通核卷积狄拉克质量"范式推广到曲线图元：用折线近似曲线并做逐段体渲染，实现无重叠计数的连续拼接。该表示是 3DGS 的严格超集（点=零长度曲线），在通用场景具竞争力、在类线几何上优于专用方法，原生支持抗锯齿，并输出可直接用于物理仿真的长连通曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统中的 3D 几何由点、线、三角形等图元表示，渲染硬件对其高度优化；但点、三角形已获大量研究，曲线（线状）图元在"从图像经体渲染学习图元"的框架下仍缺乏处理。同时，曲线是狄拉克质量，直接体渲染困难，需借助低通核卷积，且需解决线段拼接处的重复计数问题。

Method: 1) 将曲线用折线（polyline）近似，其卷积收敛到连续曲线的卷积；2) 对每个线段分别做体渲染，使相邻线段在关节处无重叠计数地复合（compositing）；3) 为实现快速训练与推理，对每个线段线性化相机投影；4) 该线性化同时允许使用高斯之外的核（如 Beta 核）；5) 由于点是零长度曲线，表示为 3DGS 的严格超集。

Result: 在通用场景上与 SOTA 保持竞争力；在类线状几何上优于专门方法；原生支持抗锯齿；生成的长连通曲线可直接用于物理仿真。

Conclusion: "低通核 × 图元"范式可有效推广到曲线图元，Spaghetti Splatting 在保持通用性能的同时填补了曲线表示的空白，并因输出连通结构而具备下游物理仿真可用性。

Abstract: In production systems, 3D geometry is represented by primitives such as points, lines, and triangles, as rendering hardware is optimized to generate images from them very efficiently. Learning such primitives from images by volume rendering is challenging, as they are Dirac masses defined at infinitesimal locations in space. EWA splatting, Poisson reconstruction, and 3D Gaussian Splatting overcome this by convolving Dirac masses with a low-frequency kernel, a recipe that generalizes along two axes: the kernel and the primitive. As points and triangles have already received significant attention, we investigate this recipe for curvilinear primitives, and introduce Spaghetti Splatting. We approximate curves by polylines whose convolution converges to that of the continuous curve, and volume render each segment so that connected segments composite without overcounting at their joints. For fast training and inference, we linearize the camera projection per segment, which also accommodates kernels beyond Gaussians, such as Beta kernels. As a point is a curve of zero length, our representation is a strict superset of 3D Gaussian Splatting. It remains competitive with the state-of-the-art on general scenes, outperforms methods specialized to line-like geometry, natively supports anti-aliasing, and produces long connected strands that are directly amenable to physics simulation.

</details>


### [239] [OptiCover: A Scalable and Multi-Surface Framework for Cold-Start Visual Optimization under Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12727)
*Guillaume Guy,Mihajlo Grbovic,Joe Mills,Selen Uguroglu,Liwei He,Pratiksha Kadam,Nalini Kartha,Qian Aria Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OptiCover：面向 Airbnb 冷启动封面图选择的可扩展框架，结合 CLIP 轻量视觉头、VLM 语义校验与跨表面探索日志模块，已上线上线，每日数千万次换图，带来可观的互动与预订提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封面图选择在电商视觉陈列中关键但研究不足。Airbnb 房源有多张照片，但仅能手动选一张静态封面并复用于异构产品表面；90%以上照片无互动标签（严重冷启动），不同表面与搜索意图下偏好不同，且视觉吸引力强的照片可能语义上与房源不符。规模上涉及数亿照片、每日数十万新上传。

Method: 提出 OptiCover，包含四部分：(i) 候选照片生成器，通过多种代理信号筛选候选；(ii) 冻结 CLIP 嵌入上的轻量视觉头，做大规语义打分；(iii) 基于 VLM 的跨模态接地校验器，强制语义代表性；(iv) 探索日志模块（ELM），在异构产品表面与上下文搜索意图上编排随机化互动数据采集。

Result: 大量离线研究与 HomePage、Search 多表面在线实验验证。已投产，每日执行数千万次换图；HomePage 互动提升 1.5%，Search 预订者增加 0.35%，带来数百万美元增量收入。

Conclusion: 动作空间整形（action-space shaping）与语义约束是性能提升的主要驱动因素；该框架证明在冷启动、多表面、语义受限条件下大规模视觉优化可行。

Abstract: Selecting an effective cover photo is a critical yet understudied problem in visual merchandising for e-commerce marketplaces. On Airbnb, hosts upload many photos depicting different rooms, amenities, and points of interest, but must manually choose a single static cover photo that is reused across heterogeneous product surfaces. This creates substantial challenges: over 90\% of listing photos have no engagement labels, leading to a severe cold-start regime; guest preferences vary across surfaces and search intents, and visually captivating photos may be semantically misaligned with the underlying listing. At Airbnb scale, this corresponds to hundreds of millions of photos and hundreds of thousands of new uploads per day.
  We propose \emph{OptiCover}, a scalable framework for cold-start visual optimization under semantic constraints and multi-surface engagement signals. The framework combines (i) a photo candidate generator that identifies candidate photos through various proxies, (ii) lightweight vision heads applied to frozen CLIP embeddings for large-scale semantic scoring, (iii) a cross-modal grounding validator based on a vision-language model (VLM) that enforces semantic representativeness, and (iv) an Exploration Logging Module (ELM) that orchestrates the collection of randomized engagement data across heterogeneous product surfaces and contextual search intents.
  We evaluate OptiCover through extensive offline studies and multiple online experiments across HomePage, and Search surfaces. OptiCover is now deployed in production and performs tens of millions of photo swaps daily. Online results show a 1.5\% engagement lift on the HomePage and a 0.35\% increase in bookers on Search, generating millions of dollars in incremental revenue. Our findings suggest that action-space shaping and semantic constraints are primary drivers of performance.

</details>


### [240] [Plant Orbiter: 3D Plant Reconstruction and Generation with Video Diffusion Orbit Cameras and 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12739)
*Zane K. J. Hartley,Yong En Kok,Lewis A. G. Stuart,Andrew P. French,Michael P. Pound*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Plant Orbiter，利用视频扩散模型从单张图像生成高质量植物 3D Gaussian Splats，实现高精度、高通量的植物三维形态重建，并结合 2D 扩散模型实现文本到 3D 植物生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数字表型流水线需精确测量三维植物形态性状以加速育种与品种开发。3D 技术虽推动从 2D 向 3D 转型，但因植物形态复杂、结构纤细、遮挡严重，高通量获取植物 3D 数字孪生仍十分困难。

Method: Plant Orbiter 借助视频扩散模型的进展，从单张图像生成植物的 3D Gaussian Splatting 表示；并将该方法与 2D 扩散模型结合，用于从文本提示生成 3D 植物模型。

Result: 在所有基线方法中取得最高 3D F-score，更准确地重建植物形态；由于对齐更容易、相机布置成本更低，更适合高通量流水线；结合 2D 扩散模型后在文本到 3D 植物生成上达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: Plant Orbiter 以单图输入实现高质量植物 3D 重建与文本驱动生成，兼顾精度与高通量实用性，为数字表型研究提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Modern digital phenotyping pipelines require the precise measurement of a range of 3D morphological plant traits. The accurate, high throughput acquisition of these traits helps breeders and researchers accelerate the development of new varieties, techniques and products. Recent advances in 3D technologies have enabled a rapid transition from 2D to 3D pipelines, but high throughput acquisition of digital 3D twins of plants remains a significant challenge due to complex morphology, thin structures and occlusions. In this paper we present Plant Orbiter, a new approach to generating 3D plants that leverages recent advances in video diffusion to produce high quality 3D Gaussian Splats of plants from a single image. We show that our method more accurately recreates plant morphology than other state-of-the-art methods, achieving the highest 3D F-score against all baselines, while being more practical for high throughput pipelines due to easier alignment and reduced cost of camera placement. Combined with a 2D diffusion model, we show that our method also achieves state-of-the-art results in the generation of 3D plant models from a text prompt.

</details>


### [241] [Complementarity-Driven Dual-Teacher Hierarchical Alignment for Zero-Shot EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12798)
*Lukun Wu,Jie Li,Ziqi Ren,Ruiyang Xia,Yan Gao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出动机-范式对应视角，将视觉加工层级与跨范式视觉基础模型(VFM)的互补偏差对应，并据此提出双教师层级对齐(DTHA)框架，在THINGS-EEG零样本检索上达92.4% Top-1，超SOTA 12.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法仅与CLIP族VFM对齐，未充分利用神经响应中的层级视觉信息。作者观察到纯图像模型(如DINO)与图像-文本模型(如CLIP)在捕捉"外观到语义"层级上具有互补偏差，故需跨范式多教师监督。

Method: 构建"机制-范式对应"观点后提出DTHA框架：共享EEG编码器由冻结的多模糊CLIP与浅层DINOv2双教师通过双分支对比对齐监督；融合检索目标学习自适应权重组合互补表征用于零样本EEG-to-image检索；编码器采用注意调制头皮模式库编码器(AMSPBE)，结合时空间注意与可学习头皮模式库，建模区域时序动态与滤波器组地形。

Result: THINGS-EEG零样本检索Top-1达92.4%，超先前SOTA 12.4个百分点。空间、频谱与表征相似性分析及THINGS-MEG评估验证了有效性、生物合理性与跨模态泛化性。

Conclusion: 跨范式VFM的互补监督能够更完整地从脑活动中提取层级视觉信息，为EEG视觉解码提供新视角与可解释的特征提取方案。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from EEG is central to brain--computer interfaces and brain-inspired intelligence, and contrastive alignment with pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) has become the dominant paradigm. However, existing methods align EEG solely with CLIP-family VFMs, leaving hierarchical visual information in neural responses under-exploited. To address this gap, we formulate a mechanism-paradigm correspondence view linking hierarchical visual processing (from appearance to semantics) to the complementary biases of cross-paradigm VFMs, with image-only models (e.g., DINO) and image--text models (e.g., CLIP) capturing distinct facets of this hierarchy. Guided by this view, we propose the Dual-Teacher Hierarchical Alignment (DTHA) framework. Concretely, a shared EEG encoder is supervised by frozen multi-blur CLIP and shallow DINOv2 teachers via dual-branch contrastive alignment, so each branch fully supervises the visual cues it emphasizes. A fused-retrieval objective learns adaptive weights to combine their complementary visual representations for zero-shot EEG-to-image retrieval. For the shared EEG encoder, we introduce the Attentive Modulated Scalp-Pattern Bank Encoder (AMSPBE), combining temporo-spatial attention with a learnable scalp-pattern bank for accurate, interpretable visual feature extraction, while modeling regional temporal dynamics and filter-bank topographies. On THINGS-EEG zero-shot retrieval, DTHA achieves 92.4% Top-1 accuracy, exceeding prior state of the art by 12.4 percentage points. Spatial, spectral, and representational-similarity analyses, together with THINGS-MEG evaluation, validate DTHA's effectiveness, biological plausibility, and cross-modality generalization. This complementarity-driven perspective shows that supervision from cross-paradigm VFMs enables more complete extraction of hierarchical visual information from brain activity.

</details>


### [242] [2D Gum-Net: Unsupervised Dense Elastic Image Registration via Symmetric Correlation and Spectral Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12805)
*Aarya Bhave,Anushka Ajay Bhave*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 2D Gum-Net：将原用于冷冻电镜 3D 子断层对齐的几何无监督匹配网络迁移到 2D，实现全无监督的稠密弹性形变场预测；在指纹数据上验证，合成训练、真实数据零样本测试，结构相关性最高提升 15.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密非刚性配准需恢复像素级形变场，但真实数据几乎无法获得形变场真值，学习型方法因此受严重瓶颈制约；同时常规池化（最大/平均）会破坏指纹等准周期细结构。作者希望完全不依赖真值监督，同时保住细粒度结构。

Method: 端到端无监督架构：三个模块全部重设计为 2D，用 Dense Spatial Transformer 替代刚性变换头，回归稀疏控制点网格并以双三次插值上采样为全分辨率形变场 Φ；下采样采用 DCT 谱池化，以冻结基矩阵乘法实现（约 50× GPU 加速）。在接触式指纹上评估，以细节点（minutiae）为独立匹配器可外部核查的地标。仅在合成按压图像上训练，在真实基准上零样本评估。

Result: FVC2004 上结构相关性最高提升 15.7%；独立匹配器下真匹配分数上升，假匹配分数基本不变，说明预测形变恢复真实结构而非伪造对应关系。

Conclusion: 无监督稠密弹性配准可在无真值条件下获得拓扑正确的形变场，DCT 谱池化对保留准周期细结构有效；合成训练 + 零样本真实评估具备可行性。

Abstract: Dense non-rigid registration, the recovery of a pixel-wise deformation field that warps one image into geometric agreement with another, is fundamental across medical imaging, structural biology, and correspondence estimation, yet learning-based approaches face a severe bottleneck: ground-truth deformation fields are notoriously difficult to obtain for real data. We present 2D Gum-Net, an end-to-end architecture that predicts dense elastic deformation fields fully unsupervised. We transfer the Geometric Unsupervised Matching Network (Gum-Net), originally developed for 3D subtomogram alignment in cryo-electron tomography, to 2D elastic registration: all three modules are redesigned in 2D, and the rigid transformation head is replaced by a Dense Spatial Transformer that regresses a sparse control-point grid, lifted to a full-resolution field $Φ$ by bicubic interpolation. Downsampling uses DCT spectral pooling, implemented as frozen-basis matrix multiplications ($\approx 50\times$ GPU speedup), preserving the fine, quasi-periodic structure that max and average pooling destroy. We evaluate on contact-based fingerprints, a challenging testbed for this task: locally self-similar ridge patterns make correspondence maximally ambiguous, skin pressed against a sensor generates authentic large-displacement elastic distortion, and minutiae act as precise landmarks through which an independent matcher externally verifies the topological correctness of predicted fields. Trained purely on synthetic impressions and evaluated zero-shot on real benchmarks, our method improves structural correlation by up to 15.7% on FVC2004 and raises genuine match scores under an independent matcher while leaving imposter scores essentially unchanged. This suggests that the predicted deformations restore true structure without fabricating correspondences.

</details>


### [243] [What kills $v$-prediction? A Patch-wise PCA Perspective on Pixel-Space Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12852)
*Tianze Luo,Haofeng Huang,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过patch-wise PCA揭示像素空间Flow Matching的带宽瓶颈，据此提出Raw-injected Transformer (RiT)，在解耦语义建模与原始状态传播后恢复v-prediction的有效性，并在ImageNet 256×256取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Latent Diffusion存在两阶段训练复杂性与预训练自编码器的重建上限；像素空间Flow Matching虽简化端到端流程，但标准v-prediction目标训练困难。已有工作将其归因于噪声目标偏离流形并转向x-prediction，本文旨在更深入地厘清这一机理并给出架构层面的解决方案。

Method: 1) 用patch-wise PCA分析发现带宽瓶颈：次要主成分方向的速度主要由原始噪声输入决定，深层骨干须保留与主成分建模相竞争的信息；2) 通过受控实验与表示分析解释x-prediction的有效性——其参数化为原始输入提供旁路(bypass)，学到的输入表示可自发集中于主成分方向；3) 在低秩观测下分析诱导流，将x-prediction刻画为隐式条件均值解码器，从保留信息重建被丢弃的分量；4) 据此设计RiT：轻量级raw-conditioned解码器加输出端shortcut，分离语义建模与原始状态传播。

Result: RiT在像素空间恢复有效的v-prediction，并在ImageNet 256×256上取得有竞争力的生成性能。

Conclusion: 预测目标与架构带宽之间存在耦合：v-prediction的困难源于骨干在原始状态传播上的带宽约束，而非仅目标本身的缺陷；通过架构显式提供原始输入通路即可恢复v-prediction的有效性，为生成建模目标与架构设计的协同提供了新视角。

Abstract: Latent Diffusion Models dominate generative modeling but face two-stage training complexity and the inherent reconstruction limits of pre-trained autoencoders. Pixel-space Flow Matching offers a simplified end-to-end alternative yet often encounters training difficulties when using the standard $v$-prediction objective. Recent research attributes this difficulty to the off-manifold nature of noised targets, motivating a shift toward $x$-prediction. In this paper, we investigate this explanation through patch-wise Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and identify a bandwidth bottleneck. The velocity in minor components is largely determined by the raw noisy input, requiring deep backbones to preserve information that competes with principal-component modeling. Through controlled experiments and representation analysis, we further examine why $x$-prediction remains effective: its parameterization provides a raw-input bypass, while its learned input representation can spontaneously concentrate on principal directions. Under low-rank observations, our analysis of the induced flow characterizes $x$-prediction as an implicit conditional-mean decoder, reconstructing discarded components from the retained information. These observations motivate the Raw-injected Transformer (RiT), which combines a lightweight raw-conditioned decoder with an output-side shortcut to separate semantic modeling from raw-state propagation. Our method restores effective $v$-prediction and achieves competitive performance on ImageNet $256\times256$, providing a perspective on the interaction between prediction objectives and architectural bandwidth.

</details>


### [244] [AI-based characterization of lymphoid structures and the tumor microenvironment reveals lower mature tertiary lymphoid structures, lymph-node follicles, and plasma cells after neoadjuvant therapy in lung squamous cell carcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12855)
*Arne Schmidt,Justin Nieder,Cornelius Boehm,Julika Ribbat-Idel,Marius Teodorescu,Marie-Lisa Eich,Simon Schallenberg,Gabriel Dernbach,Katja Lingelbach,Miriam Haegele,Timo Milbich,Adam Narai,Maria Stoelben,Hans Pinckaers,Lukas Ruff,Kai Standvoss,Christian Watermann,Christiane Kuempers,Sven Perner,Verena Sailer,Jasmin Tiebach,Torsten Goldmann,Sebastian Marwitz,Johannes Braegelmann,Frederick Klauschen,Marija Pezer,Rosemarie Krupar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 新辅助治疗与肺鳞癌中成熟三级淋巴结构（TLS）及成熟淋巴结滤泡（SLS）减少、体液免疫与肿瘤耦联减弱相关，而非免疫指标普遍下降；间质浆细胞及其与淋巴细胞的共定位轴具预后价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟TLS与成熟淋巴结滤泡维持浆细胞应答并影响肺癌预后及免疫治疗获益，但新辅助治疗与治疗初治肿瘤之间这一"局部-区域轴"的差异尚不明确。

Method: 分析配对的新辅助治疗（neoLUSC, n=20）与初治（naivLUSC, n=20）肺鳞癌H&E全切片及近端/远端淋巴结；用Aignostics Atlas HETA模型量化各组织分区细胞密度与细胞共定位；用经病理学家标注验证的深度学习模型检测TLS/SLS并分级成熟度；在独立队列（n=458，46例治疗）验证预后价值。

Result: neoLUSC间质浆细胞密度低72%（p=0.004）、间质淋巴细胞低48%（p=0.015），巨噬细胞与粒细胞无显著差异；总TLS与未成熟TLS无差异，但肿瘤内成熟TLS（p=0.034）与肿瘤远端淋巴结成熟SLS（p=0.003）在neoLUSC更低；naivLUSC中TLS周围浆细胞密度与肿瘤淋巴细胞密度相关（rho=0.54, p=0.017），neoLUSC中则无（rho=0.07）。验证队列中间质浆细胞密度（HR 0.69, p=0.011）与淋巴细胞-浆细胞共定位（HR 0.66, p=0.004）与更长生存相关，新辅助状态与更短生存相关（HR 1.50, p=0.026）。

Conclusion: 新辅助治疗伴随成熟淋巴结构减少及体液区室与肿瘤耦联减弱，而非免疫读数整体下降；间质浆细胞/共定位轴可作为候选预后生物标志物。

Abstract: Mature, germinal centre-bearing tertiary lymphoid structures (TLS) and mature lymph node follicles (secondary lymphoid structures, SLS) sustain the plasma cell response that shapes outcome and immunotherapy benefit in lung cancer. How this loco-regional axis differs between neoadjuvant-treated and therapy-naive tumors is poorly defined. We analyzed H&E whole-slide images of resected tumors and paired proximal and distal lymph nodes from matched neoadjuvant-treated (neoLUSC, n=20) and therapy-naive (naivLUSC, n=20) lung squamous cell carcinoma (LUSC) patients. Aignostics Atlas H&E Tissue Analysis (HETA) models quantified cell densities per tissue compartment and cell-cell co-localization; a dedicated deep-learning model validated against pathologist annotations detected TLS and SLS and graded their maturity. Prognostic value was tested in an independent cohort (n=458; 46 treated). In neoLUSC tumors, stromal plasma cell density was 72% (p=0.004) and stromal lymphocyte density 48% (p=0.015) lower than in naivLUSC; macrophages and granulocytes did not differ significantly. Total and immature TLS did not differ, whereas mature TLS in the tumor (p=0.034) and mature SLS in the tumor-distal lymph node (p=0.003) were lower in neoLUSC patients. Peri-TLS plasma cell density correlated with tumor lymphocyte density in naivLUSC (rho=0.54, p=0.017) but not in neoLUSC tumors (rho=0.07). In the validation cohort, stromal plasma cell density (HR 0.69, p=0.011) and lymphocyte-plasma cell co-localization (HR 0.66, p=0.004) were associated with longer survival, and neoadjuvant status with shorter survival (HR 1.50, p=0.026). Neoadjuvant therapy is thus associated with fewer mature lymphoid structures and weaker coupling of the humoral compartment to the tumor rather than uniformly lower immune readouts. The stromal plasma cell/co-localization axis is a candidate prognostic biomarker.

</details>


### [245] [SWAM: Scaling World Action Models with Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12870)
*Yu Zhou,Xinyuan Xia,Xuankun Yang,Yinghao Yang,Wendi Chen,Bowen Liu,Jianhan Ma,Weicheng Duan,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Yanwei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统研究了世界动作模型（WAM）中动作学习对语言-视频对齐的影响，基于约47,000小时多源数据考察不同监督形式与训练配方，给出可维护对齐的数据与监督设计，并在RoboTwin与真实任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM研究主要关注架构与训练机制，默认视频预训练获得的语言-视频对齐是稳固基础，但引入动作学习可能损害该对齐；关于在动作学习过程中维持语言-视频对齐的数据与监督设计的原理性指导仍然有限。

Method: 构建约47,000小时的通用视频、第一人称视频、UMI演示与机器人演示数据池；系统对比语言-视频监督、语言-视频-动作监督及两者联合使用的效果，分析语言标注质量对预训练语言-视频能力的影响，以及UMI与机器人演示的动作监督如何将该能力扩展至语言-视频-动作建模；并进一步研究联合训练配方与模型架构的扩展性。

Result: 固定训练预算下，混合第一人称视频与UMI演示可提升控制泛化；模型架构影响经验利用效率，在选定配置下扩展预训练带来进一步增益。最终配方仅在RoboTwin Clean演示上后训练，在Easy（Clean-to-Clean）与Hard（Clean-to-Random）条件下分别达到88.10%和61.56%成功率；真实世界三项任务平均成功率40.00%，最强基线为23.33%。

Conclusion: 动作学习中的语言-视频对齐需通过数据混合与监督设计加以维护；合理的配方与架构扩展可在有限预算下显著提升泛化能力，且模型在未见条件下表现优于基线。作者开源了数据、训练代码与模型权重。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict video and actions conditioned on language. Existing studies focus primarily on architectures and training mechanisms, typically treating language-video alignment acquired through video pretraining as an established foundation. However, introducing action learning may impair this alignment. Principled guidance on data and supervision design for maintaining language-video alignment during action learning remains limited. To investigate this, we curate a pool of approximately 47,000 hours of general videos, egocentric videos, UMI demonstrations, and robot demonstrations. Building on this, we systematically study how language-video supervision, language-video-action supervision, and the joint use of these two forms affect WAM performance. In particular, we examine how language annotation quality affects pretrained language-video capabilities, and how action supervision from UMI and robot demonstrations extends these capabilities to language-video-action modeling. To better scale the model, we further conduct detailed studies on joint training recipe and model architecture. Under a fixed training budget, mixing egocentric videos and UMI demonstrations improves control generalization. Model architecture affects how effectively these experiences are used, while scaling pretraining under the selected configuration brings further gains. The resulting recipe, post-trained on RoboTwin Clean demonstrations only, achieves success rates of 88.10% and 61.56% under Easy (Clean-to-Clean) and Hard (Clean-to-Random) conditions, respectively. Our model achieves 40.00% SR across three real-world tasks, compared with 23.33% for the strongest baseline, indicating better generalization under unseen conditions. We release the curated data, training code, and model weights.

</details>


### [246] [Click3R: Interactive Stereo 3D Reconstruction with Sparse Correspondence Clicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12875)
*Hao Gu,Jin Yao,Zezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Click3R：利用稀疏人工对应点点击对前馈三维重建模型进行交互式几何纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈大三维重建模型只输出单一且不可撤回的预测，失败时缺乏纠错机制。

Method: 给定图像对与用户提供的少量跨视角点对应，通过轻量级点对应适配器将几何约束注入重建网络；并构建针对现有前馈方法失效模式的挑战性基准。

Result: 仅需单次对应点击即可降低重建误差，在标准基准与自建基准上均取得提升，可缓解重复纹理、对称结构、大视角变化与无纹理区域等歧义。

Conclusion: 稀疏人工交互是纠正几何重建误差的有效且实用机制。

Abstract: Despite impressive progress, existing feed-forward large 3D reconstruction models produce a single, irrevocable prediction. When these models fail, there is no mechanism to correct their mistakes. To address this limitation, we introduce Click3R, a framework for interactive geometric correction of feed-forward models using sparse human correspondence clicks. Given an image pair and a small set of cross-view point correspondences provided by a user, Click3R injects geometric constraints into the 3D reconstruction networks through a lightweight point correspondence adapter, enabling the model to resolve ambiguities in challenging scenarios such as repetitive textures, symmetric structures, large viewpoint changes, or textureless regions. To evaluate interactive reconstruction, we curate a challenging benchmark specifically designed to expose failure modes of existing feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods. Experiments show that Click3R reduces reconstruction error with as few as a single correspondence click, improving performance on both standard and our curated benchmarks. Our results highlight that sparse human interaction provides an effective and practical mechanism for correcting geometric reconstruction errors.

</details>


### [247] [Grasping the Future: Joint 3D Hand-Object Forecasting From Internet Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12879)
*Rustin Soraki,Matthew Wallingford,Ryan Aby,Brandon Chee,Jason Ren,Ali Farhadi,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Roozbeh Mottaghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一生成模型 Grasping-the-Future，用掩码时空补全与 flow matching 联合建模 3D 手-物运动（腕部位姿、手指关节、物体位姿），并通过可扩展重建管线从互联网视频获取 3D 监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与物体交互需同时预判物体运动与手部如何随之运动；人类日常活动视频天然蕴含此类手-物关系，但从中学习需先从已完成的交互中恢复 3D 运动，再用于预测后续交互。

Method: 单一生成模型对联合 3D 手-物轨迹建模（腕部运动、手指关节、物体位置与朝向）；以掩码时空补全 + flow matching 统一多任务（预测未来交互、由物体预测手或反向、生成指向目标物体位姿的双轨迹、补全部分观测交互）；显式建模手-物关系并引入接触约束以在运动中保持抓握关系；数据上结合预训练感知模型与联合几何优化的可扩展管线从网络视频重建完整交互轨迹，并与带标注交互数据集联合训练。

Result: 在带 3D 标注数据集上所有任务均表现良好，优于已发布的预测方法；将预测轨迹重定向到机器人本体后，验证了其几何与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 单一掩码生成框架可统一多种手-物交互预测任务，且互联网视频结合几何优化可提供有效 3D 监督，预测结果具备可迁移至真实机器人的可行性。

Abstract: To interact with an object, we need to anticipate both how it will move and how our hands should move with it. Human videos capture these relationships across everyday activities, offering a natural source for learning to predict future interactions. Learning from them requires recovering motion from completed interactions and using it to anticipate what could happen next. We present $\textit{Grasping-the-Future}$, a unified generative model of joint 3D hand-object motion, including wrist motion, finger articulation, and object position and orientation. Through masked spatiotemporal completion and flow matching, we train a single model for several prediction tasks: forecasting future interactions, predicting hand motion given object motion or vice versa, generating both trajectories toward a desired final object pose, and completing partially observed interactions. We explicitly model hand-object relations and incorporate contact constraints to help preserve grasp relationships during motion. To obtain 3D supervision from internet videos, we develop a scalable pipeline that combines pretrained perception models with joint geometric optimization to reconstruct completed interactions. We combine these recovered trajectories with annotated interaction datasets as training data for all tasks. We demonstrate strong performance across all tasks, outperforming released forecasters on datasets with 3D annotations. Moreover, we show geometric and physical plausibility of the predicted trajectories by retargeting them to a robotic embodiment. Videos are available at https://grasping-the-future.github.io.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Modality Preference: Controlling Multimodal Arbitration under Modality Conflict in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12883)
*Zhaoying Pan,Shenyu Lu,Jun Hui Wong,Junyi Chai,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 识别出少量"仲裁注意力头"（arbitration heads）因果性决定MLLM在模态冲突时偏向图像还是文本，并提出一种无需训练、轻量级的推理时干预以按任务指定可信模态进行双向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在模态信息冲突时不可靠，可能偏向并非本任务最可信的模态，因此需要在推理阶段对模态仲裁进行控制。

Method: 通过因果分析定位一小集合的注意力头（arbitration heads），这些头影响预测是否遵循视觉或文本证据；在此基础上，在推理时对这些头施加偏置以倾向指定可信模态，无需训练。

Result: 所识别的注意力头对校准集大小稳定、可跨设置泛化；引导这些头可在多个基准上实现对模态依赖的有效双向控制。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个有效控制机制，可使MLLM预测与任务指定的模态优先级对齐。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) integrate information across modalities, but become unreliable when those modalities disagree. Under conflicting inputs, MLLMs may favor modalities that are not the most reliable for the task, thus motivating inference-time control of modality arbitration. We identify a small set of attention heads, termed arbitration heads, that causally influence whether predictions follow visual or textual evidence. Building on this, we introduce a lightweight, training-free intervention that biases these heads toward a designated trusted modality at inference time. The identified heads are stable across calibration set sizes and generalize across settings, and steering them enables effective bidirectional control of modality reliance across diverse benchmarks. Our findings identify an effective control mechanism to align MLLM predictions with task-specified modality priorities. The code is available at https://github.com/zhaoyingpan/modality-arbitration.

</details>


### [249] [World In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12885)
*Heng Fang,Zehui Guo,Yuehan Zhang,Mingyang Du,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 World In-Context Learning (WICL) 范式与 RoboScript 框架：冻结模型从单条示教出发，在仿真世界中排练任务，将反馈写入上下文而非权重，形成 Task Memory，从而在无梯度更新条件下实现跨场景泛化的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统训练型操作策略将技能固化进网络权重，导致所学与训练分布强耦合；而现有上下文学习（ICL）虽避免了耦合，却因示教来自其他场景、缺乏当前世界反馈而性能不及训练策略。作者观察到智能体在上下文中习得的是语义信息（任务步骤与物体角色）而非视觉外观，故经验可跨世界迁移。

Method: 提出 WICL 范式：冻结模型以单条示教为起点，在仿真器等世界中排练任务，并把环境反馈写入上下文而非权重。据此构建模块化基础设施 RoboScript，用于智能体化操作：从单条示教出发在仿真中排练学习每个任务，将学得知识冻结为 Task Memory，并以数据隔离方式在新场景部署该冻结记忆，确保每次部署输入在真实机器人上均可获得。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个任务上，每任务仅用一条示教、无梯度更新，RoboScript 在干净与随机化设置下取得 83.4% 平均成功率，超过最强训练型动作策略 20 个百分点以上；方法可跨视觉分布泛化、通过反思提升、随基础模型规模扩展，并经跨具身记忆适配迁移至实体双臂机器人。

Conclusion: 引入"世界在环"后，机器人可以在上下文中学习操作任务，效果可与在权重中学习相当。这为摆脱权重耦合、实现可迁移且免微调的操作策略提供了新路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Trained action policies write manipulation skill into network weights, tying what is learned to the distribution it is learned from. In-context learning (ICL) avoids this coupling but has not matched trained policies, because a demonstration recorded in another scene carries no feedback from the world the robot currently faces. We observe that what an agent learns in context is semantic, recording task steps and object roles rather than visual appearance, so experience gathered in one world transfers to another. Based on this insight, we propose World In-Context Learning (WICL), a paradigm in which a frozen model starts from a demonstration, rehearses the task in a world such as a simulator, and writes the feedback into its context rather than its weights. We build RoboScript, a modular infrastructure that implements WICL for agentic manipulation. From a single demonstration, RoboScript learns each task through rehearsal in simulation, freezes the learned knowledge as a Task Memory, and deploys the frozen memory on new scenes with data isolation that ensures every deployment input is available on a real robot. On all 50 tasks of RoboTwin 2.0, RoboScript reaches 83.4% mean success across clean and randomized settings with one demonstration per task and no gradient updates, exceeding the strongest trained action policy by over 20 points. The method generalizes across visual distributions, improves through reflection, scales with the foundation model, and transfers to a physical dual-arm robot through cross-embodiment memory adaptation. With a world in the loop, a robot can learn a manipulation task in its context as well as it learns one in its weights. All code is available at https://github.com/ArcheOne-AI/RoboScript

</details>


### [250] [Reframe: Observation-Aligned Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12886)
*Haozhi Yuan,Handong Li,Yifan Zhang,Qianshan Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Reframe后训练框架，将"观测状态"而非仅输出分布作为对齐对象，结合GD-GRPO与OG-MOPD两个阶段，在14个图像与视频基准上优于Mix-RL与vanilla MOPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RL与多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD）是互补的后训练路径，但视觉智能体的工具使用存在两个问题：模型可能忽略工具返回的有用观测；动作接地不良会限制后续蒸馏可用的证据。

Method: Reframe分两阶段：1）Ground-Drop-GRPO（GD-GRPO）用证据感知奖励并随机移除原始视觉输入，训练空间与时间专家以获取接地证据并依赖工具返回观测；2）Observation-Gated-MOPD（OG-MOPD）在rollout中途将缺陷工具动作替换为规范动作，并从修复后的观测恢复蒸馏。两阶段互补，且均无需任务特定的SFT冷启动。

Result: 在14个图像与视频基准上，Reframe优于Mix-RL和vanilla MOPD；消融显示GD-GRPO相比基于结果的GRPO提升了观测依赖性与工具增益。

Conclusion: 将观测状态纳入对齐目标可有效改善视觉智能体的工具使用与蒸馏效果，验证了RL与OPD联合后训练的互补性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) offer complementary routes to post-training. For visual agents, however, tool use presents two related challenges. Models may overlook useful tool-returned observations, while poorly grounded actions can limit the evidence available for subsequent distillation. We introduce Reframe, a post-training framework that treats the observation state, rather than only the output distribution, as an object of alignment. First, Ground-Drop-GRPO (GD-GRPO) trains spatial and temporal specialists with evidence-aware rewards and stochastic removal of the original visual input, encouraging them to acquire grounded evidence and use the returned observations. Second, Observation-Gated-MOPD (OG-MOPD) replaces deficient tool actions with canonical ones mid-rollout and resumes distillation from the repaired observation. The two stages complement each other, as learning to rely on tool-returned observations can help the policy benefit from observation repair. Neither stage requires a task-specific SFT cold start. Extensive experiments over 14 image and video benchmarks demonstrate that Reframe outperforms both Mix-RL and vanilla MOPD. Ablation results show that GD-GRPO increases observation dependence and tool gain compared with outcome-based GRPO.

</details>
